### [1] [Barron Smoothness for Layers in Deep Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34805)
*G. Welper*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文把深度网络逼近误差界拆为两步：先为每层选取最优无穷宽极限，再将各层最优离散化得到有限宽网络；期望意义下后者等价于Barron范数的逐层推广，并给出优化景观分析，表明局部极小满足类似自适应样条与有限元中的等分布原理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深层神经网络的逼近误差难以界定，需要将"选极限宽度"与"离散化实现"两类误差源分离处理，以获得可控的误差界与可解释的最优性刻画。

Method: 分两步框架：第一步对每层选取最优的无穷宽极限；第二步对所有层进行最优离散化以获得有限规模网络。借助期望意义下的分析，证明离散化问题等价于Barron范数的逐层广义化，并对优化景观（landscape）进行分析。

Result: 证明在期望下，最优离散化问题可被逐层推广的Barron范数等价刻画；并刻画了局部极小点的性质：其满足一种等分布（equidistribution）原理，与自适应样条及有限元逼近中的经典思想一致。

Conclusion: 逼近误差界可在"无穷宽极限选取+逐层最优离散化"两步框架下统一处理，等价准则为逐层Barron范数；局部极小具有等分布结构，为宽度分配与网络设计提供理论依据。

Abstract: In order to bound approximation errors for deep neural networks, we split the problem into two steps: First the selection of optimal infinite width limits for each layer and second an optimal discretization of all layers to obtain finite sized networks. We show that in expectation, the latter problem is equivalently characterized by a layer wise generalization of Barron norms. Moreover, we provide an analysis of the optimization landscape and show that local minima are characterized by an equidistribution principle akin to similar ideas in adaptive spline and finite element approximation.

</details>


### [2] [Attributing Agentic Failure in Reasoning Vision-Language Models with Interactive 2D Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33162)
*Pranav Guruprasad,Sean Rivera,Helen Lu,Arushi Jain,Hangliang Ren,Harshvardhan Sikka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 MultiNet v2.0-Gridworld，一个用于失败归因而非排名的交互式迷宫环境；通过可独立增删的机制类型与 BFS 精确最优步数，分离智能体失败的不同成因，并揭示现有 VLM 在机制推理上的严重不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体基准仅报告单一成功率，混合了误读环境、未理解组件运作方式、操作顺序错误、错误后无法恢复等不同失败原因，而每类失败需要不同的改进方案，因此现有基准无法为诊断提供有效信息。

Method: 构建可归因的网格迷宫环境：支持钥匙-门、开关-闸门、干扰/诱饵物及其有序组合四类机制，机制可独立增删，而渲染器、动作空间与评分函数保持固定；对位置、朝向、库存与机制状态的联合状态做广度优先搜索，得到每个实例的精确最优动作数，用以设定难度并评分未完成回合；在 15 个留出迷宫、540 个回合上固定评测协议，并在 50 个迷宫上评测三个视觉语言模型。

Result: 三个 VLM 在 150 个回合中仅解出 6 个；50 个迷宫中有 45 个无任何模型解出；包含开关-闸门机制的回合无一解出。

Conclusion: 该环境通过机制级可控性与精确最优解，实现了失败原因的可归因诊断；初步结果表明当前视觉语言模型在环境机制理解与有序操作上存在系统性短板，尤其对条件式开关-闸门机制完全失效，为后续针对性改进提供了定位依据。

Abstract: Agentic benchmarks report a success rate that confounds distinct causes of failure: misreading the environment, never working out how components of the environment operates, operating the components in the wrong order, and failing to recover from a mistake, each of which calls for a different fix. We present an early preview of MultiNet v2.0-Gridworld - an interactive maze environment built for attribution rather than ranking. Four different types of mechanisms, namely key-door pairs, switch-gate pairs, distractors and decoys, and ordered combinations of these mechanisms, can be added, modified, or removed independently while the renderer, the action space, and the scoring function stay fixed. A breadth-first search over the joint state of position, facing direction, inventory, and mechanism state gives every instance an exact optimal action count, which sets its difficulty and grades unfinished episodes. We fix the evaluation protocol on 15 held-out mazes over 540 episodes, then evaluate three vision-language models on 50 mazes. They solve 6 of 150 episodes, 45 of the 50 mazes were not solved by any model, and no episode containing a switch-gate mechanism was solved.

</details>


### [3] [Quantum Machine Learning for Signature Forgery Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33163)
*Nabila,Md Farhan Shahriyar,Nowshin Tabassum Puspa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究将量子核方法与混合量子-经典管线用于离线手写签名伪造检测，提出以EfficientNetB0冻结特征+量子兼容PCA降维+最近邻判别的混合模型，单折测试准确率92.62%，五折交叉验证均值91.26%±1.13%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线手写签名仍是法律与金融文档中的主要行为生物特征，但静态栅格图像缺失笔压、时序与笔顺信息，导致熟练伪造检测困难；作者希望探索可迁移至NISQ硬件的量子机器学习管线在该任务上的可行性。

Method: 在Kaggle公开二分类签名图像集上，构建ZZ特征映射量子核搭配KNN、SVM、随机森林三类经典学习器；并进一步提出混合模型：EfficientNetB0作为冻结特征提取器输出1280维表示，经量子兼容主成分投影压缩至量子比特寄存器规模子空间，再由最近邻规则判定类别；同时给出六种量子变体的受控消融、混淆矩阵、t-SNE可视化及各类别精确率/召回率/F1。

Result: 单次留出测试折上混合管线达92.62%准确率；分层五折交叉验证平均准确率91.26%，标准差1.13%；消融覆盖六种量子变体并报告混淆矩阵与分类指标。

Conclusion: 所提混合管线在离线签名伪造检测上取得较高且稳定的精度，同时保留可移植至NISQ硬件的量子嵌入步骤，验证了量子-经典混合范式在该任务上的潜力。

Abstract: Offline handwritten signatures remain a primary behavioural biometric in legal and financial documents. Detecting skilled forgeries from a static raster image is hard because pen pressure, timing and stroke order are missing. This work studies a family of quantum machine learning pipelines for offline signature forgery detection on a public Kaggle corpus of images split into two classes. We pair a ZZ feature map quantum kernel with three classical learners: K-Nearest Neighbours, Support Vector Machine and Random Forest. We then propose a deeper hybrid that uses EfficientNetB0 as a frozen feature extractor, reduces the resulting 1280 dimensional representation to a qubit register sized subspace through a quantum compatible principal component projection and decides class membership with a nearest neighbour rule. On a single held out test fold the proposed hybrid reaches 92.62 percent accuracy. On stratified five fold cross validation the same pipeline yields a mean accuracy of 91.26 percent with a standard deviation of 1.13 percent. The pipeline keeps a quantum embedding step that is portable to NISQ hardware. We report a controlled ablation across six quantum variants, confusion matrices, a t SNE projection and per class precision, recall and F1 score.

</details>


### [4] [Code availability is not enough: schemas for documenting and extending data analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33167)
*H. Sherry Zhang,Roger D. Peng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种用自然语言表示数据分析决策的schema，使LLM能直接生成并验证分析脚本，并可通过alternative文件把单一分析扩展为multiverse比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复现某一结果不等于理解决策链条：代码把决策与实现细节混杂，手稿只陈述主要决策且散落各处、遗漏次要决策；读者想问"换一种决策结果会怎样"却难以实现。

Method: 构建以自然语言决策集合为核心的schema作为手稿与代码之间的中间表示，由LLM据此生成并校验分析脚本，并用alternative文件描述替代决策以自动展开multiverse。

Result: 应用一：为已发表的"堆积吸烟数据"回归分析构建schema，复现报告估计值，并将审稿人建议的替代设定经alternative文件自动转为代码；应用二：在无schema的"多分析师足球研究"中，该流程用作可复现性检查。

Conclusion: schema作为中间表示给出分析的自包含描述，并从读者视角提升了分析的可扩展性。

Abstract: A data analysis involves a sequence of decisions, from data cleaning to model specification, each of which can affect the reported result. Reproducing that result is not the same as understanding the sequence of decisions that produced it and the reasons for each: the code mixes those decisions with implementation details, while the manuscript states the major decisions and their rationale but scatters them across its sections and often leaves out the minor ones. A reader may then ask how the result would change under a different decision: the question itself is easy to pose, but implementing it is often non-trivial in practice. In this work, we propose a schema that represents a data analysis as a collection of decisions in natural language, from which large language models (LLMs) can generate and validate analysis scripts directly. This workflow can also expand a single analysis into a full multiverse of alternatives, specified in an alternative file, ready for comparison. We demonstrate the proposed workflow in two applications. In the first, we construct a schema for a published regression analysis of heaped smoking data and show that it reproduces the reported estimates, and that a reviewer-suggested alternative can be specified through the alternative file and turned into code automatically. In the second, where no schema is provided, we show how the workflow serves instead as a reproducibility check, applied to the many-analysts football study. As an intermediate representation between the manuscript and code scripts, our schema gives a self-contained account of an analysis and makes it extensible from a reader's perspective.

</details>


### [5] [Temporal Exposure to Byzantine Vulnerability Under Intermittent Participation in Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33168)
*Sasa Maric Robert Abbas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出"动态参与暴露"(DPE)概念：全局拜占庭比例低于聚合规则容忍阈值时，单轮参与客户端集合仍可能违反鲁棒聚合假设，并将拜占庭鲁棒性由静态总体属性重构为随机的、时间维度的属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习等分布式场景中客户端参与具有间歇性与随机性，而现有鲁棒聚合分析通常基于全局拜占庭比例这一静态总体假设，忽略了每轮活跃集合的波动可能导致的单轮鲁棒性失效风险。

Method: 形式化定义DPE现象；在随机参与模型下推导单轮暴露概率；分析活跃集合规模对观测拜占庭比例波动性的影响；考察多轮暴露累积效应；并建模在有利轮次选择性参与的对手策略。

Result: 分析表明间歇性参与并不必然导致聚合失败，但会使拜占庭鲁棒性从静态总体属性转变为随机的、时间相关的属性；小活跃集合显著放大拜占庭比例的波动，多轮累积与选择性参与对手进一步抬升暴露风险。

Conclusion: 提出基于最小活跃集合约束、时间维度监控、参与历史记录与自适应聚合的可操作设计建议，以缓解DPE风险。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the effect of participation variability on per-round Byzantine vulnerability. We formalise Dynamic Participation Exposure (DPE), a phenomenon in which the global Byzantine fraction remains below an aggregation rule's tolerance threshold, while a participating client set violates, or approaches violation of, the assumptions required for robust aggregation. Under stochastic participation, we derive the probability of per round exposure and analyse how active-set size affects the variability of the observed Byzantine fraction. We further examine the accumulation of exposure over multiple rounds and consider adversaries that selectively participate during favourable rounds. Our analysis shows that intermittent participation does not automatically imply aggregation failure. Rather, it transforms Byzantine robustness from a static population-level property into a stochastic and temporal property. To mitigate this risk, we outline actionable design recommendations based on minimum active-set constraints, temporal monitoring, participation history, and adaptive aggregation.

</details>


### [6] [Geophysics-informed invertible operator network for Bayesian geophysical inversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33178)
*Yangwen Zhang,Xintong Wang,Xiaofei Guan,Peng Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出地球物理信息可逆算子网络GI-ION，将可逆神经网络作为DeepONet分支网络，联合学习正算子与隐变量以刻画反演不确定性，实现稀疏观测下的高效贝叶斯采样式反演。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地球物理反问题因观测稀疏与测量噪声而病态：MCMC类采样式贝叶斯方法需大量正演模拟、计算成本过高；数据驱动机器学习方法在训练数据有限时难以保证物理一致性。

Method: 在深度算子学习框架中嵌入可逆神经网络（INN）作为分支网络，将模型参数映射为分支系数（表示正演解）与隐变量（表示反问题不确定性）；通过学习正演算子并强制分支系数与隐变量统计独立，构造可学习的条件近似分布，从而支持稀疏边界观测下的高效条件采样。

Result: 在一维面波频散、二维随机场模型与地震剖面模型的走时层析三个代表性算例上进行验证，实验表明在多种问题设置下性能稳健，并能实现高效条件采样。

Conclusion: GI-ION将可逆网络的不确定性建模能力与算子学习的正演学习能力结合，为稀疏观测下的地球物理贝叶斯反演提供了兼顾物理一致性与采样效率的可行方案。

Abstract: Geophysical inverse problems are inherently ill-posed because of sparse observations and measurement noise. Classical sampling-based Bayesian inference methods, such as Markov chain Monte Carlo (MCMC), are computationally prohibitive because they require many forward simulations. Data-driven machine-learning methods often struggle to produce physically consistent solutions when training data are limited. To address these issues, we propose a geophysics-informed invertible operator network (GI-ION) that integrates an invertible neural network (INN) within a deep operator-learning framework for efficient Bayesian geophysical inversion. The INN serves as the branch network and transforms the model parameters into branch coefficients that represent the forward solution and a latent variable that captures uncertainty in the inverse problem. By learning the forward operator and enforcing statistical independence between the branch coefficients and the latent variable, GI-ION enables efficient sampling from a learned conditional approximation, even with sparse boundary observations. We validated GI-ION on three representative cases: 1D surface-wave dispersion and travel-time tomography for a 2D synthetic random-field model and a seismic-slice model. Numerical experiments demonstrate robust performance across diverse problem settings and efficient conditional sampling.

</details>


### [7] [Deep Learning-Based Mesoscopic Emission Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33179)
*Abdelkader Dib,Milos Balac,Antonio Sciarretta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于GRU的Direct Emission Prediction (DEP)深度学习模型，实现兼顾微观精度与宏观效率的路网级排放预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交通排放模型存在根本性权衡：宏观模型计算高效但依赖平均速度导致系统性误差，微观模型精度高但难以支撑大规模仿真。研究旨在弥合二者差距。

Method: 用真实GNSS速度轨迹与微观排放模型MOBSim耦合生成超100万条合成行程训练GRU模型；以路段级序列输入（车辆特征、道路属性、环境温度）直接预测CO2与NOx；将DEP集成到MATSim工作流并在Ile-de-France区域应用。

Result: DEP与混合介观-微观参考模型SPG-M精度相当（CO2误差0.35%，NOx误差1.87%，涵盖冷启动与热启动），运行时间仅为其2.2%；对遥感实测数据验证显示DEP能复现Euro 2-6柴油车真实NOx水平，而COPERT、HBEFA等宏观工具系统性低估；并有效刻画冷启动排放的空间分布与城区热点。

Conclusion: 深度学习驱动的介观框架可在极低计算成本下达到微观级精度，支持不依赖预计算排放因子库的可扩展高分辨率城市排放建模。

Abstract: Traffic-related emissions are a major contributor to urban air pollution. However, existing emission models face a fundamental trade-off: macroscopic models are computationally efficient but rely on average speeds, leading to systematic errors, while microscopic models achieve high accuracy but are computationally prohibitive for large scale simulations. To bridge this gap, this study introduces Direct Emission Prediction (DEP), a Gated Recurrent Unit (GRU)-based deep learning model. DEP was trained on over one million synthetic trips generated by coupling real-world GNSS speed profiles with the microscopic emission model MOBSim. By processing sequential link-level inputs, including vehicle characteristics, road attributes, and ambient temperature, DEP predicts CO$_2$ and NOx emissions. We integrated DEP into a MATSim-based workflow and applied it to the Ile-de-France region. DEP matches the accuracy of SPG-M, a hybrid mesoscopic-microscopic reference model, within 0.35% for CO$_2$ and 1.87% for NOx across both hot and cold-start scenarios, while reducing computational runtime to just 2.2%. Furthermore, validation against empirical remote sensing data confirms that DEP accurately reproduces real-world NOx emission levels across Euro 2-6 diesel vehicles, whereas traditional macroscopic tools like COPERT and HBEFA systematically underestimate them. Finally, DEP effectively captures the spatial distribution of cold-start emissions, identifying key hotspots in dense urban areas. This work demonstrates that deep learning-based mesoscopic frameworks can achieve microscopic accuracy at a fraction of the computational cost, enabling scalable, high-resolution urban emission modeling without relying on pre-computed emission factor databases.

</details>


### [8] [Neighbor Crowding Predicts Feature Stability in Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33182)
*Denis Federico Lim,Naveen Dasari,George Flint,Kevin Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明在TopK稀疏自编码器中，单次训练得到的六个廉价统计量即可高精度预测特征跨种子稳定性，其中"邻居拥挤度"是最强单一预测因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAE可将语言模型的多义特征分解为稀疏可解释特征，但难以区分哪些特征是真实稳定的、哪些只是初始化造成的假象。常规做法是训练多个随机种子并用余弦相似度比较，计算成本很高，因此需要研究能否在不训练多种子的情况下识别稳定特征。

Method: 在不同训练token规模下考察跨种子特征稳定性，并从单次训练中提取六个低计算成本的统计量（包括字典的几何性质如邻居拥挤度）来预测稳定性。

Result: TopK SAE的跨种子稳定性高度可预测；邻居拥挤度（位于单位球稠密区域的特征比孤立特征更稳定）是最强的单一预测因子。

Conclusion: 稳定特征可通过单次训练的低成本统计量识别，无需多种子训练，从而显著降低评估SAE特征可靠性的计算开销。

Abstract: Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) offer an approach to identify language model representations by breaking apart polysemantic features into more sparse, interpretable ones. Yet, it is challenging to identify which of the learned features are actually stable, rather than artifacts of initialization. One approach is to train multiple SAE seeds, and compare the learned features across seeds by cosine similarity, but this is notably costly. This raises the question of whether stable features can be identified without the computational expense of training multiple seeds. In this work, we examine cross-seed feature stability across differing levels of training tokens, and demonstrate that in TopK SAEs, it is highly predictable using six computationally cheap statistics derived from a single training run. Neighbor crowding, a geometric property of the dictionary, is the strongest single predictor, with features that sit in dense regions of the unit sphere more likely to be stable than isolated ones.

</details>


### [9] [REI: Learning Ideation from Repository Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33183)
*Muyu Zou,Xiang Feng,Yufei Xu,Shanshan Ye,Zulong Chen,Lingyu Mu,Jinxin Hu,Jing Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出REI框架，从代码仓库演化中学习可复用的构思策略，并针对"语义多样性塌缩"问题引入SPOD机制，在公开基准与工业场景中提升构思质量、方法多样性、跨任务泛化与长尾探索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI系统能执行和评估实验，构思（ideation）成为自动化科研迭代的瓶颈；现实领域中有效想法稀疏、验证成本高，直接在线试错效率低。作者观察到仓库演化是可扩展的技术经验来源：代码改动记录具体技术假设，后续修订、采纳与复用提供效用信号。此外，质量驱动的构思优化存在隐性问题——语义多样性塌缩：想法虽词汇多样，却集中于少数主导方法族，削弱跨任务泛化与长尾探索。

Method: REI（Repository Evolution-driven Ideation）：从仓库演化中学习可复用的构思策略，将领域特定技术经验注入模型参数，减少对高成本试错的依赖。SPOD（Semantic Prior Optimization and Debiasing）：一种保持多样性的对齐机制，显式建模方法身份，校正类别级奖励偏差，并在保留强代表性样本的同时抑制同一方法族内的冗余累积。

Result: 在公开基准与真实工业场景的实验中，构思质量、方法多样性、跨任务泛化和长尾探索均取得提升。

Conclusion: 仓库演化可作为可扩展的构思经验来源，参数化注入可降低对在线试错的依赖；质量优化需配合语义层面去偏与多样性约束以缓解方法族塌缩，二者结合可改善构思质量与探索广度。

Abstract: As AI systems become increasingly capable of executing and evaluating experiments, ideation is emerging as a bottleneck in automated scientific and engineering iteration. In many real-world domains, useful ideas are sparse and their validation is costly, making direct online exploration inefficient. We observe that repository evolution provides a scalable source of reusable technical experience: code changes record concrete technical hypotheses, while subsequent revisions, adoption, and reuse provide signals about their utility. Based on this observation, we propose REI, a Repository Evolution-driven Ideation framework that learns reusable ideation policies from repository evolution, injecting domain-specific technical experience into model parameters and reducing reliance on costly trial-and-error. We further identify a hidden failure mode in quality-driven ideation optimization: semantic diversity collapse. Although generated ideas can remain lexically diverse, they increasingly concentrate in a small number of dominant method families, limiting cross-task generalization and long-tail exploration. To address this problem, we introduce Semantic Prior Optimization and Debiasing (SPOD), a diversity-preserving alignment mechanism that makes method identity explicit, corrects category-level reward bias, and limits redundant accumulation within the same method family while retaining strong representatives. Experiments on public benchmarks and a real-world industrial setting demonstrate improvements in ideation quality, method diversity, cross-task generalization, and long-tail exploration.

</details>


### [10] [Semantic Density Does Not Predict Agent-Memory Collapse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33184)
*Saksham Gupta,Yash Choudhary,Arpit Upadhyay*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文检验持久化文本记忆的存储统计量能否在留出任务准确率下降前发出预警，结论是否定的：全局语义密度（平均成对余弦相似度ρ）零中位提前量，局部拥挤度指标跨设计不可迁移，唯一起作用的预测信号是无界全库提示下的token增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体借助持久化文本记忆可提升表现，但反复更新记忆会侵蚀任务性能。作者希望验证一个低成本、与任务无关的假设：记忆库的内部统计量（相似度几何、检索、图结构）能否在留出准确率下降之前提供预警，从而实现无需反复评测的失效监测。

Method: 在805个记忆快照上注册并追踪ρ（平均成对余弦相似度）及13个几何/检索/图统计量，覆盖13种记忆方法（存储轨迹、规则、工作流、策略或链接记录；选择性检索或全库提示）与ALFWorld、AppWorld、ScienceWorld、WebShop及两个ARC-AGI设置。以准确率峰值后的检查点作为次级下降点做回溯分析；再用该语料训练分类器并原样迁移到PropWorld上长达5,000个任务的运行中测试。

Result: 没有任何统计量可靠预测准确率下降的早期征兆，ρ的中位提前量为0。相对次级下降点，高相似对与最近邻拥挤类指标变化更早，但并未稳定优于"存储条目数"这一基线。跨域迁移分类器在有容量上限、准确率稳定的记忆库上误报56.9%的检查点，在任务成功率降至零之后仅检出18%的检查点。

Conclusion: 全局语义密度不构成可靠预警信号，局部拥挤度无法跨记忆设计一致迁移。长程运行中有容量上限的记忆库保持稳定，唯一失败源于无界全库提示超出模型输入容量，而这一点可由token增长直接预测——提示记忆失效的根因更偏向上下文预算而非语义退化。

Abstract: Persistent textual memory can improve LLM agents, but repeated updates can erode task performance. We ask whether memory-store statistics warn before held-out accuracy declines. We registered mean pairwise cosine similarity ($ρ$) and 13 additional geometry, retrieval, and graph statistics on 805 snapshots from 13 memory methods across ALFWorld, AppWorld, ScienceWorld, WebShop, and two ARC-AGI settings. These methods store trajectories, rules, workflows, strategies, or linked records, using selective retrieval or whole-store prompting. No such statistic reliably predicted early signs of task accuracy decline, and $ρ$ had zero median lead. With the checkpoint after peak accuracy as a secondary decline point, measures of highly similar pairs and nearest-neighbor crowding changed earlier but did not consistently outperform stored-item count. We trained classifiers on this corpus and applied them unchanged to PropWorld runs of up to 5,000 crafting tasks. They falsely flagged 56.9% of checkpoints from size-limited stores with stable accuracy and detected only 18% of checkpoints after task success fell to zero. Thus, global semantic density did not provide a reliable warning, while local crowding did not transfer consistently across memory designs. In these long-horizon runs, size-limited stores remained stable, while the sole failure arose when unbounded whole-store prompting exceeded model input capacity, which token growth predicted directly.

</details>


### [11] [KiRA: Kronecker Input-Gated Rank Adaptation for Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning of Pretrained Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33236)
*Akil Ahmad Taki,Shaikh Anowarul Fattah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: KiRA：用冻结随机矩阵与可训练矩阵的Kronecker积突破低秩上限，并以输入门控动态调节秩，实现参数高效微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全量微调成本高，主流PEFT采用低秩更新，秩远低于全量微调且对所有输入固定；输入自适应变体仍受低秩瓶颈限制，且训练前无法判断任务是否需要输入条件化。

Method: ΔW = A ⊗ M(x)，A为冻结随机p×p矩阵，M(x)=B1·diag(g(x))·B2；因积的秩具乘性，A将M(x)的秩放大p倍而不增加可训练参数；g(x)由跨层共享网络逐样本计算一次，以步长p调节秩；无需条件化时在部署阶段将门控折叠进B1。

Result: 六个医学影像与语言基准上，KiRA相对五个已发表基线取得最高平均分，可训练参数比LoRA少8.7–9.7倍；预训练判据在全部六个任务上与实测的输入条件化收益一致。

Conclusion: Kronecker分解可解除低秩瓶颈并以离散秩步进使输入条件化收益可预判，兼顾参数效率与性能。

Abstract: Full fine-tuning of large pretrained models is expensive, and parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) is the standard alternative. Most PEFT methods use low-rank updates, whose rank lies far below that of full fine-tuning and is the same for every input. Input-adaptive variants vary the capacity per input but remain within the low-rank bottleneck, and whether a task benefits from input conditioning is not known before training. This paper introduces Kronecker input-gated rank adaptation (KiRA). Instead of the conventional low-rank product, the update $ΔW$ is the Kronecker product of a frozen random $p\times p$ matrix $A$ and a trainable factor $M(x)$, which overcomes the rank ceiling: because rank is multiplicative under this product, $A$ multiplies the rank of $M(x)$ by $p$ without adding trainable parameters. The factor $M(x)$ is further decomposed as $B_1\,\mathrm{diag}(g(x))\,B_2$, where the input gate $g(x)$, computed once per sample by a network shared across all adapted layers, adjusts the rank of the update to each input in steps of $p$. Where input conditioning is unnecessary, the gate is folded into $B_1$ at deployment. Because the gate changes the rank only in discrete steps, the benefit of input conditioning can be predicted before training from a few hundred labeled inputs. Across six medical imaging and language benchmarks, KiRA attains the highest mean score against five published baselines with 8.7--9.7 times fewer trainable parameters than low-rank adaptation, and the pre-training criterion agrees with the measured benefit of input conditioning on all six tasks.

</details>


### [12] [Resource-Efficient Multimodal Fusion for Early Rice Blast Forecasting in Sustainable Agriculture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33237)
*Shubhajyoti Das,Pritam Bikram,Arindam Biswas,Samrat Paul,Parimal Sinha,Bhargab B. Bhattacharya*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出轻量级多模态融合框架（注意力位置编码Transformer + CNN嵌GRU），在计算与能耗约束下提前5-10天预测稻瘟病风险，验证准确率96.93%、F1 95.79%，并显著降低计算复杂度、推理延迟与碳排放。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可持续农业场景下需在有限算力与能耗约束中实现及时病害预警；早期稻瘟病估计可优化作物管理、减少过量农药使用并保障粮食安全。但现有框架计算开销高，且难以建模异构气象与遥感数据中复杂的光谱—时序依赖关系。

Method: 轻量级多模态融合框架：①基于注意力增强位置编码的Transformer，对顺序气象token赋予自适应重要性以建模时序特征；②轻量CNN提取判别性光谱特征，GRU捕捉两个时间点遥感影像的时序动态；③多模态融合层整合异构表征，在多样农业气候区实现症状显现前5-10天的稻瘟病风险预测。

Result: 实验表明框架优于现有SOTA方法，验证准确率96.93%、F1-score 95.79%；轻量多模态架构显著降低计算复杂度、推理延迟与碳排放。

Conclusion: 该框架为可持续农业中资源高效人工智能提供了实用基础，是面向大规模病害预测的能效型解决方案。

Abstract: Resource-efficient artificial intelligence has become increasingly important for sustainable agriculture, where timely disease forecasting must be achieved under computational and energy constraints. Early estimation of rice blast can enhance food security by optimizing crop management practices while reducing excessive pesticide use. However, existing frameworks often suffer from high computational overhead and limited capability to model the complex spectral-temporal dependencies present in heterogeneous meteorological and remote sensing data. To address these challenges, this paper proposes a lightweight multimodal fusion framework comprising an attentive positional encoding-based Transformer and a convolutional neural network (CNN)-embedded gated recurrent unit (GRU) for efficient spectral-temporal representation learning. The attention-enhanced positional encoding assigns adaptive importance to sequential meteorological tokens, enabling effective modelling of temporal characteristics. In parallel, the lightweight CNN extracts discriminative spectral features, while the GRU captures temporal dynamics from remote sensing imagery acquired over two time-instances. A multimodal fusion layer integrates these heterogeneous representations to forecast rice blast risk 5-10 days before symptom manifestation across diverse agro-climatic zones. Extensive experiments demonstrate that the proposed framework outperforms state-of-the-art methods, achieving a validation accuracy of 96.93% and an F1-score of 95.79%. In addition to its predictive performance, the lightweight multimodal architecture substantially reduces computational complexity, inference latency, and carbon emissions, making it an energy-efficient solution for large-scale disease forecasting. The proposed framework provides a practical foundation for resource-efficient artificial intelligence in sustainable agriculture.

</details>


### [13] [Sequential Testing on Complex and High Dimensional Data Streams Leveraging Unsupervised Model Errors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33238)
*George Stamatelis,Iordanis Koutsopoulos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出两种分布无关的序贯假设检验与序贯变点检测统计量，利用仅在零假设/变点前数据上训练的预测或重构模型之误差，实现无需在线重训练、可扩展的流式检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式数据（如网络流量元数据）过于复杂，难以进行传统似然比检验所需的精确概率建模；且存在信息不对称：仅有零假设/变点前数据，缺乏备择/变点后数据（如零日攻击、新发疾病）。

Method: 基于“泛化不完美”的假设，构造检验统计量监控仅在零假设/变点前数据上训练的机器学习模型的预测或重构误差；在模型误差的温和假设下推导严格的非渐近分析保证。

Result: 对SHT给出严格的非渐近误差控制；对SCPD给出平均运行长度(ARL)与期望检测延迟(EDD)的显式界。在目标跟踪、混沌动力系统及真实网络安全数据集上，持续满足预设约束，而现有算法出现漏检与虚警膨胀。

Conclusion: 所提框架分布无关、无需在线重训练与核计算、仅作用于标量误差，具备高度可扩展性，适用于信息不对称的流式检测场景。

Abstract: We propose two general, distribution-free test statistics for binary Sequential Hypothesis Testing (SHT) and Sequential Change Point Detection (SCPD) respectively, in streaming environments where the data (e.g., network traffic metadata) are too complicated for the exact probabilistic modeling required by conventional likelihood ratio-driven tests. Our framework also addresses the challenge of information asymmetry, where data is available only from the null/pre-change regime and not from the alternative/post-change one such as zero-day attacks or emerging disease related regimes. The proposed statistics monitor the errors of a predictive or reconstructive machine learning model trained solely on null/pre-change data, relying on imperfect generalization (the model's performance will degrade when deployed on data from the alternative/post-change class). Under mild assumptions on the model's errors, we establish rigorous analytical guarantees: strict non-asymptotic error control for SHT, and explicit bounds on the Average Run Length (ARL) and the Expected Detection Delay (EDD) for SCPD. Extensive evaluations in target tracking, chaotic dynamical systems, and real-world network security datasets, demonstrate that our framework consistently satisfies prescribed constraints where existing algorithms succumb to inflated missed detections and/or false alarms. Finally, because our methods operate on scalar errors without requiring online retraining or kernel evaluations, they are highly scalable.

</details>


### [14] [Typical Angina, Reversed: A Long-Standing Documentation Error in the UCI Heart Disease Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33248)
*Tejas Tovinkere Pattabhi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明UCI Heart Disease数据集中胸痛变量的编码与文档记载完全相反：文档标为"无症状"的编码4实际对应典型心绞痛患者，而编码1对应几乎无典型心绞痛特征者。作者通过两项互补检验确证这一反转，并揭示下游SHAP解释文献与零样本语言模型分类器被该文档错误误导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UCI Heart Disease是被广泛使用的机器学习基准数据集，但其胸痛（chest-pain）变量的语义标签可能与其实际含义不符。由于常规预测模型对该编码反转不敏感，错误长期未被发现，而依赖类别名称进行特征归因解释的研究与读取标签的零样本大模型则会受到实质误导。

Method: 采用两项互补检验：(i) 用原始1986年Cleveland研究的胸痛交叉表与回归系数，仅在校正后的解读下才能复现；(ii) 在Budapest、Switzerland、Long Beach数据中，编码为4的患者有93–96%具备全部三项典型心绞痛特征。此外审计了预设定Scholar检索的150条记录抽查（124篇可读），统计对命名类别方向陈述与校正后不一致的比例，并检验零样本语言模型在给定年龄、性别、胸痛类型下使用文档标签时的AUC变化。

Result: 在32篇明确陈述某命名类别方向的论文中，25篇（78%，95% CI 61–89%）的陈述与校正后的类别身份不一致；其中22篇（69%，51–82%，事后分析）可归因于文档错误，9篇还据此给出临床解释；124篇可读论文均未识别出该反转。此外，1990年带词标签的Cleveland文件、Statlog(Heart)、OpenML、PMLB及两个高票Kaggle副本（合计约40万次下载）均沿用文档标签。常规预测模型基本不受影响；但零样本语言模型在使用文档标签时AUC下降0.12–0.33。

Conclusion: UCI Heart Disease胸痛变量的官方文档标签与其实际语义相反。常规预测性能掩盖了这一错误，但依赖类别名称的可解释性结论被系统性反转，读取标签的零样本语言模型分类器被误导。作者提供了校正后的映射、证据与代码，呼吁数据使用者与下游平台修正标签。

Abstract: The UCI Heart Disease data are a widely used machine-learning benchmark. We show that their chest-pain variable is stored in reverse of its documentation. At all four sites, the code documented as "asymptomatic" (4) marks patients with typical angina, and the code documented as "typical angina" (1) marks patients with almost none of the classical angina features (at Cleveland, no chest pain).
  Two complementary checks establish this: (i) the chest-pain table and regression coefficients of the original 1986 Cleveland study are reproduced only under the corrected reading; (ii) in the Budapest, Switzerland and Long Beach files, 93-96% of patients coded 4 have all three classical angina features. A 1990 word-labeled Cleveland file already uses the documented labels, and Statlog (Heart), a Cleveland subset, stores the same codes. OpenML, PMLB and two highly voted Kaggle copies that name the categories use the same documented labels (about 400,000 downloads combined).
  We audited a seeded random sample of 150 records from a pre-specified Scholar query for SHAP studies of these data mentioning asymptomatic chest pain (124 readable). Of 32 papers that state a direction for a named category, 25 (78%; 95% CI 61-89%) state one inconsistent with the corrected category identities; 22 (69%; 51-82%; post hoc) are attributable to the documentation error, and nine of these explain it clinically. None of the 124 readable papers identifies the reversal.
  Conventional models are essentially unaffected, one reason the error went unnoticed. Interpretations that attach the documented names to model effects are reversed, and zero-shot language-model classifiers that read the labels are misled: with the documented labels, three open-weight models lose 0.12-0.33 in area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUC) given age, sex and chest-pain type. We give the corrected mapping, evidence and code.

</details>


### [15] [FERRETS: A Scalable Symbolic Transform for Time Series Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33249)
*Antoine Guillaume*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FERRETS：基于WEASEL 2的无标签字典变换，采用随机长度/膨胀窗口、8位词、层级词与跨通道词，在UCR上最快（十线程），精度仅显著落后MultiRocket-Hydra且快24.7倍，与WEASEL 2无显著差异但快125倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统时间序列字典方法精度高但可扩展性差，其精度优势已不足以抵消计算成本；需要一种兼顾精度与效率的字典变换方法。

Method: FERRETS是构建于WEASEL 2之上的无标签字典变换：将随机长度与膨胀的窗口映射为精确8位词；为每个窗口加入基于均值与离散度的"层级词"；在多变量数据上加入跨通道词；并配有流式分类器。

Result: 在UCR、Multiverse与MONSTER数据集上与领先分类器、深度网络及基础模型比较：UCR十线程下为11种方法中最快；其余10种中仅MultiRocket-Hydra显著更准但耗时24.7倍；与WEASEL 2精度无显著差异（后者慢125倍），显著优于其他字典方法；Multiverse核心上与HIVE-COTE 2、MultiRocket-Hydra精度无显著差异（UCR上落后于二者）；UCR官方划分上显著优于五个冻结基础模型；MONSTER上MiniRocket拟合耗时3.7倍且精度显著更低。

Conclusion: FERRETS在保持字典方法级精度的同时大幅降低计算成本，在效率-精度权衡上优于现有字典方法及多数强基线，且可流式处理，适用于大规模时间序列分类。

Abstract: Dictionary methods for time series build bags of symbolic words from subsequences, but the accurate ones scale poorly. Against other families of methods, their accuracy no longer pays for their cost. We present FERRETS, a label-free dictionary transform built on WEASEL~2. It keys windows of random length and dilation into exact eight-bit words, adds a level word of each window's mean and spread, and on multivariate data adds cross-channel words. We compare it with leading classifiers, deep networks and foundation models included, on the UCR, Multiverse and MONSTER archives. FERRETS is the fastest of the eleven methods timed on UCR under a ten-thread budget. Of the ten others, only MultiRocket-Hydra is significantly more accurate, taking 24.7 times as long. FERRETS is not significantly different from WEASEL~2, the most accurate earlier dictionary method, which takes 125 times as long, and is significantly ahead of the other dictionary methods. On the Multiverse core, it is not significantly different in accuracy from HIVE-COTE~2 and MultiRocket-Hydra, though behind both on UCR. It is also significantly more accurate than five frozen foundation models on UCR's published split. On the MONSTER problems both complete, MiniRocket takes 3.7 times as long to fit as the streaming FERRETS classifier, which is significantly more accurate.

</details>


### [16] [One Token, Many Lessons: Sliding-Window Attention within Gated Delta Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33257)
*Yuchen Song,Yangbin Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SWGDN，用滑窗注意力替代短卷积对q/k/v做内容相关的局部聚合，从而改善形成状态更新所用键值学习对的方式，在GDN2与KDA上均超越原Delta-Rule基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从测试时回归（TTR）视角看，线性注意力的状态矩阵是在线学习的回归模型，其学习效果不仅取决于更新规则，也取决于用于更新状态的键值学习对的构造方式；已有工作主要关注状态的更新与组织，而本文转向研究学习对如何构造。

Method: 提出Sliding-Window Gated DeltaNet（SWGDN）：将Delta-Rule线性注意力中的短卷积替换为滑窗注意力，对投影后的query、key、value执行内容依赖的局部聚合；该局部混合策略可直接嵌入任意Delta-Rule线性注意力架构，不改变状态更新方程与分块并行（chunkwise parallel）算法；在GDN2与KDA两种变体上实现。

Result: 实验表明SWGDN使GDN2与KDA中连续更新所用的键值对更加相似；同一token的信息跨更新被携带，可帮助循环状态强化先前学到的关联；在多个基准上SWGDN优于原始Delta-Rule基线。

Conclusion: 构造状态更新学习对的方式是提升线性注意力状态学习质量的有效切入点；滑窗注意力的局部内容相关聚合是一种通用、即插即用且不改变核心算法结构的改进手段，在多种Delta-Rule变体上具备一致收益。

Abstract: Linear attention compresses historical context into a fixed-size recurrent state, reducing the computational and memory costs of long-sequence modeling. From the perspective of test-time regression (TTR), the state matrix can be viewed as an online-learned regression model, where keys and values serve as inputs and targets respectively, and queries are used to read from the model. From this perspective, the effectiveness of state learning depends not only on the update rule but also on how the key-value learning pairs used for state updates are constructed. While existing work has largely focused on how the state is updated and organized, we investigate how the learning pairs used for state updates are constructed. To this end, we propose Sliding-Window Gated DeltaNet (SWGDN), which replaces short convolutions in Delta-Rule linear attention with sliding-window attention to perform content-dependent local aggregation of projected queries, keys, and values. Our experiments show that SWGDN makes the key-value pairs used in consecutive updates more similar in both GDN2 and KDA. In SWGDN, carrying information from the same token across updates can help the recurrent state reinforce a previously learned association. The local mixing strategy can be incorporated into any Delta-Rule-based linear attention architecture without changing its state update equations or chunkwise parallel algorithms. We apply this strategy to two Delta-Rule variants, GDN2 and KDA, and experimental results demonstrate that SWGDN outperforms the original Delta-Rule baseline across multiple benchmarks.

</details>


### [17] [Fast and Scalable Abstract Interpretation for Deep Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33259)
*Avaljot Singh,Dushyant Bharadwaj,Yasmin Chandini Sarita,Heng Zhong,Charith Mendis,Gagandeep Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Scepter通过结构化语义特化DNN抽象解释器，将数值求值延迟并与结构计算分离，生成可复用的张量程序，在CPU/GPU上实现显著加速、降低内存并扩展可分析网络规模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 抽象解释可证明DNN性质，但精确分析开销大。通用实现在同一架构、不同输入与性质下重复工作；将分析与数值操作交错执行会限制GPU利用率，并限制张量编译器可整体处理的规模。

Method: 提出基于结构化语义的特化框架：语义决定中间抽象值的结构而延迟其数值求值，使连续操作的结构计算在认证前合并执行，生成可执行张量程序；当抽象解释器与网络架构固定时，程序可复用于不同权重与输入区域。证明其在精确算术下与通用解释器得到相同边界，并暴露操作及依赖以供张量编译与内存规划。实现于Neuton从ConstraintFlow规范生成的张量程序。

Result: 在8种抽象解释器、44个DNN、6个基线系统上于GPU和CPU评估：可处理神经元数量最多为对应基线完成的最大网络的153倍；完成ResNet50与VGG-16上的DeepPoly，而所有DeepPoly基线均未完成；在双方均完成的配置上，GPU几何平均加速11.9x–44.4x，峰值内存几何平均降低2.60x；输入分裂案例中证明68个基线超时的性质。

Conclusion: 结构语义特化能有效消除重复分析与数值求值交错带来的开销，在保证边界等价性的同时，大幅提升可扩展性、速度与内存效率，并支持性质证明。

Abstract: Abstract interpretation can certify properties of Deep Neural Networks (DNNs), but precise analyses remain expensive. General implementations repeat work when analyzing the same architecture under different inputs and properties. Interleaving this work with numerical operations limits GPU utilization and restricts how much of the analysis a tensor compiler can process together.
  We present Scepter, a framework for specializing DNN abstract interpreters through structural semantics. The semantics determine the structure of intermediate abstract values while deferring their numerical evaluation. Structural computations from successive operations can therefore be performed together before certification, generating an executable tensor program. With the abstract interpreter and DNN architecture fixed, the program can be reused with different weights and input regions. We prove that it computes the same bounds as the general interpreter in exact arithmetic. The generated program also exposes operations and their dependencies for tensor compilation and memory planning. We implement Scepter for the tensor programs that Neuton generates from ConstraintFlow specifications.
  We evaluate Scepter on eight abstract interpreters and 44 DNNs against six baseline systems on GPUs and CPUs. Scepter handles networks with up to 153x as many neurons as the largest completed by the corresponding baselines. It completes DeepPoly on ResNet50 and VGG-16, whereas none of the evaluated DeepPoly baselines completes either network. On configurations completed by both systems, GPU geometric-mean speedups over Neuton range from 11.9x to 44.4x across eight analyses, with a 2.60x geometric-mean reduction in peak memory. In an input-splitting case study, Scepter proves 68 properties for which the baselines time out.
  Code: https://github.com/uiuc-focal-lab/constraintflow.git

</details>


### [18] [Two Timescales of Forgetting in Fine-tuning of Language Models: Fast Behavioral Suppression and Slow Representational Drift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33302)
*Yilin Luo,Jin Hwa Lee,Andrew M. Saxe,Antonio Sclocchi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过受控实验揭示监督微调中"行为抑制"与"表征遗忘"存在显著时间尺度分离：被删规则的生成快速改变，而其潜在表征长期保留。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练+微调是主流范式，但微调如何改变预训练习得的表征仍不清楚，尤其是不确定表面行为抑制是否伴随潜在表征的擦除。

Method: 在具有层级潜在结构的受控上下文无关文法（Random Hierarchy Model, RHM）上预训练Transformer，再用其生成规则的受限子集进行监督微调（SFT），追踪生成行为与内部表征的动态；并在自然语言真实设定中验证。

Result: （i）被删规则的生成在快速时间尺度上改变，速度与token-潜在变量相关性大小成反比，但对应表征保持不变；（ii）被删规则的隐藏表征在更长得多的时间尺度上才被遗忘，伴随网络参数的缓慢漂移；自然语言实验呈现类似结果。

Conclusion: 微调中的遗忘存在时间尺度分离，行为抑制与表征擦除并不同步，这一现象可能是语言模型微调的普遍规律。

Abstract: Today, a core pipeline of artificial intelligence systems is pretraining on a broad range of text corpora and then fine-tuning on specific tasks or desired behaviors. Yet we still have limited understanding of how fine-tuning on a restricted data distribution modifies representations acquired during pretraining. In particular, it remains unclear whether surface behavioral suppression happens together with the erasure of latent representations that support it. In this work, we study supervised fine-tuning (SFT) by pretraining transformers on a controlled context-free grammar with hierarchical latent structure, the Random Hierarchy Model (RHM), and fine-tuning with a restricted subset of its generative rules. While during pretraining the model's ability to generate with the correct grammar rules coincides with building the internal representations of the corresponding latent, we find that SFT exhibits a very different dynamics: (i) the generation of deleted rules changes on a fast timescale inversely proportional to the magnitude of the token-latent correlation; meanwhile, their corresponding representations stay intact; (ii) the hidden representations of the deleted rules become forgotten on a much longer timescale, associated with a slow drift of the network parameters. Finally, we observe analogous results in a realistic setup with natural language, suggesting that this timescale separation in forgetting is a more general phenomenon of fine-tuning language models.

</details>


### [19] [Change Partners at Every Layer: Balanced Random Regrouping for Model Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33308)
*Kaiyang Li,Shaobo Han,Shihao Ji*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出平衡随机重分组(BRR)：逐层独立随机等分专家再合并，优于跨层固定分组与相似度分组，并用一阶分析解释其机理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型合并可将多个微调专家整合为单一多任务模型而免于重训练，但合并大量专家会显著降低精度。分组是折中方案：每层或每个权重矩阵将N个专家分为K组，组内合并为一个共享模块。自然选择是跨层保持相同伙伴或按相似性分组，但作者发现这些选择并非最优。

Method: BRR：在每一层(BRR-layer)或每个权重矩阵(BRR-matrix)独立地将专家随机划分为K个等大小组，组内用现有合并方法合并；合并尺度由验证集选择。并通过一阶分析研究伙伴选择对输出特征偏移的影响。

Result: 在全部24个CLIP设置中，两种BRR变体均优于跨层复用同一分组，提升1.0–6.8个点；BRR-matrix在所有设置中也优于基于相似度的FlexMerge。一阶分析与系列实验支持该解释，并进一步指导分组搜索，可超越BRR。

Conclusion: 跨层固定伙伴导致任务输出特征偏移方向相近并累积，而逐层更换伙伴使偏移独立、期望累积更小，故BRR更优；该机理可指导更优的分组搜索。

Abstract: Model merging combines fine-tuned experts into one model that serves many tasks without retraining, but merging many experts this way substantially reduces accuracy. Grouping offers a middle ground: at every layer or weight matrix, the $N$ experts are divided into $K<N$ groups, the experts in a group are merged into one shared module, and a task uses the module merged from its expert's group. Natural choices are to keep the same partners across layers or to group similar experts. Surprisingly, we find that randomly changing partners at every layer outperforms both. We call this Balanced Random Regrouping (BRR): it independently partitions the experts into $K$ equal-sized random groups at each layer (BRR-layer) or weight matrix (BRR-matrix) and merges each group with an existing method. With validation-selected merge scales, both BRR variants outperform reusing one grouping across layers in all 24 CLIP settings, by 1.0-6.8 points, and BRR-matrix also outperforms the similarity-based FlexMerge in every setting. A first-order analysis explains this gain. At each layer, merging a task's expert with its partners shifts the task's output features; when the partners are the same at every layer, these shifts tend to point in similar directions and add up, whereas with new partners at every layer they vary independently and add up less on average. A series of experiments supports this analysis, which further guides a grouping search that can outperform BRR.

</details>


### [20] [Sparse Adaptation for In-Context Learning on Data Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33325)
*João Neves,Afonso Lourenço,Lizy K. John,Eric P. Xing,João Gama*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SAIL：面向表格基础模型（TFM）的稀疏自适应框架，通过复用KV缓存、结合序贯误差监测与在线类特定微聚类检测流结构漂移，仅在必要时重建支持集，从而降低上下文冗余与计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IoT海量数据流与5G低时延应用需要边缘计算；表格基础模型可借助上下文示例而非参数更新实现自适应，但持续演化的数据流会使上下文相关性下降并带来计算效率问题，因此需要按需（稀疏）而非频繁的自适应机制。

Method: 1）在自适应事件之间复用模型的key–value缓存；2）仅当流结构漂移表明上下文可能失效时才重建支持集，而结构表示本身由预测性能变化驱动更新；3）将序贯（prequential）误差监测与在线、类特定的微聚类结合，检测簇质量、类可分性和特征空间覆盖度的变化；4）触发自适应时，按类质量比例分配上下文预算，由最近更新的微簇提供代表性观测用于重拟合。

Result: 论文未给出定量实验结果（摘要中无具体性能指标），所宣称的效果为：限制上下文冗余与计算成本，同时保留近期结构信息。

Conclusion: SAIL通过“漂移触发的稀疏自适应+KV缓存复用+类质量比例上下文预算”实现TFM在演化数据流上的高效、相关性保持的自适应，为边缘IoT场景下的表格基础模型部署提供可行路径。

Abstract: The Internet of Things (IoT) generates massive data streams, making edge computing essential for low-latency applications and 5G networks. Tabular foundation models (TFMs) enable adaptation through in-context examples rather than parameter updates, but evolving streams challenge context relevance and computational efficiency. This work introduces SAIL, a framework for sparse adaptation of TFMs that reuses the model's key--value cache between adaptation events. SAIL reconstructs its support set only when drifts in stream structure indicate potential context inadequacy, while the structural representation itself is updated by changes in predictive performance. SAIL combines prequential error monitoring with online, class-specific micro-clustering to detect changes in cluster mass, class separability, and feature-space coverage. Upon adaptation, a context budget is allocated proportionally to class mass, and the most recently updated micro-clusters provide representative observations for refitting. This limits context redundancy and computational cost while preserving recent structural information.

</details>


### [21] [Online Prediction System for Proactive Autoscaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33332)
*Jun Gao,Areg Poghosyan,Pavel Paulau,Sumit Mohanty,Tengfei Mu,Shawn Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一个用于短期时间序列预测的在线学习系统，支撑大规模分布式系统的主动自动扩缩容；采用“季节性分解 + 残差校正”两阶段轻量线性模型架构，每预测周期在线重训练，生产中每小时超90万次预测，离线MAPE较原生产模型降低18.6%，每日节省约11.8万台服务器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统预测方法侧重季节性与长期趋势建模，难以满足互联网级基础设施动态资源管理所需的“近期区间高精度、低延迟”预测；且持续模型存储与特征漂移监控成本高，需要能实时适应需求变化的轻量在线预测方案。

Method: 两阶段架构：先做季节性分解（STL），再以工程化特征驱动的轻量线性模型进行残差校正；每个预测周期对模型进行在线即时重训练（retraining on-the-fly），从而持续适应需求模式变化，无需持久化模型存储或特征漂移监控。

Result: 系统部署于多个异构服务，每小时处理超90万次预测；在生产流量离线评估中，基于STL的配置相较既有生产模型将MAPE降低18.6%；生产环境中每日估计节省11.8万台服务器。

Conclusion: 轻量线性模型配合在线重训练与分解式建模，可在超大规模场景下实现低延迟、高精度的短期预测，并转化为显著的资源节约，验证了“简单模型 + 持续在线学习”路线在自动扩缩容中的实用价值。

Abstract: We present an online learning system for short-term time series forecasting that enables proactive autoscaling in large-scale distributed systems. Unlike traditional forecasting methods that focus on modeling seasonality and long-term trends, the system targets high-accuracy, low-latency predictions for immediate future intervals for dynamic resource management in internet-scale infrastructures. The system employs a two-stage architecture: a seasonality decomposition stage followed by a residual correction stage using lightweight linear models with engineered features. By retraining models on-the-fly for every prediction cycle, the online prediction system continuously adapts to changing demand patterns without requiring persistent model storage or feature drift monitoring. Deployed across many heterogeneous services, the system processes over 900K predictions per hour; in an offline evaluation on production traffic, its STL-based configuration reduces prediction error (MAPE) by 18.6\% relative to the previous production model. In production, the system has saved an estimated 118K servers daily.

</details>


### [22] [A Hierarchy of Risk Certificates for Representation-Based Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33333)
*Mohit Kumar,Somayeh Kargaran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究固定表示下Lipschitz解码器类的最优总体MSE，证明基于iid观测块（b≥2）的期望块分数是非降下界并收敛到最优类风险，进而在有界目标与预测下给出同时置信界，可用于风险认证与表示-解码器配置的选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已拟合预测器未达到精度要求，并不能证明其所在表示-解码器类无法达到该要求；因此需要一个关于整个解码器类的最优总体风险（而非单个模型的经验风险）的可证明评估与认证工具。

Method: 考虑固定表示上Lipschitz解码器的总体MSE最小化；对b≥2个iid观测构成的块，在成对Lipschitz约束下最小化经b-1归一化的总平方误差，以期望块分数构造最优类风险的下界；结合已验证的对偶下界与独立于模型开发的iid校准观测（有界目标与预测），构造最优类风险的同时下置信界与拟合预测器风险的上置信界。

Result: 证明期望块分数构成最优类风险的非降下界，并在紧致度量特征空间上收敛于该风险，但不存在对任意块规模一致的相对保证；所得置信界可判定所有可允许解码器均超过给定MSE阈值，或某个可允许的拟合预测器满足该阈值；受控实验展示总体风险认证，固定PTB-XL与Bike Sharing实验给出确定性的经验风险结论。

Conclusion: 结果为表示-解码器配置的原则性评估与选择提供理论支撑；大规模高效认证与依赖观测下的推广仍是未解决问题。

Abstract: Representation-based prediction underlies many machine-learning systems. Yet a fitted predictor's failure to meet an accuracy requirement does not establish that its representation-decoder class cannot meet it. We study optimal population mean squared error (MSE) over Lipschitz decoders acting on a fixed representation. For blocks of $b\geq2$ independent and identically distributed (iid) observations, we minimize total squared error (normalized by $b-1$) under pairwise Lipschitz constraints. We prove that expected block scores form nondecreasing lower bounds on optimal class risk and converge to that risk on compact metric feature spaces, although no fixed block size provides a uniform relative guarantee. Verified dual lower bounds and iid calibration observations independent of model development, with bounded targets and predictions, yield simultaneous lower confidence bounds on optimal class risk and upper confidence bounds on fitted-predictor risk. These bounds can establish that every admissible decoder exceeds a prescribed MSE threshold or that an admissible fitted predictor meets it. Controlled experiments illustrate population-risk certification; fixed PTB-XL and Bike Sharing experiments provide deterministic empirical-risk conclusions. The results support principled assessment and selection of representation-decoder configurations. Efficient certification at scale and extensions to dependent observations remain open problems.

</details>


### [23] [FlowersBench: A FluidFlower-Inspired Dataset for Benchmarking Carbon Storage Surrogates Across Geological Structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33335)
*Farah Rabie,Hannah Menke,Florian Doster,Amy Gough,Ahmed H. Elsheikh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FlowersBench：跨244种地质构造的5000个CO₂封存模拟基准数据集，用于评估代理模型对未见地质结构的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地质结构是地质碳封存（GCS）不确定性量化的主要来源，但现有基准数据集多在固定地质背景下仅改变岩石物理与运行参数，代理模型对未见地质结构的泛化能力尚缺乏系统评估。

Method: 构建含5000个二维储层尺度CO₂封存模拟、覆盖244种构造几何的基准数据集；几何由受FluidFlower实验与SPE11B启发的自研结构生成器采样，模拟采用OPM Flow，包含50年注入期与500年注入后监测期。测试几何完全从训练集剔除；另设两个子集考察结构偏移：其一将生成器参数范围外推至训练范围之外，其二引入多平行断层的未见拓扑。以U-Net代理模型预测CO₂气体饱和度作为基线。

Result: 在代表性测试案例中，代理模型能还原主要运移路径，包括低渗层下伏的侧向铺展及沿断层损伤带的向上流动，误差集中于羽流边界附近。两种结构偏移下的预测误差均高于留出几何，且相对误差增幅在未见断层拓扑情形下更大。

Conclusion: FlowersBench公开可用，为跨地质结构的GCS代理模型评估提供了统一基准，并表明结构偏移（尤其是拓扑变化）是当前代理模型泛化的主要挑战。

Abstract: Machine learning surrogates are emerging as a computationally efficient tool for uncertainty quantification in geological carbon storage (GCS). Geological structure constitutes a major source of uncertainty; however, existing benchmark simulation datasets predominantly vary petrophysical and operational parameters within a fixed geological setting. Generalisation to unseen geological structures therefore remains largely unexplored. We introduce FlowersBench, a benchmark dataset of 5,000 two-dimensional, reservoir-scale CO$_2$ storage simulations across 244 structural geometries. The geometries are sampled from an in-house structural generator inspired by the FluidFlower experiments and Case B of the 11th Society of Petroleum Engineers Comparative Solution Project (SPE11B). Each simulation is run with Open Porous Media (OPM) Flow over a 50-year injection period and a 500-year post-injection monitoring period. Test geometries are withheld from training to assess generalisation to unseen structural geometries. Two additional subsets support evaluation under structural shift. The first extends the parameter ranges of the structural generator beyond those used for training, and the second introduces an unseen topology of multiple parallel faults. A baseline U-Net surrogate is trained to predict CO$_2$ gas saturation. In representative test cases, the surrogate recovers the main migration pathways, including lateral spreading beneath low-permeability layers and upward flow along fault damage zones, with errors concentrated near the plume boundary. Under both structural shifts, prediction error exceeds that on the held-out geometries, and the increase in relative error is greater for the unseen fault topology. FlowersBench is publicly available and provides a common basis for evaluating GCS surrogates across geological structures.

</details>


### [24] [Understanding Failure Patterns in LLM-Based Mathematical Optimization Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33351)
*Ryoichi Takase,Xian Yeow Lee,Hikaru Tomonari,Masaya Tsunokake*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将LLM优化建模过程分解为数学推理、求解器使用与领域知识三部分，构建针对性数据集分析失败模式，发现不同组件关联不同类型的失败，且"可行但错误"是主导失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业优化约束复杂，建模自动化困难；LLM虽被用于自然语言生成优化模型，但端到端可靠性有限，常产生不可执行、不可行或可行但错误的解，需系统理解其失败来源。

Method: 将优化建模分解为数学推理、求解器使用、领域知识三个组件，并针对每个组件构建数据集；基于这些数据集分析各组件与错误类型的关系，并检验组件组合对执行可靠性的影响。

Result: 各组件与不同类型的失败相关联，但这种关联并不总是强且一致；组合组件可提升执行可靠性，但"可行但错误"的解仍是最主要失败模式，表明可执行性与正确性之间存在差距。

Conclusion: 现有数据集设计存在局限，需构建更好刻画约束完整性与模型表述准确性的数据，以提升LLM优化建模的可靠性。

Abstract: Industrial optimization involves complex constraints, making optimization modeling difficult to automate. Large language models (LLMs) have been explored for generating optimization models from natural language, but their reliability in end-to-end settings remains limited, often producing non-executable, infeasible, or feasible but incorrect solutions. In this work, we study failure modes in LLM-based optimization modeling by decomposing the process into three components: mathematical reasoning, solver usage, and domain knowledge, and constructing datasets that target each aspect. Using these datasets, we analyze how different components relate to errors and find that they are associated with different types of failure, although these relationships are not always strong or consistent. Furthermore, while combining these components improves execution reliability, feasible but incorrect solutions remain the dominant failure mode, suggesting a remaining gap between executability and correctness. These findings highlight limitations of current dataset designs and suggest the importance of data that better capture constraint completeness and formulation accuracy to enhance reliability.

</details>


### [25] [The Marauder's Map: Bézier Manifolds Reveal Hidden Surfaces for Model Merging and Ensembling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33353)
*Abhiram Iyer,Mark T. Harnett,Sarthak Chandra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Nimbus，学习连接 N 个专家模型的高维 Bézier 流形，仅需 3 阶交互修正即可实现多任务解决方案的高维连通区域，作为神经网络中"灵活简并性"的深度学习对应物。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物神经网络比深度网络更鲁棒，常归因于简并性（结构不同但功能相同）。但真实生物系统具有更灵活的简并性：专门化回路通过局部改变持续适应新能力同时保留原有功能。现有深度学习仅通过 mode connectivity（低损失曲线/曲面/体积连接同任务网络）研究简并性，缺少"灵活简并性"的对应物——即是否存在连接多个不同任务独立训练模型的高维多任务解区域。

Method: 提出 Nimbus，学习一个连接 N 个专家模型的高维 Bézier 流形，超越 mode connectivity 的一维路径与二维曲面。朴素参数化会随 N 组合爆炸，但 Nimbus 只需学习 3 个交互阶的修正，表明连接流形复杂度出乎意料地低却能承载多任务能力。所得流形形成可导航的"活点地图"（Marauder's Map），通过共享的胜任解几何连接各专家。

Result: 在视觉与语言领域训练，Nimbus 产生的流形在功能与局部权重上均平坦：从流形采样的集成在输入扰动与权重扰动下退化更平缓，优于基线，在 NLL 损失与校准误差上增益最大。

Conclusion: Nimbus 为灵活简并性提供了深度学习的解释，将模型连通性从"遥远解之间的低维路径"重构为"邻近解之间的高维体积"。

Abstract: Neural circuits are famously more robust than deep networks. This is often explained through degeneracy, where structurally distinct configurations support the same function. However, real biological systems exhibit a more flexible form of degeneracy in which specialized circuits continually adapt towards new competencies through localized changes while preserving existing function. Deep learning studies degeneracy through mode connectivity, which connects same-task networks by low-loss curves, sheets, or volumes. However, the field lacks a counterpart to flexible degeneracy, which demands more than ordinary mode connectivity. Defining such an analogue requires asking whether many independently initialized models trained on distinct tasks can be connected by a high-dimensional region of multi-task solutions. We introduce $\textit{Nimbus}$, which learns a high-dimensional Bézier manifold connecting $N$ specialists, going beyond the one-dimensional paths and two-dimensional sheets of mode connectivity work. A naive parameterization would scale combinatorially with $N$, but $\textit{Nimbus}$ requires learning corrections of only 3 interaction orders, showing that the connecting manifold has surprisingly low complexity yet supports multi-task competencies. The resulting manifold forms a navigable "Marauder's Map" that connects specialist models through a shared geometry of competent solutions. Trained across vision and language domains, $\textit{Nimbus}$ produces manifolds that are functionally and locally weight-flat: ensembles sampled from them degrade more gracefully than baselines under input and weight perturbations, with the largest gains in NLL loss and calibration error. $\textit{Nimbus}$ supplies a deep learning interpretation of flexible degeneracy, recasting model connectivity from a low-dimensional path between distant solutions into a high-dimensional volume of nearby ones.

</details>


### [26] [Discovering Explicit Magnetic Core-Loss Equations via Symbolic Sparse Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33364)
*Haoyu Wang,Jialin Zheng,Ziyang Xu,Yihao Wu,Alex Hanson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出符号稀疏辨识(SSI)框架，将磁芯损耗建模转化为数据驱动的符号回归问题，直接从测量数据恢复解析方程，兼顾精度与可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁芯损耗紧凑方程是磁性元件设计的关键工具，但现有方法存在精度与可解释性的权衡：经验模型(如Steinmetz方程)表达式紧凑但精度有限，机器学习方法精度高却是黑箱预测器。本文旨在弥合这一差距。

Method: SSI框架三步：1) 构建远超SE的、全面扩展的符号候选项库；2) 通过稀疏回归从库中分离主导项；3) 用AdamW优化器精修保留项的系数，以最大化精度并形成解析方程。该方法不预设函数形式。

Result: 在Fair-Rite 78、79、95材料上测试，测试集平均绝对百分比误差分别为1.89%、1.01%、1.56%，仅使用10、10、7个活跃项。与经验、数值和符号模型的对比量化了其精度与紧凑性的平衡。

Conclusion: SSI为高频磁性表征与设计提供了一种显式且具物理引导性的方法。

Abstract: Compact core loss equations are essential tools in magnetic component design, but existing approaches trade accuracy against interpretability. Empirical models such as the Steinmetz Equation (SE) yield compact expressions of limited accuracy, while machine learning methods achieve high accuracy as black-box predictors. This paper bridges the gap with a Symbolic Sparse Identification (SSI) framework, which casts core loss modeling as a data-driven symbolic regression problem and recovers unknown equations directly from measured data, without presuming a functional form. First, a comprehensively expanded library of symbolic candidate terms far richer than the SE is constructed. Second, the governing terms are isolated from this library by sparse regression. Third, the coefficients of the retained terms are refined with an AdamW optimizer to maximize accuracy and form an analytical equation. Experiments on Fair-Rite 78, 79, and 95 yield test mean absolute percentage errors of 1.89%, 1.01%, and 1.56%, respectively, with only 10, 10, and 7 active terms. Comparisons with empirical, numerical, and symbolic models quantify the resulting balance of accuracy and compactness. SSI thus provides an explicit and physically guided approach for high-frequency magnetic characterization and design.

</details>


### [27] [Federated Learning with Uncertainty Quantification on Pseudo Label Exchange](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33395)
*Jingqiao Tang,Phuoc-Toan Huynh,Richard Archibald,Feng Bao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出两阶段联邦随机算子学习框架FedSON：先通过交换本地确定性神经算子及其输入函数生成伪标签辅助数据，再在冻结的确定性算子上训练随机算子网络，实现异质分布下的联邦SPDE求解与不确定性量化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习可避免交换原始解数据，但传统联邦平均在客户端数据分布异质（尤其是输入函数支撑集不相交）时性能显著下降；同时SPDE求解还需刻画随机波动并量化不确定性。

Method: 两阶段框架：第一阶段交换本地训练的确定性神经算子及其输入函数，生成带伪标签的辅助数据，使联邦模型能跨异质输入分布学习且不共享原始解轨迹；第二阶段在冻结的确定性算子之上训练随机算子网络，刻画随机波动并量化预测解的不确定性。

Result: 在热方程、粘性Burgers方程和二维对流-扩散方程上的数值实验表明，FedSON在异质客户端分布下显著提升确定性预测精度，且不确定性估计与集中式训练相当。

Conclusion: FedSON通过辅助数据构造与随机算子学习的两阶段结合，有效缓解了联邦平均在异质分布下的退化问题，并在保护原始数据的前提下实现了接近集中式训练的不确定性量化能力。

Abstract: In this work, we investigate federated stochastic operator learning for stochastic partial differential equations (SPDEs), with an emphasis on heterogeneous client data distributions and uncertainty quantification. Federated learning enables clients to collaboratively train models without exchanging their original solution data, but conventional federated averaging can suffer significant performance degradation when client distributions differ, particularly when their input function supports are disjoint. To address these challenges, we propose the Federated Stochastic Operator Network (FedSON), a two-phase framework that combines auxiliary data construction with stochastic operator learning. In the first phase, locally trained deterministic neural operators and their corresponding input functions are exchanged to generate pseudolabeled auxiliary data, enabling the federated model to learn across heterogeneous input distributions without sharing original solution trajectories. In the second phase, a stochastic operator network is trained on top of the frozen deterministic operator to characterize stochastic fluctuations and quantify uncertainty in the predicted solutions. Numerical experiments involving the heat equation, viscous Burgers' equation, and two-dimensional advection-diffusion equation demonstrate the FedSON substantially improves deterministic prediction accuracy under heterogeneous client distributions while producing uncertainty estimates comparable to those obtained through centralized training.

</details>


### [28] [Sequentially Acquiring Concept Knowledge to Guide Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33399)
*Shivanand Kundargi,Kowshik Thopalli,Tejas Gokhale*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SACK是一个即插即用的持续学习方法，将类别知识拆解为细粒度概念，计算新旧概念间的语义关系并用其对样本排序优先训练，在正则化、回放、提示三类CL方法及类增量/任务增量设置下提升精度、降低遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CL方法主要围绕稳定性—可塑性权衡来缓解灾难性遗忘，但忽视了“新数据样本的顺序与性质”这一未充分探索的因素。作者认为CL算法应能根据样本与先验知识的关系及其对学习过程的影响对输入样本排序。

Method: 提出SACK（Sequentially Acquiring Concept Knowledge）：把模型的类别知识分解为细粒度概念；在每个experience中计算已学概念与新概念之间的关系；利用该关系知识对新样本进行打分与优先级排序，从而指导训练顺序。该方法为plug-and-play，可嵌入已有CL框架。

Result: 在正则化、回放与基于prompt的多种CL方法中，于class-incremental和task-incremental设置下实验：集成SACK不仅提升准确率、减少遗忘，还能处理长尾分布，使模型聚焦于语义可解释区域，并获得更好的校准性与鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 通过在样本层面引入基于语义概念关系的排序机制，SACK为持续学习提供了稳定性—可塑性之外的新视角，且作为通用插件具有良好的普适性与附加收益。

Abstract: The goal of continual learning (CL) is to adapt to new data (plasticity) while retaining the knowledge acquired from old data (stability). Existing methods are explicitly designed to focus on balancing stability and plasticity to mitigate the challenge of catastrophic forgetting while promoting learning. However, the impact of order and nature of samples from the new data that the network is trained on remains an under explored factor. We argue that a CL algorithm should have the ability to rank incoming samples in terms of their relationship with prior data and their effect on the learning process. In this work, we investigate if scoring and prioritizing incoming data based on their semantic relationships with the model's current knowledge can benefit CL. We propose SACK, short for Sequentially Acquiring Concept Knowledge, a plug and play technique for continual learning methods. SACK dissects categorical knowledge of the model into fine-grained concepts, computes the relationships between previously learned concepts and new concepts in each experience, and uses this relationship knowledge for prioritizing new samples. Experiments across several types of CL methods (regularization, replay, and prompt based) in class incremental and task incremental settings demonstrate that integration of SACK not only improves accuracy and reduces forgetting but also handles long tail distribution, helps focus on semantically interpretable regions, and yields better calibrated and robust models compared to prior approaches.

</details>


### [29] [Temporal Selective Exploration for Reinforcement Learning-Guided Continuous-Discrete Flow Matching in 3D Molecular Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33417)
*Lianghong Chen,Yan Yi Li,Gen Zhou,Yuxi Long,Ganlin Feng,Mike Domaratzki,Pingzhao Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种强化学习引导的连续-离散流匹配框架，配合时间选择性探索与奖励优势校准，用于3D从头分子设计，并在EGFR与Pin1上获得经计算验证的新颖抑制剂候选。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流匹配在生成任务中潜力显著，但其强化学习优化研究不足，尤其在连续-离散混合场景。一方面，流匹配以ODE形式给出确定性轨迹，不天然支持RL探索；常用的将整条轨迹转为SDE的随机化做法会带来冗余探索与额外噪声，因为并非所有时间步都影响最终结果。另一方面，多目标优化中部分目标会主导训练，导致其他目标更新不足。

Method: 构建RL引导的连续-离散流匹配框架：对原子坐标使用连续流匹配、对分子身份（离散类型）使用离散流匹配并联合优化；将随机探索限制在主要贡献目标属性的时间步上（时间选择性探索），以减少无效探索；引入基于奖励的优势校准（advantage calibration）机制，缓解多目标优化中的目标支配问题。应用于EGFR与Pin1靶点的抑制剂设计。

Result: 在成熟靶点EGFR与更具挑战性的Pin1上均识别出若干新颖候选抑制剂，并获得in silico（计算）验证支持。

Conclusion: 时间选择性探索与优势校准可有效提升流匹配的RL优化效率与多目标平衡性；连续-离散联合流匹配结合RL为3D从头分子设计提供了可行且有效的范式。

Abstract: Flow matching has shown strong potential in generative tasks, while its optimization via Reinforcement Learning (RL) remains underexplored, especially in continuous-discrete mixed settings. Moreover, flow matching is formulated as an Ordinary Differential Equation (ODE), whose deterministic trajectories do not naturally support RL optimization. Introducing stochasticity by converting the entire trajectory into a Stochastic Differential Equation (SDE) is a common practice. Nevertheless, not all timesteps contribute to the final outcomes, and redundant exploration introduces additional noise. Furthermore, in multi-objective settings, some objectives may dominate the optimization while others receive insufficient updates. In this study, we propose an RL-guided continuous-discrete flow matching framework with temporal selective exploration for 3D de novo molecular design. Specifically, our method jointly optimizes continuous and discrete flow matching for atomic coordinates and molecular identities, respectively, while restricting exploration to timesteps that primarily contribute to target properties, reducing ineffective exploration. We also introduce a reward-based advantage calibration mechanism to mitigate objective dominance in multi-objective optimization. The framework is applied to design inhibitors for both the well-studied target protein EGFR and the challenging Pin1 protein. We identify some novel candidate inhibitors, supported by in silico validation.

</details>


### [30] [Yet Another Concept Drift Benchmark? A Principled Framework for Evaluating Drift Detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33424)
*Lorenzo Iovine,Giacomo Ziffer,Emanuele Della Valle*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于显式漂移可观测性与报警语义的漂移检测基准框架，引入有限检测窗口与重复报警机制，分离检测准确性、及时性与稳定性，并给出归一化指标与综合评分，在10种检测器、9种学习器配置、16个数据集上做大规模压力测试验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有概念漂移检测的经验评估依赖定义松散的评价协议、语义模糊的报警定义与不一致的度量指标，导致结果难以复现与横向比较，缺乏可控、可解释的性能信号。

Method: 以漂移可观测性与报警语义的显式形式化为基础构建基准：引入有限检测窗口与重复报警概念，将检测准确性、及时性、稳定性解耦评估；采用可复现的“观察者”（observer）提供受控且可解释的性能信号；据此定义归一化评价指标及规避现有度量缺陷的综合得分。

Result: 在10种漂移检测器、9种学习器配置与16个数据集上开展大规模压力测试，验证了框架的可行性与判别力，并揭示不同检测器在准确性、及时性与稳定性维度上的差异。

Conclusion: 漂移检测评估需建立在明确的形式化语义与可复现协议之上；所提基准通过窗口化与重复报警机制实现多维度解耦评价与归一化综合打分，为检测器比较提供统一、可解释的标准。

Abstract: Concept drift detection is a fundamental component of data stream learning, yet its empirical evaluation remains based on loosely defined protocols, ambiguous alarm semantics, and inconsistent metrics. We propose a principled benchmark grounded in an explicit formulation of drift observability and alarm semantics. Our framework introduces finite detection windows and repeated alarms, enabling a clear separation between detection accuracy, timeliness, and stability, and employs a reproducible observer to provide a controlled and interpretable performance signal. Building on this formulation, we define normalized evaluation metrics and a composite score addressing limitations of existing measures. We validate the framework through a large-scale stress-testing evaluation of 10 detectors across 9 learner configurations and 16 datasets.

</details>


### [31] [Scalpel and Brush: Pruning and Targeted Weight Editing of a Small Language Model by a Single LLM Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33426)
*Sergei Koltcov,Vera Ignatenko,Anton Surkov,Rabie Abdultawab Ramadan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文将"LLM编辑自身权重"拆解为可对照的稀疏权重编辑框架，用实验区分agent贡献与随机/提示/标准流程带来的等效收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作显示LLM可以编辑语言模型权重，但无法判定性能提升究竟来自agent的智能搜索，还是可被同形状随机编辑、提示指令或无LLM的标准流程复现，因此需要统一、可控的归因实验。

Method: 将修复与剪枝统一为以指定指标（如BLEU）为目标的稀疏权重编辑，构造三类任务：定向权重修复（brush）、结构化MLP神经元剪枝（scalpel，剪枝权重置零）、带修复的层移除；由DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash驱动的Harness agent执行，开发集评估上限50次。对象为Qwen2.5-Math-1.5B-Instruct，每任务3次独立运行，并在两个隐藏测试划分上终评；另设随机编辑、提示指令、无LLM标准流程等对照。

Result: 常微分方程任务上，最多改动0.0084%权重使二阶方程测试BLEU提升24–32%，多项式右端方程提升1.8–2.5倍；独立替换检验表明BLEU提升反映符号/记法对齐而非正确性提高。剪除12.5–15% MLP神经元使模型缩小9.4–11.2%，两个隐藏划分上平均BLEU损失≤0.01；而按层等比例的标准剪枝在所有测试比例下均违反约束。GoEmotions七分类上，编辑使macro-F1由0.100升至0.396–0.451（test）、由0.175升至0.455–0.476（holdout）；写入agent选定权重行的岭分类器达0.424与0.464。相同评估预算下剪枝搜索去除15.9%神经元，agent运行通过隐藏检验者为14–21.4%。

Conclusion: 一旦给定agent找到的干预位置与形式，无LLM的标准流程即可复现大部分性能，说明增益主要来自"编辑什么、在哪里编辑"的定位，而非LLM本身；agentic与非agentic搜索的优劣比较仍待解决，指标提升的语义有效性也需更严格验证。

Abstract: It has recently been shown that an LLM can itself edit the weights of a language model, yet it remains unclear which part of such a result is due to the agent and which part is reproduced by a random edit of the same shape, by an instruction in the prompt, or by a standard procedure without an LLM. We unify repair and pruning as sparse weight edits to optimize a metric we specify, using BLEU as an example: three tasks -- targeted weight repair ("brush"), structured MLP neuron pruning ("scalpel", zeroing pruned weights), and layer removal with repair - are assigned to a DeepSeek Harness agent powered by DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash, with up to 50 dev evaluations. We study Qwen2.5-Math-1.5B-Instruct in three independent runs per task, with final evaluation on two hidden splits. On ordinary differential equations, changing at most 0.0084% of weights raised test BLEU by 24-32% on second-order equations and by a factor of 1.8-2.5 on equations with polynomial right-hand sides. An independent substitution check showed that higher BLEU reflects notation alignment rather than improved correctness. Masks that remove 12.5-15% of MLP neurons shrink the model by 9.4-11.2% with mean BLEU loss of at most 0.01 on both hidden splits, whereas standard pruning with equal shares per layer failed the constraint at all tested shares. On seven-class GoEmotions classification, edits raise macro-F1 from 0.100 to 0.396-0.451 on test and from 0.175 to 0.455-0.476 on holdout. A ridge-based classifier written into agent-selected weight rows yields macro-F1 of 0.424 and 0.464. Pruning search with the same evaluation limit removes 15.9% of neurons versus 14-21.4% for agent runs passing the hidden check. Standard procedures without an LLM reproduce much of the performance once given the location and form of intervention found by the agent; a comparison of agentic and non-agentic search remains open.

</details>


### [32] [Fast Muscle-Driven Whole-Body Motion Tracking with a Differentiable World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33460)
*Kaibo He,Maciej Bakowicz,Shihe Zhou,Yusen Feng,Yanan Sui,Dhanush Radhakrishnan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将 SuperMimic（基于世界模型回传的跟踪策略训练）扩展到 700 肌肉与 354 肌肉全身肌骨模型，通过一套优化训练配方在单张 RTX 5090 上将训练时间缩短约 34–37 倍，并保持或超越原超参的跟踪精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全身肌骨模型的动作捕捉跟踪需在接触条件下协调数百块肌肉，现有控制器训练需数小时至数天 GPU 时间，调参成本极高，亟需快速可用的训练方案。

Method: 在世界模型内部嵌入 MuJoCo 的激活动力学，并对其 rollout 反向传播训练跟踪策略。加速配方沿用 Unitree G1 的加速技巧，替换其中 3 项在肌肉上失效的设置，并新增 4 项改动：无肌肉几何的观测、数据采集与参数更新并发、保证激励始终位于 [0,1] 的 Beta 探索噪声、50 Hz 控制频率。另在测试时利用世界模型对策略动作进行精炼。

Result: 单张 RTX 5090 上，MS-Human-700 用 6.3 分钟达到原超参 3.9 小时（37×）的留出回报，20 分钟达到低于其 10 小时的跟踪误差；MyoFullBody 无需重调即加速 34×。消融显示：更高策略学习率使一半训练停滞；截断高斯噪声引入持续的协同收缩（tonic co-contraction），而 Beta 噪声可避免。测试时动作精炼使位置误差再降 26%，无留出片段失败，达到同等回报的训练时间由 6.3 分钟降至 5.1 分钟。

Conclusion: 针对肌肉驱动特性的定制化加速配方（尤其是探索噪声分布与控制频率）是实现肌骨跟踪策略快速训练的关键；世界模型可在测试时进一步提升精度，为全身肌骨运动跟踪提供实用、低成本的训练流程。

Abstract: Tracking motion capture with a whole-body musculoskeletal model means coordinating hundreds of muscles through contact, and current controllers take hours to days of GPU time to train, so every tuning decision is expensive. SuperMimic trains a tracking policy by backpropagating through the rollouts of a learned world model. We extend it to MS-Human-700 (700 muscles) and MyoFullBody (354 muscles), with MuJoCo's activation dynamics inside the world model. To make its training fast, our recipe takes the speedups developed for the Unitree G1, replaces three of their settings that fail on muscles, and adds four changes of our own: observations without muscle geometry, data collection concurrent with the updates, Beta exploration noise that keeps every excitation in $[0,1]$, and 50 Hz control. On one RTX 5090 it trains MS-Human-700 in 6.3 minutes to the held-out return that SuperMimic's published hyperparameters reach in 3.9 hours, 37$\times$ sooner, and in 20 minutes to lower tracking errors than theirs after 10 hours. It trains MyoFullBody 34$\times$ sooner without retuning. Ablations show what is specific to muscles: a higher policy learning rate stalls half of the runs, and clipped Gaussian noise adds a tonic co-contraction that Beta noise avoids. Finally, refining the policy's actions through the world model at test time lowers the position error by a further 26% with no held-out clip failing, and reaches the same return after 5.1 instead of 6.3 minutes of training. Code, the training recipe and videos are on the project page https://muscle-supermimic.beanpow.top.

</details>


### [33] [Differentially Private Policy Optimization in Linear MDPs with Adversarial Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33469)
*Yi He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究自适应对抗线性奖励、转移未知的回合制线性MDP下联合差分隐私（JDP）的策略优化，提出批量PPO结合乐观策略评估的算法，在隐私保护下保持与非隐私情形相同的K^{3/4}主导遗憾阶，隐私仅带来低阶附加代价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化服务中的强化学习依赖敏感交互数据，需在保护轨迹与奖励函数的前提下进行策略优化；该设定存在两大挑战：历史轨迹被反复用于策略评估，奖励选择可能依赖已发布的私有统计量。

Method: 在全信息反馈的回合制线性MDP（转移未知、自适应对抗线性奖励）中，将批量近端策略优化（PPO）更新与乐观策略评估结合，使用私有回归统计量与奖励估计，在联合差分隐私（JDP）框架下同时保护轨迹与奖励函数。

Result: 高概率遗憾界在固定其余参数时保持与非隐私批量乐观PPO相同的K^{3/4}主导项；隐私相关项分别为K^{5/8}与K^{1/2}量级，故隐私仅引入关于回合数K的低阶加性代价，且在自适应对抗奖励下依然成立。

Conclusion: 差分隐私可与批量乐观PPO有效结合，在自适应对抗奖励等较强设定下实现隐私-效用近乎无损的权衡。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) in personalized services often relies on sensitive interaction data. Motivated by the need to protect these data, we study differentially private (DP) policy optimization in episodic linear Markov decision processes (MDPs) with unknown transitions and adaptive adversarial linear rewards under full-information feedback. This setting poses two key challenges: past trajectories are repeatedly reused for policy evaluation, and reward choices may depend on previously released private statistics. The proposed algorithm combines batched proximal policy optimization (PPO) updates with optimistic policy evaluation, using private regression statistics and reward estimates to protect both trajectories and reward functions under joint differential privacy (JDP). With all other parameters fixed, our high-probability regret bound retains the same leading $\widetilde{O}\left(K^{3/4}\right)$ dependence on the number of episodes $K$ as the corresponding non-private bound for batched optimistic PPO. The additional privacy terms scale as $\widetilde{O} \left(K^{5/8}\right)$ and $\widetilde{O} \left(K^{1/2}\right)$. Thus, privacy incurs only lower-order additive costs in $K$, even under adaptive adversarial rewards.

</details>


### [34] [The Geometry Is the Memory: Task-Density Routing for Continual LoRA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33487)
*Alireza Heidari,Thomas Veasey,Mariano Tepper*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CONTLORA用紧凑的类别统计（高斯类模型 + 累积特征矩）替代存储样本，实现免样本的适配器选择与分类；在20任务CIFAR-100达88.55%、CUB-200达86.99%，与存储特征基线相差≤0.07个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冻结旧适配器并不能保证持续学习器在推理时正确选择和使用它们；当存在多个适配器时，如何在它们之间路由、并识别出与查询答案表征空间匹配的适配器十分困难。同时，现有方法多依赖样本回放（存储图像/特征），存在存储与隐私代价。

Method: 提出CONTLORA：以紧凑类别统计取代存储样本。在冻结表征中用高斯类模型进行LoRA适配器选择；对固定训练特征，用累积特征矩精确复现类平衡岭分类器。训练过程不保留图像或特征向量。

Result: 20任务CIFAR-100准确率88.55%，CUB-200为86.99%；在匹配组件研究的三个基准上，其最终平均准确率与独立训练的存储特征对照版本相差至多0.07个百分点。消融显示任务覆盖提升不一定带来分类提升，适配器组合方式同样重要；在留出类上，以竞争类为中心的似然分数在不改变路由排序的前提下提升新颖性AUROC。

Conclusion: 在所评估设定下，统计记忆可作为样本保留式方法的替代方案；任务歧义（task ambiguity）仍是持续存在的局限。

Abstract: Freezing older adapters does not ensure that a continual learner will select and use them properly at inference time. When multiple adapters exist, routing among them and identifying the adapter whose representation space matches the query's answer can be difficult. We propose CONTLORA, which replaces stored examples with compact class statistics for adapter selection and classification. Gaussian class models in a frozen representation select LoRA adapters, while accumulated feature moments exactly recover a class-balanced ridge classifier for fixed training features. Trained without retaining images or feature vectors, CONTLORA reaches 88.55% accuracy on 20-task CIFAR-100 and 86.99% on CUB-200. Across three benchmarks in the matched component study, its mean final accuracy differs from an independently trained stored-feature counterpart by at most 0.07 percentage points. Ablations show that improved task coverage need not improve classification and adapter composition also matters. On held-out classes, competitor-centered likelihood scores improve novelty AUROC over raw likelihood without altering routing ranks. These results support statistical memory as an alternative to example-retention approaches in the evaluated settings, while identifying task ambiguity as an ongoing limitation.

</details>


### [35] [Evaluation and Restoration of Safety in Reasoning-Tuned LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33515)
*Ehsan Latif,Roberto Dailey,Risto Miikkulainen,Elliot Meyerson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 开源推理优化大模型（如 VibeThinker）虽提升了可及性，但缺乏监管与外部护栏；本文提出评估推理优化模型安全性的指标，发现推理能力增强反而提高不安全率，并提出 ESPG 方法（进化策略+后训练护栏）以低成本恢复安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源推理优化 LLM 广泛可得但缺乏监管与外部护栏，安全集成到人类社会至关重要。流行观点认为提升推理能力会改善安全性，但近期证据表明可能相反，因此需要刻画并缓解这一问题，为开源模型提供安全的推理后训练路径。

Method: 1) 构建用于评估推理优化 LLM 安全性的度量指标；2) 在多个模型家族上分析推理强度与不安全率的关系；3) 提出 ESPG（Evolution Strategies 结合后训练护栏）方法，作为 GRPO 等安全后训练方法的替代方案。

Result: 在多个模型家族中，推理能力增强均导致不安全率上升（所有情况下皆成立）；ESPG 相较 GRPO 等替代方法实现简单、除推理显存外无需额外 GPU 内存、仅需极少训练样本（如 30 条）即可显著提升模型安全性。

Conclusion: 推理能力提升未必带来安全提升，反而可能加剧不安全行为；ESPG 提供了一种低成本、易实现的安全后训练方案，为开源 AI 模型的安全后训练建立了可行路径。

Abstract: Open-source LLMs that are optimized for reasoning, such as VibeThinker, aim to democratize AI by bringing high-capability models to users with limited resources. They are broadly available but lack regulation and external guardrails; therefore, in order to integrate them safely into human society, it is critical that they perform in a safe manner. There are popular and intuitive arguments for why improved reasoning should improve safety, but recent evidence suggests that, in fact, the opposite may be true. This paper aims to characterize and mitigate this issue. First, it develops a metric for evaluating LLMs optimized for reasoning, and shows that increasing reasoning across multiple model families increases the unsafe rate in all cases. Second, it proposes a method for restoring safety by combining evolution strategies with a post-training guardrail (ESPG). Compared to alternative methods for safety post-training such as GRPO, ESPG is simple to implement, requires no GPU memory beyond that of inference, and works with remarkably few training samples (e.g.\ 30), yet makes the model significantly safer. The study thus establishes a path towards safe post-training of open-source AI models.

</details>


### [36] [A Perturbation-Driven Framework for Sensitivity Analysis of Dimensionality Reduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33523)
*Md Afridi Hasan,Jaemin Jo,Takanori Fujiwara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种模型无关、以扰动为驱动的降维方法敏感性评估框架，通过多次扰动输入并计算结构保持度量（trustworthiness、continuity等），得到各DR方法的敏感性剖面，并用于方法比较、行为分析与鲁棒性改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实数据是潜在分布的有限、含噪样本，DR方法不应在数据微小变化下产生显著不同的结构；而传统失真度量只衡量单次嵌入的结构保持，未刻画对合理输入变化的敏感性。
