Method: 构建模型无关的扰动驱动敏感性评估框架：将扰动视为对合理数据变化的受控探针；在多种扰动族与强度水平下反复扰动输入、执行DR、再用一组失真度量评价低维表示，从而为每个DR方法导出敏感性剖面；并结合案例研究进行方法比较、行为分析与鲁棒性改进。

Result: 常规失真度量表现良好的方法（如t-SNE）仍可能高度敏感，导致轨迹、簇等视觉显著结构不稳定；而某些方法（如MDS）在特定扰动下结构保持反而改善。

Conclusion: 敏感性分析对比较DR方法、诊断扰动敏感的视觉模式以及指导更鲁棒的DR方法发展、实现可信可视分析具有重要意义。

Abstract: Low-dimensional representations produced by dimensionality reduction (DR) are commonly evaluated using distortion measures such as trustworthiness and continuity. In this paper, we study the sensitivity of DR methods to plausible input variations by quantifying changes in structural preservation and visually diagnosing how these changes manifest in the resulting representations. This perspective reflects the fact that real-world datasets are finite, noisy samples of an underlying distribution, and DR should not produce substantially different structures under small data changes. We introduce a model-agnostic, perturbation-driven sensitivity evaluation framework that treats perturbations as controlled probes of plausible data variation. Across multiple perturbation families and strength levels, we repeatedly perturb the input, apply a DR method, and evaluate the resulting low-dimensional representations using a set of distortion measures to derive sensitivity profiles for each DR method. We demonstrate the utility of our framework through case studies on DR method comparison, behavior analysis, and robustness improvement. We find that methods performing well according to conventional distortion measures (e.g., t-SNE) can still exhibit substantial sensitivity, leading to instability in visually salient structures such as trajectories and clusters, while certain methods (e.g., multidimensional scaling) may exhibit improved structural preservation under specific perturbations. These results highlight the importance of sensitivity analysis for comparing DR methods, diagnosing perturbation-sensitive visual patterns, and guiding the development of more robust DR methods toward trustworthy visual analytics.

</details>


### [37] [Deletion You Can Check: Auditing LLM Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33525)
*Mayank Gulati,Gerhard Wunder*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文指出"成员推断分数接近随机(ROC-AUC≈0.5)即表示遗忘成功"这一常见判据缺乏验证,通过2×2对照实验揭示基准对照群体可能存在偏差,并发现无配置通过三重判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有近似遗忘(unlearning)评估普遍以成员推断分数接近随机作为"已遗忘被删除记录"的判据,但该解读成立的前提是对比群体能真实反映记录在有效删除后的行为(反事实),而这一前提极少被验证。

Method: 构建受控2×2研究:两个MIA基准(ArxivMIA、BookMIA)×两个语言模型(Llama-3.2-1B、Qwen2.5-1.5B)。用精确重训练(exact retraining)作为成员性参照来验证"随机"基线;事后对BookMIA/Qwen单元做诊断,以同池、从未微调的记录替换标签为0的记录;并联合评估三类近似遗忘方法(GA+GD、NPO-RT、SCRUB)的检查点在成员移除、超出非删除波动的删除目标偏移、效用三方面表现。

Result: 精确重训练的成员参照在ArxivMIA上落入预设随机区间,但在BookMIA上达到0.122,导致同一指标反而拒绝了经验删除参照;诊断替换后该单元参照由0.257升至0.523,支持群体失配。在通过准入的ArxivMIA/Llama轴上,GA+GD、NPO-RT、SCRUB均无配置通过联合判据。

Conclusion: 在参照明确"随机"含义之前,接近随机的分数不能作为遗忘证据;现有近似遗忘评估的"随机"基线需经反事实验证,否则会产生误判。

Abstract: A common indication that a fine-tuned model has forgotten a deleted record is a membership-inference score near chance (ROC-AUC $\approx 0.5$). This reading is sound only if the comparison population represents how the record should behave after a valid deletion---a counterfactual that is rarely validated. We test this premise in a controlled $2\times2$ study pairing two membership-inference benchmarks, ArxivMIA and BookMIA, with two language models, Llama-3.2-1B and Qwen2.5-1.5B. Exact retraining validates the membership reference: it lies within the prespecified chance band on ArxivMIA but reaches $0.122$ on BookMIA, causing the same metric to reject an empirical deletion reference. A post-hoc BookMIA/Qwen diagnostic replaces benchmark label-0 records with same-pool, never-fine-tuned records and moves the reference from $0.257$ to $0.523$, supporting population mismatch in that cell. Each approximate-unlearning checkpoint is judged jointly on membership removal, deletion-targeted shift beyond non-deletion variation, and utility. On the admitted ArxivMIA/Llama axis, no tested configuration of gradient ascent with retain descent (GA+GD), negative-preference optimization with retain training (NPO-RT), or teacher--student unlearning (SCRUB) passes. A score near chance is not evidence of forgetting until a reference establishes what chance means.

</details>


### [38] [Casper: A Projection-Based Neurosymbolic Layer for Scalable & Guaranteed Constraint Satisfaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33527)
*Lohith Konathala,Luca Andolfi,Eleonora Giunchiglia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Casper 是一种可微神经符号层，将约束满足建模为到可行域的闭式欧氏投影，单次前向即完成，同时保证约束满足、可规模化推理、全自动化且可与任意神经网络组合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实问题常要求严格满足约束，但现有神经符号方法存在权衡：能保证满足的无法扩展，能扩展的只能近似；传统深度学习则完全不保证满足。作者希望同时实现保证满足、可扩展、自动化与可微性。

Method: 提出 Casper 神经符号层，把约束满足转化为对约束可行域的闭式欧氏投影（closed-form Euclidean projection），可在一次前向传播中计算，因而完全可微，训练与推理阶段均可与任意神经预测器组合，且无需问题特定的工程。

Result: 在 MNIST 算术（最多 1024 位）、数独求解与 Warcraft 寻路任务上，Casper 是唯一能在大规模下产出保证有效输出的方法：30×30 Warcraft 网格上，精确基线超时、近似方法有效路径率低于 7%，而 Casper 达到 100%。

Conclusion: Casper 以闭式投影方式统一了约束保证、可扩展性与可微性，为可组合的神经符号约束满足提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Many real-world problems demand exact constraint satisfaction to be meaningfully solved, yet existing neurosymbolic methods face a stark trade-off: those that guarantee satisfaction fail to scale, while those that scale must resort to approximation. Meanwhile, traditional deep learning approaches offer no satisfaction guarantees at all. In this paper, we propose Casper, a neurosymbolic layer that simultaneously (i) guarantees constraint satisfaction, (ii) scales at inference time, (iii) is fully automated, requiring no problem-specific engineering, and (iv) is fully differentiable, making it composable with any neural predictor both at training and at inference time. Casper casts constraint satisfaction as a closed-form Euclidean projection onto the constraint-feasible region, computable in a single forward pass. Experiments across MNIST arithmetic (up to 1024 digits), Sudoku solving, and Warcraft pathfinding show that Casper is the only method that produces guaranteed-valid outputs at scale: on $30\times30$ Warcraft grids, exact baselines time out and approximate ones produce valid paths less than 7% of the time, while Casper produces them 100% of the time.

</details>


### [39] [DiffER$^2$: Diffusion Ensembles for Retrosynthesis Prediction with SMILES Adapted Particle Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33557)
*Sean Current,Daniel Adu-Ampratwum,Xia Ning,Srinivasan Parthasarathy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该工作将离散时间与连续时间扩散模型集成用于SMILES序列的反合成预测，并把粒子引导（particle guidance）适配到SMILES生成，在提升唯一分子数量的同时提高top-k准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算逆合成可自动为目标产物生成候选反应，从而缩短新药研发周期；现有数据驱动方法多把该任务视为SMILES上的序列到序列生成，输出多样性不足，需要提升采样多样性与准确性。

Method: 构建离散时间与连续时间两类扩散模型的集成（ensembles）进行分子生成，引入引导机制以改善输出多样性；并提出将particle guidance适配到SMILES序列生成的方案。

Result: 适配后的particle guidance在扩散集成中显著提升了唯一生成分子数量，同时提高了top-k准确率。

Conclusion: 结果进一步拓展了离散扩散在SMILES生成中的有效性，实证分析为扩散模型在化学逆合成中的能力提供了新见解。

Abstract: Computational retrosynthesis prediction has the potential to reduce development time for newly discovered drugs by automatically generating potential reactions for a target product. Data-driven approaches commonly treat this as a sequence-to-sequence generation task on SMILES strings. In this work, we construct ensembles of discrete-time and continuous-time diffusion models for molecular generation and incorporate guidance mechanisms for improved output diversity. We propose an adaptation of particle guidance to SMILES sequence generation which significantly improves the number of unique molecules generated by diffusion ensembles while increasing top-k accuracy. These results further expand the efficacy of discrete diffusion for SMILES generation, and our empirical analyses offer new insights into the capabilities of diffusion models for chemical retrosynthesis.

</details>


### [40] [Trigger-Invariant Contrastive Unlearning for Backdoor Removal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33571)
*Santosh Chapagain,Shah Muhammad Hamdi,Soukaina Filali Boubrahimi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出触发不变对比遗忘框架TICU，通过强制模型对触发词插入保持预测与表征不变来消除文本后门。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于梯度上升（GA）的后门遗忘方法通过最大化中毒样本损失来移除后门，但存在关键缺陷：可能仅将触发词的影响转移到其他类别，而非真正消除触发效应。

Method: TICU构建干净样本与插入触发的反事实样本对，微调模型使二者输出分布一致且隐层表征对齐；同时保留干净数据上的监督分类损失，并约束参数相对原模型的漂移，以维持干净任务性能。

Result: 在多数据集、多模型架构与多攻击设置下，TICU相比已有基线提升了触发准确率、减少了触发引起的预测漂移，并保持有竞争力的标签翻转性能与较小的干净任务性能退化。

Conclusion: 以"触发不变性"为核心约束的对比遗忘范式可有效缓解文本后门行为，同时兼顾干净任务效用，优于仅依赖损失最大化的传统遗忘方法。

Abstract: Backdoor attacks pose a severe threat to modern NLP models by embedding hidden triggers that induce malicious behavior at inference time. Recent unlearning methods such as gradient ascent (GA) attempt to remove backdoors by maximizing the loss on poisoned samples; however, these methods can suffer from a critical limitation: they may shift the trigger's influence to other classes rather than eliminating it. In this work, we propose Trigger-Invariant Contrastive Unlearning (TICU), a framework for mitigating textual backdoor behavior by enforcing prediction and representation invariance to trigger insertion. TICU constructs clean and trigger-inserted counterfactual pairs, then fine-tunes the model so that both inputs produce consistent output distributions and aligned hidden representations. To preserve clean-task performance, TICU also retains the supervised classification loss on clean data and constrains parameter drift from the original model. Extensive experiments across multiple datasets, model architectures, and attack settings show that TICU improves triggered accuracy and reduces trigger-induced prediction shifts compared with existing baselines, while maintaining competitive label-flipping performance and limiting clean-task degradation.

</details>


### [41] [Amortizing Exact Orthogonality: Drift-Triggered Restoration for Orthogonal Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33589)
*Lanxin Zhao,Xiaoyu Li,Dai Shi,Junbin Gao,Andi Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出RTOFT：用一阶切向更新W(I+S)（S反对称）并在漂移‖S‖₂达到容差τ时才做极分解恢复精确正交性；因I+S的正交性缺陷恰为‖S‖₂²，故每次前向传播的缺陷≤τ²。在GLUE上训练时间降至LoRA的0.97–1.13×，块对角版本六任务平均超出LoRA 1.98±0.39分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OFT方法在每个优化步都构造精确或近似正交变换，开销大；且OFTv2截断Cayley-Neumann级数时，截断阶数并不决定正交性缺陷——参考实现默认阶数下检查点中各block缺陷跨越五个数量级，缺乏可控的缺陷保证。

Method: 以正交性缺陷‖AᵀA−I‖₂度量偏离程度。RTOFT对权重W做一阶切向更新W(I+S)，S反对称，此时I+S的缺陷恰为‖S‖₂²；每次更新后读取漂移‖S‖₂，仅当达到容差τ时用极分解恢复精确正交性，并在训练结束做最终恢复，从而保证每个前向传播的缺陷上界为τ²，且与损失、数据、模型、优化器无关。

Result: GLUE上块对角、子空间、蝴蝶三种参数化的训练时间由LoRA的1.10–1.58×降至0.97–1.13×；块对角版本五seed六任务平均超LoRA 1.98±0.39分。τ=0.10时训练中最大漂移在DeBERTaV3、Qwen与扩散模型上均处于0.10–0.12，而每epoch恢复一次时为0.17–1.65。消融的七个设置中，无恢复的切向更新均劣于带恢复版本；每步恢复相比按容差恢复无显著精度增益，且每步开销高4.5–27.5%。

Conclusion: 以漂移阈值触发极分解的“按需恢复”策略可在保证正交性缺陷有界（≤τ²）的同时避免每步正交化开销，性能与效率均优于LoRA及现有OFT变体；缺陷应由漂移量而非截断阶数控制。

Abstract: Orthogonal finetuning (OFT) adapts a pretrained weight by an orthogonal transform and preserves its singular values and row angles, but existing methods construct an exact or approximate orthogonal transform at every optimization step. We measure how far a transform $A$ is from orthogonal by its orthogonality defect $\|A^\top A-I\|_2$. For OFTv2, which truncates a Cayley-Neumann series, we show that the truncation order does not determine the defect. At its default order, block defects in checkpoints from the reference implementation span five orders of magnitude. We propose Restarted Tangent Orthogonal Finetuning (RTOFT), which uses the first-order update $W(I+S)$ of a weight $W$ with a skew-symmetric $S$ and restores exact orthogonality by a polar decomposition only when the drift $\|S\|_2$ reaches a tolerance $τ$. The defect of $I+S$ is exactly $\|S\|_2^2$. Therefore, reading the drift after every update and restoring before the next forward pass guarantees a defect of at most $τ^2$ in every forward pass, independently of the loss, data, model, and optimizer. A final restoration makes the returned transform exactly orthogonal. On GLUE, RTOFT reduces the training time of block-diagonal, subspace, and butterfly parameterizations from $1.10$-$1.58\times$ to $0.97$-$1.13\times$ that of LoRA, and its block-diagonal version exceeds LoRA by $1.98\pm0.39$ points on the six-task average over five seeds. At $τ=0.10$, the largest drift read during training stays between $0.10$ and $0.12$ across DeBERTaV3, Qwen, and diffusion models, whereas it ranges from $0.17$ to $1.65$ when restoring once per epoch. In all seven settings of our ablation, the tangent update is worse without restoration than with it, and restoring after every step yields no measurable accuracy gain over restoring at the tolerance and costs $4.5$-$27.5\%$ more per step.

</details>


### [42] [State Space Attention: Learning Context-Dependent Memory Retention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33625)
*Ege Çakar,Cengiz Pehlevan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出State Space Attention (SSA)，将"保留哪些过去表示"变为可学习的选择：用一个学习到的打分器对每个chunk相对于新到上下文及记忆其余部分重打分，保持有界记忆并用标准注意力读取。固定窗口Transformer是其特例。检索、推理与语言建模实验显示SSA在多数任务上接近全注意力，显著优于同等可读预算的Transformer，推理内存恒定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准注意力KV cache随上下文长度线性增长；要对其进行有界化，必须决定序列模型为长上下文推理应保留什么信息。作者主张这一选择应当被学习，而非依赖固定启发式（如保留最近token的滑动窗口）。

Method: 1) 学习一个有界记忆：打分器对每个chunk依据新到上下文与记忆其余部分重新打分，决定保留集合；2) 用标准注意力读取保留的记忆，故固定上下文Transformer为"总是保留最近token"的特例；3) 打分器与读取器联合训练，可先从可微的软加权记忆逐步过渡到硬保留，也可直接硬保留；4) 训练中生成监督样本为打分器提供信号，称为recovery；5) 与循环模型、有界记忆注意力方法、全注意力Transformer对比，覆盖检索、变量追踪、多跳推理与语言建模；6) 预训练并后训练400M参数模型，测量训练与推理开销随上下文长度和模型规模的变化。

Result: 在检索与推理基准上，SSA在多数任务上达到或与全注意力相差数点以内；在所有评估的长上下文检索任务上远超同等可读预算的Transformer，并达到或超过约4倍该预算的模型；推理内存恒定。小规模语言模型下与全注意力的最大剩余差距出现在RULER上。

Conclusion: SSA使固定数量的token在更长上下文上发挥作用；由于以标准注意力读取记忆，可与KV cache eviction方法叠加。作者认为实验整体考察了"学习式记忆保留"在长上下文序列建模中的能力与局限，剩余差距（尤其RULER）是主要局限。

Abstract: Standard attention's key-value cache grows linearly with context length. Bounding it raises the question of what a sequence model should retain for long-context reasoning. We argue this choice should be learned, and introduce State Space Attention (SSA), which learns to keep a bounded set of past representations and reads them with standard attention. A learned scorer rescores every chunk against newly arriving context and the rest of memory. A fixed-context Transformer is then the special case that always keeps the most recent tokens. We train the scorer and reader jointly, either moving gradually from a differentiable soft weighting of memories to hard retention, or with hard retention from the start. To produce signal for the scorer, we generate supervised samples during training, a technique we call recovery. We compare SSA with recurrent models, bounded-memory attention methods, and full-attention Transformers across retrieval, variable tracking, multi-hop reasoning, and language modeling. On our retrieval and reasoning benchmarks, SSA matches or comes within a few points of full attention on most tasks, far outperforms a Transformer with the same readable budget on every long-context retrieval task we evaluate, and matches or outperforms one with about 4 times that budget, while using constant inference memory. The largest remaining gap to full attention at small language model scale is on RULER. SSA makes a fixed number of tokens useful over a longer context, and since it reads memory with standard attention, we propose that it can be stacked with KV cache eviction methods. We also pretrain and post-train 400M-parameter models and measure how SSA's training and inference costs scale with context length and model size. Together, these experiments examine the capabilities and limitations of learned memory retention for long-context sequence modeling.

</details>


### [43] [Pretraining for Forecasting in Relational Databases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33645)
*Ziyue Lin,Sebastian Regalado Lozano,Michael Guerzhoy,Ricardo Baptista,George Stein*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Query-Conditioned Relational pretraining (QCRP)自监督框架，使标准关系深度学习（RDL）骨干网络通过结构化时间查询语法生成多样化预测目标，学到可冻结复用的通用预测表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实预测任务依赖关系数据库中异步的事务日志，而非均匀采样的数组。现有RDL模型通常针对每个特定预测目标和根实体类型分别训练，无法在全库范围内通用，复用性差。

Method: QCRP用结构化时间查询语法生成多样的预测目标（不同目标、不同根实体类型），并通过共享的条件预测器交错训练，得到稳健的可冻结编码器；下游任务可通过轻量预测器或零样本接口复用该表示。

Result: 在大型RelBench数据库上评估：学到的冻结表示可支持跨任务、跨实体类型的竞争性预测；单个统一QCRP模型检查点与各实体专门训练的独立专家模型性能相当。

Conclusion: 验证了复用标准RDL骨干网络所学表示用于关系数据库预测任务的可行性，为构建数据库级通用预测器提供了有效路径。

Abstract: Real-world forecasting increasingly relies on asynchronous transaction logs in relational databases rather than uniformly sampled arrays. While relational deep learning (RDL) models can forecast from these complex histories, they are typically trained separately for each specific target and root entity type. We introduce Query-Conditioned Relational pretraining (QCRP), a self-supervised framework that enables a standard RDL backbone to act as a universal forecaster across a database. QCRP generates diverse forecasting objectives using a structured temporal query grammar and interleaves them through a shared conditional predictor. This produces a robust, frozen encoder reusable for downstream tasks via lightweight predictors or a zero-shot interface. Evaluated on large-scale RelBench databases, QCRP learns frozen representations that support competitive forecasting across tasks and entity types. Furthermore, a single unified QCRP model checkpoint is competitive with isolated, entity-specific specialists. We demonstrate promising results for reusing the learned representations from standard RDL backbones for forecasting tasks in relational databases.

</details>


### [44] [The Danger of Double Precision: Working Precision as a Hidden Constraint in Gaussian Process Maximum Likelihood](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33647)
*Thomas A. Scott*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GP 超参数常用的双精度边际似然拟合在平滑核、无噪声数据下数值不可解析：对 SE 核需约 70 位有效位才收敛，故双精度解实质是另一个模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GP 超参数惯例上用 IEEE 双精度最大化边际似然，但当核平滑且数据无噪声时协方差矩阵的条件数常超出双精度可分辨的范围，导致拟合结果失真，作者要定量刻画这一数值失效的机制与阈值。

Method: 在贝叶斯优化聚类设计上对 SE 核做多有效位数（40–64 位）重拟合，并以高精度解为参考；以 Cholesky 失败作为隐式条件数约束进行分析，对理想化问题给出局部展开；并在 43 组核、设计与问题设定（含负对照）上检验所需位数与对数条件数的关系。

Result: 所需位数约等于参考最优处的对数条件数加一个依赖容差的偏移；双精度拟合与参考解属不同模型，且需约 70 位才收敛。仅凭工作精度预测的约束解在 40–64 位中的五档与最佳 Powell 拟合相差 20% 以内。双精度超参数会高估聚类附近的后验标准差，并在似然趋于平坦的二次目标上使闭环主动学习停滞；将整个似然提升至 80 位可在所有固定设计上恢复参考精度，而固定 jitter 仅以牺牲失败率换取后验偏差。

Conclusion: 双精度边际似然最大化不足以可靠拟合平滑核 GP 超参数；应依据对数条件数选择工作精度（如 80 位），而非依赖固定 jitter。

Abstract: Gaussian process (GP) hyperparameters are routinely fitted by maximizing the marginal likelihood in IEEE double precision, although for smooth kernels and noiseless data the covariance matrix is often more ill conditioned than double precision can resolve. For a squared-exponential kernel on a design with a Bayesian-optimization cluster, refits over a range of significand widths, judged against a high-precision reference, show that the double-precision fit is a different model and that fits converge only once the width exceeds the base-2 logarithm of the covariance condition number, about 70 bits. Cholesky failure acts as an implicit conditioning constraint, caused mostly by rounding of the kernel entries; the constrained solution predicted from the working precision alone is within 20% of the best Powell fits at five of the six precisions from 40 to 64 bits, with a local expansion proved for the idealized problem. Across 43 kernels, designs and problem settings, including negative controls that double precision already resolves, the bits needed follow the log condition number at the reference optimum plus a tolerance-dependent offset, and at the loosest tolerance double precision suffices for the Matern 1.5 kernel but not for the squared-exponential kernel on clustered designs. Double-precision hyperparameters overstate the posterior standard deviation near the design cluster and, on a quadratic objective whose likelihood heads to the flat limit, stall closed-loop active learning. Promoting the whole likelihood to 80 significand bits recovers the reference accuracy on every fixed design tested, whereas a fixed jitter trades failures for a posterior bias.

</details>


### [45] [PRIOR: Pre-Call Reasoning in SFT and Its Influence on Tool-Use Retention under RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33648)
*Kaiwen Hu,Hyunin Lee,Joseph Suh,Serina Chang,Shuai Shao,Yong Zhang,Namyong Park,Xiaoqiang Luo,Luke Simon,Somayeh Sojoudi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究SFT阶段习得的工具使用在后续RL中为何易丢失，发现"工具调用前推理长度"是关键因素，并提出按该指标筛选SFT数据的策略以提升RL后的工具使用保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SFT学到的工具使用行为在RL中并不总能保留，作者希望厘清SFT数据属性如何影响这种保留，尤其是数据中工具调用深度与调用前推理量的差异。

Method: 构造Low/Medium/High三个token预算近似匹配但工具调用深度不同的SFT数据集，在相同RL配方下训练；比较工具发起率与调用前推理量；给出理想化的选择性保留理论分析；并按平均调用前推理长度对High轨迹排序、在匹配累计token曝光的前提下保留高排名样本进行筛选。

Result: 三种初始化RL后训练准确率相近，但工具发起动态显著分化：Low上升、Medium下降、High崩塌；High轨迹调用前推理明显更少。基于推理长度的筛选策略在多个基准上提升RL后准确率与工具条件下最终答案成功率，且未一致推高工具发起率。

Conclusion: 调用前推理是理解并改善下游RL中工具使用保持的有价值的SFT监督维度；only-outcome优化可保留整体与工具条件准确率优势，而工具发起仅在工具轨迹优于无工具替代方案时增长。

Abstract: Tool use learned during supervised fine-tuning (SFT) is not always retained during reinforcement learning (RL). We study how properties of SFT data may affect this retention by constructing Low, Medium, and High SFT datasets with approximately matched token budgets but different tool-call depths. Under the same RL recipe, the three initializations reach similar RL training accuracy yet exhibit markedly different tool-use dynamics: tool initiation increases in Low, declines in Medium, and collapses in High. We observe that these datasets also differ substantially in the amount of reasoning preceding each tool call. In particular, High trajectories contain considerably less pre-call reasoning, motivating the hypothesis that pre-call reasoning may affect how well tool use is calibrated and, consequently, whether the corresponding behaviors are retained during RL. An idealized theoretical analysis provides a selective-retention view of this process: advantages in overall and tool-conditioned accuracy can be preserved under outcome-only optimization, while tool initiation increases only when tool-using trajectories are more successful than the tool-free alternative. Guided by this analysis, we rank the original High trajectories by average pre-call reasoning length and retain the highest-ranked examples while approximately matching cumulative SFT token exposure. This simple filtering strategy improves post-RL accuracy and tool-conditioned final-answer success without consistently increasing tool initiation across multiple benchmarks. These results suggest that pre-call reasoning is a useful dimension of SFT supervision for understanding and improving tool-use retention under downstream RL.

</details>


### [46] [Self-supervised Clipped-objective Reinforcement Leaning Framework for Risk-aware Portfolio Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33652)
*Zongshen Mu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出自监督裁剪目标组合管理框架SCP，通过两阶段架构融合微观市场结构与宏观指标并稳定策略学习，在A股三大指数数据集上取得优于SOTA的风险-收益权衡表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于深度强化学习的组合管理模型存在两点不足：一是忽略tick级日内市场动态（如订单流不平衡、流动性波动）所反映的资金快速流动信息；二是强化学习固有的高方差导致训练过程不稳定。

Method: SCP采用两阶段架构：市场特征整合阶段将微观市场结构模式与宏观金融指标相结合，提升对日内资产趋势动态的敏感性；策略优化阶段基于中间资产状态表示，利用自监督裁剪目标策略学习器推导最优组合配置，兼顾有效性与计算效率。

Result: 在中国A股市场三个主要指数数据集上的大量实验表明，SCP在风险-收益权衡方面优于最先进基线；探索性与消融实验进一步验证了关键组件的贡献。

Conclusion: SCP通过微观-宏观特征融合与自监督裁剪目标策略优化，有效缓解非平稳市场中的动态刻画不足与学习不稳定问题，为强化学习组合管理提供了兼顾性能与效率的可行方案。

Abstract: Portfolio management involves balancing diverse financial assets, a critical yet challenging task in quantitative trading. Although deep reinforcement learning has emerged as an active research domain for solving complex sequential decision-making problems over the past decade, deploying reinforcement learning frameworks in non-stationary financial markets raises formidable difficulties. Most existing reinforcement learning-based portfolio management models fail to account for tick-level intraday market dynamics (e.g., order flow imbalances and liquidity fluctuations), which reflect rapid capital movements. Simultaneously, these models suffer from unstable learning processes due to reinforcement learning's inherent high variance. This paper presents a Self-supervised Clipped-objective Portfolio management framework (SCP), which addresses these limitations through a two-phase architecture. Market feature integration phase (application contribution) integrates micro-level market microstructure patterns with macro-level financial indicators to enhance sensitivity to intraday asset trend dynamics. Policy optimization Phase (AI contribution) employs intermediate asset state representations to derive optimal portfolio allocations via a self-supervised clipped-objective policy-learner, ensuring both effectiveness and computational efficiency. Extensive evaluations on three major index datasets from China's A-share market demonstrate that SCP's superior performance in balancing risk-return trade-offs compared to state-of-the-art baselines. Exploratory and ablation studies further validate the contributions of SCP's key components.

</details>


### [47] [Flipping the Coins: Training Physical Simulators through Hard Switches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33716)
*Tianrun Gao,Ruiqi Feng,Haomin Wen,Tailin Wu,Liangqiong Qu,Xiaomeng Huang,Ruiqi Shu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出EventFlip，通过翻转近阈值硬开关并重放求解器构造跨开关的有限差分，得到结合连续路径梯度与跳变项的无偏梯度估计，在电力级联、爆轰波、皮层扩散性去极化三类任务上以相同前向预算取得最低误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合仿真器在物理求解器中嵌入神经网络并用参考轨迹训练，但求解器含硬开关（如线路跳闸、网格点火、组织去极化），损失在开关翻转处出现离散跳变，自动微分只能捕获光滑部分。现有范式存在固有取舍：基于梯度的松弛会平滑开关并在跳变处引入偏差，而无导数方法把求解器视为黑箱、丢弃连续动力学的路径梯度。

Method: EventFlip从存储状态翻转一个近阈值开关并重放原求解器，度量两次运行的损失差，等价于跨开关的有限差分；对每个阈值施加小高斯噪声，将每个开关刻画为随机二值决策，从而推导出将连续路径梯度与由反事实 rollout 构造的跳变项相结合的无偏梯度估计器。EventFlip-Rand 优化噪声平均目标；EventFlip-Hard 直接作用于部署时的确定性硬开关仿真器，并可证明针对完整的不连续损失。

Result: 在相同前向评估预算下，于电力网络级联、爆轰波与皮层扩散性去极化三个任务上与十二个基线对比，EventFlip在每项任务上误差最低，分别将最强基线误差降低约1.5、1.4、1.4倍。

Conclusion: EventFlip以反事实重放实现跨硬开关的有限差分，弥补梯度松弛偏差与无导数黑箱两大范式的缺陷，给出无偏且覆盖不连续损失的梯度估计，并在多类含硬开关的科学仿真任务上稳定优于现有方法。

Abstract: Hybrid simulators embed learnable components such as neural networks in physical solvers and train them on reference trajectories. Many scientific solvers feature hard switches, such as a tripping power line, an igniting grid cell, or depolarizing brain tissue, where the loss exhibits discrete jumps across switch flips, leaving standard automatic differentiation to capture only its smooth part. Existing training paradigms face a fundamentally challenging trade-off, as gradient-based relaxations smooth the switches and introduce bias across jumps, whereas derivative-free methods treat the solver as a black box and discard path gradients of the continuous dynamics. Here we introduce EventFlip, which flips a near-threshold switch from a stored state, replays the original solver, and measures the loss difference between the two runs, in effect a finite difference across the switch. By perturbing every threshold with small Gaussian noise, we cast each switch as a stochastic binary decision and derive an unbiased gradient estimator that combines the continuous path gradient with a jump term constructed from these counterfactual rollouts. EventFlip-Rand optimizes this noise-averaged objective, whereas EventFlip-Hard operates on the deterministic hard-switch simulator used at deployment and provably targets the complete discontinuous loss. Under an identical forward evaluation budget, we benchmark EventFlip against twelve baselines across a power-grid cascade, a detonation wave, and cortical spreading depolarization, achieving the lowest error on every task and reducing the strongest baseline's error by factors of 1.5, 1.4, and 1.4, respectively.

</details>

*Daniel Pfrommer,Chenyang Yuan,Frank Permenter,Ali Jadbabaie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文用可解析的高斯混合模型刻画无分类器引导，推导出所有噪声尺度与引导强度下的闭式引导分布，揭示引导带来均值轴方向平移与朝奇异点的持续偏斜/集中，并在大规模预训练图像扩散模型上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无分类器引导（CFG）对扩散/流模型的经验成功至关重要，能生成低温度、高保真样本，但因其对应的传输方程对任意归一化流难以求解，理论上理解匮乏；作者观察到引导效应源于条件与无条件分布在表达能力上的不匹配（条件分布更复杂、更高保真）。

Method: 提出解析模型：条件分布为均值位于椭球上的高斯混合，无条件分布为逼近该混合的单一高斯；该混合产生的流对缩放封闭，从而可对任意噪声尺度和引导强度下引导分布给出解析闭式解。

Result: 解析解表明CFG诱导两类效应：(a) 沿均值轴方向的平移；(b) 持续朝一个奇异点（对应零噪声出发的条件样本）偏斜并集中。并在预训练大规模图像扩散模型上验证了理论预测。

Conclusion: CFG的效应可归因于条件/无条件分布表征能力的不匹配，所提解析框架为理解引导机制提供了闭式、可验证的理论解释。

Abstract: The use of classifier-free guidance in diffusion and flow models has proven critical to their empirical success, as it allows for the controllable generation of lower-temperature and higher-fidelity samples. Despite its widespread use, classifier-free guidance remains both empirically and theoretically poorly understood, owing to the difficulty of solving the associated transport equations for arbitrary normalizing flows. In this paper, we observe that the effects of classifier-free guidance arise from a mismatch between the representation capabilities of the conditional and unconditional distributions, wherein the conditional distribution exhibits higher complexity and fidelity. We propose an analytical model to capture this observation: the conditional distribution is a Gaussian mixture whose means lie on an ellipsoid, and the unconditional distribution is a single Gaussian approximating the mixture. The flows arising from this mixture are closed under scaling, enabling us to derive analytic closed-form solutions for the guided distribution at all noise scales and guidance levels. Our analytic solution shows that classifier-free guidance induces (a) a shift along a mean-axis direction and (b) a persistent skew toward, and concentration around, a singular point corresponding to the conditional sample starting from zero noise. We validate our theoretical predictions on pretrained, large-scale image diffusion models.

</details>


### [49] [CovNovBench: A Benchmark for Evaluating Time Series Foundation Models under Covariate Novelty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33732)
*Wenhao Pan,Ravi Kiran Selvam,Michael W. Mahoney,Mayank Jauhari,Mengfei Cao,Dmitry Efimov,Boris N. Oreshkin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出"协变量新颖性"(covariate novelty)这一操作定义,并构建 CovNovBench 基准(88,476 条序列,覆盖类别型/数值型处理协变量与平稳/趋势目标),零样本评测六个协变量感知时序基础模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协变量感知 TSFMs 支持干预式预测,但其训练仅基于已观测模式,当未来处理协变量超出历史取值范围时行为不明,且现有基准未系统覆盖该设定。

Method: 给出协变量新颖性的操作化定义;基于四个数据集(模拟器与真实世界)生成或筛选 88,476 条评估序列构建 CovNovBench;对六个协变量感知 TSFM 进行零样本评测,采用点误差与方向准确率指标。

Result: 无模型持续可靠:新颖性下点误差几乎总是上升,方向性能变化不一致,最优模型随数据集与指标而变。

Conclusion: CovNovBench 为开发在协变量新颖性下更可靠预测的 TSFM 提供系统测试平台。

Abstract: Covariate-aware time-series foundation models (TSFMs) condition their forecasts on additional input variables, called covariates, that are supplied over the future horizon. This capability makes them appealing for interventional forecasting, in which a target is predicted under a planned action such as a new product pricing policy or a new patient treatment schedule. We refer to covariates that encode such actions as treatment covariates. Current covariate-aware TSFMs, however, are trained on observed patterns, and their behavior is unclear when future treatment covariates fall outside the range seen in the history. Examples include a numerical treatment that leaves its historical range and a categorical treatment that takes a previously unseen policy. We call this practically common setting covariate novelty, and note that it is not systematically represented in existing benchmarks. We introduce an operational definition of covariate novelty and use it to construct CovNovBench, a benchmark of 88,476 evaluation series generated or filtered from four simulator-based and real-world datasets. The benchmark spans categorical and numerical treatment covariates as well as stable and trending targets. We evaluate six covariate-aware TSFMs zero-shot using point and directional accuracy metrics. No model is consistently reliable in our benchmark: point error almost always increases under covariate novelty, directional performance changes non-uniformly, and the best observed model varies across datasets and metrics. CovNovBench provides a systematic testbed for developing TSFMs that forecast more reliably under covariate novelty.

</details>


### [50] [Don't Waste the Population: Post-Anneal Refinement for Combinatorial Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33766)
*Yuma Ichikawa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出无需训练、确定性的迭代路径重连精炼方法 I-PRP，利用并行退火群体的多样性发现更优解，理论上保证不劣于单候选精炼且有限步收敛，在 MaxCut、MaxClique、图着色上取得显著改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 并行退火会产生大量多样化的候选解，但传统后处理通常只精炼其中最好的一个，浪费了群体中其余解的潜力。

Method: I-PRP：1）从群体中筛选有潜力的候选；2）在局部优化解之间沿代价引导的路径进行探索；3）精炼中间状态以发现更优局部极小；4）将新发现的改进迭代地并入种群以促进进一步探索。方法不修改底层退火过程，无需训练且确定性。

Result: 理论上证明 I-PRP 相对同一群体上的传统单候选精炼永不降低解质量，并在有限次迭代内收敛；在 MaxCut、MaxClique、图着色等组合优化基准上多个实例显著改进；配对实验确认不退化，等运行时对比显示利用群体多样性优于增加退火时间。

Conclusion: I-PRP 是一种有效且可靠的并行退火精炼策略，说明充分挖掘已有群体多样性可替代额外的退火计算。

Abstract: Parallel annealers generate diverse candidate solutions; yet, conventional post-processing typically refines only the best, leaving much of the population's potential untapped. We introduce Iterative Path-Relinking Polish (I-PRP), a deterministic, training-free method that exploits this diversity to discover better solutions without modifying the underlying annealing process. I-PRP selects promising candidates, explores cost-guided paths between locally optimized solutions, and refines intermediate states to uncover better local minima. Newly discovered improvements are iteratively incorporated into the population, enabling further exploration. We theoretically guarantee that I-PRP never degrades solution quality relative to conventional single-candidate polishing on the same population and converges in finitely many iterations. Experiments across challenging combinatorial optimization benchmarks, including MaxCut, MaxClique, and graph coloring, demonstrate substantial improvements in several instances. Paired evaluations confirm non-degradation, while matched-runtime comparisons show that exploiting existing population diversity can outperform additional annealing. These results establish I-PRP as an effective and reliable refinement strategy for parallel annealing.

</details>


### [51] [On Grokking and the Dynamics of Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33825)
*Malik Amir*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文将Litman-Guo的grokking信号通道理论扩展到种群风险层面，区分"累积耗散小"与"迁移弱"两类评估可见性受限来源，给出任务可见性定量界、迁移降低平方种群风险的充要条件与分类误差界，并证明泛化延迟可任意长且在无特征演化下可趋于无穷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有grokking信号通道理论主要刻画训练动力学中的信号传递，但未将训练耗散与评估迁移同种群风险直接联系，也缺乏对"同一轨迹在不同评估分布下泛化/失败"以及训练损失与种群风险之间超长延迟的定量解释。本文旨在补齐这一理论缺口。

Method: 针对具有时间依赖核的预条件梯度流，区分小累积耗散与弱迁移两种评估可见性受限机制；推导任务可见性的定量界并证明其在信号滤波与评估分布变化下的稳定性；刻画任务方向经谱激活或输运进入有效信号通道；给出迁移信号降低平方种群风险的充要条件，并在margin假设下导出分类误差界；进一步将分类精度与margin穿越时间分布相联系，构造泛化延迟趋于无穷而过渡时长与种群风险变差趋于零的梯度流族。

Result: 得到任务可见性的定量界及其稳定性结论；给出迁移信号降低平方种群风险的充要条件与margin下的分类误差界；证明存在同一训练轨迹在一种评估分布下泛化、在另一种下失败的充分条件；表明谱学习率差异可造成低训练损失与低种群风险之间任意长的延迟，并构造出泛化延迟→∞、而精度过渡时长与种群风险变差→0的梯度流族，且这些现象无需训练期间的特征演化。

Conclusion: 泛化的发生与时机一般不能仅由训练动力学推断，而取决于评估迁移的几何结构及其与目标的关系；因此训练损失/动力学指标不足以作为泛化判据，需显式建模耗散、迁移与评估分布。

Abstract: We extend the signal-channel theory of grokking introduced by Litman and Guo by relating training dissipation and evaluation transfer to population risk. For preconditioned gradient flows with time-dependent kernels, we distinguish two sources of limited evaluation visibility, namely small cumulative dissipation and weak transfer. We obtain quantitative bounds for task visibility and establish their stability under signal filtering and changes of evaluation distribution. We describe how task directions enter the effective signal channel through spectral activation or transport. We establish necessary and sufficient conditions for the transferred signal to decrease squared population risk and derive classification error bounds under margin assumptions. These results yield sufficient conditions for the same training trajectory to generalize under one evaluation distribution while failing under another. We show that differences in spectral learning rates can produce arbitrarily long delays between low training loss and low population risk. By relating classification accuracy to the distribution of margin crossing times, we construct a family of gradient flows in which the generalization delay tends to infinity, while both the duration of the transition from zero to full accuracy and the corresponding variation of population risk tend to zero. These phenomena occur without feature evolution during training. Finally, we prove that the occurrence and timing of generalization cannot generally be inferred from training dynamics alone, but depend on the geometry of evaluation transfer and its relation to the target.

</details>


### [52] [FEAST: Fair and Efficient Adaptive Sampling for Task-Free On-Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33827)
*Yongxi Zhu,Ziyi Wang,Yichen Wu,Ruite Lu,Liao Chang,Haipeng Li,Ying Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文指出在自生成经验的语言智能体训练中，按当前学习信号分配有限 rollout 预算会因信号内生性形成反馈回路，导致弱能力被持续忽视；提出 Quota-Efficiency Law (QEL) 刻画该效应，并给出 FEAST 框架，将"机会分配"与"算力分配"解耦，在 GURU-18K 与 SMC 上于同等预算下提升平均性能并更好保持弱能力的进步。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自生成经验训练的语言智能体需在异构能力间分配有限 rollout 预算。常规做法是优先投入当前学习信号强的能力，但在 on-policy 学习中该信号是内生的：历史分配会改变策略，从而改变未来可用于分配的证据。由此形成反馈回路，使训练不足的能力获得的机会越来越少，难以改善。

Method: 提出 Quota-Efficiency Law（QEL）形式化该效应，说明即使在能力暴露量匹配的条件下，分配历史也会改变学习效率。基于此设计 FEAST——一种 task-free 自适应采样框架：将机会分配与算力分配分离；动态发现能力簇并保留各簇学习历史；持续维持跨簇采样机会；为每个被选簇预留 rollout 预算，并在簇内逐步将算力集中到奖励方差大、信息量高的 prompt 上。

Result: 在 GURU-18K 与 SMC 上，于匹配的 rollout 预算下，FEAST 的平均性能优于有竞争力的自适应采样基线，同时更好地保持较弱能力的学习进展。消融实验进一步支持跨簇机会保护与簇内选择性分配两者互补的作用。

Conclusion: 分配历史本身会影响学习效率，因此仅依据当前学习信号贪心分配存在系统性偏差。将机会分配与算力分配解耦、并对各能力簇保留持续采样机会与独立预算，可在同等预算下兼顾整体性能与弱能力的持续提升；代码将在录用后发布。

Abstract: Language agents trained from self-generated experience must allocate a limited rollout budget across heterogeneous capabilities. A common strategy is to prioritize capabilities with strong current learning signals, but in on-policy learning these signals are endogenous: past allocation changes the policy and therefore the evidence available for future allocation. This can create a feedback loop in which under-trained capabilities receive increasingly fewer opportunities to improve. We formalize this effect through the Quota-Efficiency Law (QEL), which shows that allocation history can change learning efficiency even under matched capability-specific exposure. Motivated by this, we introduce FEAST, a task-free adaptive sampling framework that separates opportunity allocation from compute allocation. FEAST dynamically discovers capability clusters while preserving their learning histories, maintains continued sampling opportunities across clusters, and reserves a rollout budget for each selected cluster. Within each budget, it progressively concentrates computation on prompts with informative reward variation. Across GURU-18K and SMC, FEAST improves average performance over competitive adaptive sampling baselines under matched rollout budgets, while better preserving learning progress on weaker capabilities. Ablation studies further support the complementary roles of cross-cluster opportunity protection and within-cluster selective allocation. Code will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [53] [Mitigating Overgeneralization in RND via Spectral Target Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33850)
*Minseok Jeong,Yechan Lee,Hyewon Choi,Jeongyong Yang,SooJean Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从核理论视角解释RND新颖性信号过度泛化的失败机制，指出其本质是"谱激发不足"，并提出频谱目标设计原则，以带宽可控的RFF目标替代随机网络目标，在D4RL、Atari与Fetch上提升新颖性判别与下游性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RND基于固定随机目标网络与训练预测器构造可扩展新颖性信号，但预测器会在数据流形之外外推随机目标，导致对陌生输入的新颖性分数塌缩（过度泛化），其机理缺乏理论解释。

Method: 在GP目标模型与NTK区间预测器假设下，将RND新颖性分数刻画为目标协方差核与预测器NTK共同决定的交叉核残差方差，从而把过度泛化归因于谱激发不足；据此提出"频谱目标设计"原则，并用带宽可控的随机傅里叶特征（RFF）目标替代隐式随机网络目标，将残差信号重新分配到被预测器衰减较弱的模态上。

Result: 在离线D4RL、在线Atari及Fetch机械臂操作基准上，所提RFF-RND方法相比原RND改善了新颖性判别能力，并提升了下游RL性能；代码已开源。

Conclusion: RND的新颖性几何由目标核与预测器NTK的谱特性联合决定，而非单一因素；通过显式设计目标的频谱结构（如带宽可控RFF）可有效缓解过度泛化，为内在奖励信号的设计提供了原理性指导。

Abstract: Random Network Distillation (RND) is a scalable novelty signal for RL based on a fixed random target network and a trained predictor. However, it can overgeneralize: the predictor extrapolates the random target off the data manifold, causing novelty scores to collapse on unfamiliar inputs. This paper analyzes this failure through a kernel-theoretic lens. Under a GP target model and an NTK-regime predictor, we show that the RND novelty score is a cross-kernel residual variance: it is governed jointly by the target covariance kernel and the predictor NTK, rather than by either one alone. This identifies overgeneralization as spectral under-excitation. Motivated by this view, we formulate spectral target design as a principle for shaping RND novelty geometry. As a concrete instance, we replace the implicit random-network target with a bandwidth-controlled Random Fourier Feature (RFF) target, redistributing residual signal toward modes less attenuated by the predictor. Experiments on offline D4RL, online Atari, and Fetch manipulation benchmarks show improved novelty discrimination and downstream RL performance. The code for the RL implementation is available at https://github.com/tuuktuc86/RFF-RND.

</details>


### [54] [Normalizing Flows are Capable World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33872)
*Zijia Li,Yong-Hyun Park,Shusheng Yang,Jiatao Gu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出归一化流世界模型（NFWM）：用可逆观测编码器配合动作条件概率预测器，通过最大化未来观测的精确条件似然联合训练表征与动力学，并支持潜空间规划、目标条件控制与想象轨迹上的策略改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型多依赖近似似然或重构损失，难以在潜空间上定义严格的观测分布；作者希望借助可逆编码器使潜变量预测分布能精确对应未来观测分布，从而以精确条件似然为目标同时学习表征与动力学。

Method: NFWM 将可逆（归一化流）观测编码器与动作条件概率预测器配对；由于编码可逆，潜状态预测分布可映射为未来观测分布，故可直接在观测空间最大化精确条件似然以联合训练。智能体在潜空间直接规划，并解码采样轨迹以检视可能未来。目标条件控制时引入辅助逆动力学目标来塑造用于预测状态与目标比较的坐标度量。

Result: Teleport 中采样可捕获同一动作的两种可能结局；Flappy Bird 中仅用似然训练的世界模型支持基于奖励的规划，在全部十局评估中均达到 100 根管道；LIBERO 上基于该距离选择成功动作序列的频率高于 LeWM 与 SMWM；Atari Boxing 中在想象轨迹上训练使策略优于行为克隆初始化。

Conclusion: 结果表明归一化流可作为强有力的世界模型骨干，精确似然目标足以支撑规划、目标条件控制与策略提升。

Abstract: A world model lets an agent anticipate the consequences of its actions and use imagined futures to make decisions. We introduce Normalizing-Flow World Model (NFWM), which pairs an invertible observation encoder with an action-conditioned probabilistic predictor. Because the encoder is invertible, the predicted distribution over latent states defines a distribution over future observations. Maximizing the exact conditional likelihood of those observations therefore trains the representation and dynamics jointly. An agent can plan directly in the learned latent space and decode sampled trajectories to inspect possible futures. On Teleport, these samples capture the two possible outcomes of the same action. In Flappy Bird, a world model trained by likelihood alone supports reward-based planning that reaches 100 pipes in all ten evaluated games. For goal-conditioned control, an auxiliary inverse-dynamics objective shapes the coordinates used to compare predicted states with a goal. On LIBERO, NFWM uses this distance to select successful action sequences more often than LeWM and SMWM. In Atari Boxing, training on imagined trajectories improves a policy beyond its behavior-cloned initialization. Together, these results establish normalizing flows as capable world models.

</details>


### [55] [Random Voronoi Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33875)
*Ray Verma,Pau Mateo-Bernardó,Daniel Mas Montserrat,David Bonet,Marçal Comajoan Cara,Alexander G. Ioannidis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出随机Voronoi网络(RVN)，一类三层MLP，用采样Voronoi比较显式表达近邻几何；完备时step版本精确等价欧氏k-NN，采样版本为可控近似的可微特征编码，显著提升下游表格模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最近邻方法局部、简单、可解释，但其非参数形式导致存储开销大、推理昂贵，且难以嵌入可微神经网络架构。作者希望在保持近邻几何语义的同时获得参数化、可微、可控复杂度的表示。

Method: 构建Random Voronoi Networks：第一层由选定训练样本间的仿射成对分离器计算Random Voronoi Features (RVF)；第二层将比较聚合为Voronoi单元或top-k邻域得分；读出层完成分类/回归。理论上证明包含全部样本与成对比较时，step RVN精确还原欧氏k-NN，ReLU RVN给出1-NN的violation-score实现并可松弛为可微soft加权。通过子采样得到近似RVN，可控单元数与特征数；并研究多种无监督采样策略，在随机覆盖、方向多样性、坐标稀疏性与经验特征方差之间权衡。

Result: RVF是一种高效数据编码策略，可为从线性模型到现代表格深度模型的多种下游方法带来性能提升；同时，由于基于k-NN构造并与成对局部方向相关，RVF提供可解释的特征映射。

Conclusion: RVN在最近邻规则、随机特征与现代表格模型之间建立桥梁：既保留k-NN的局部几何与可解释性，又具备神经网络的可微性与可控近似能力。

Abstract: Nearest-neighbor methods are local, simple, and interpretable, but their non-parametric form makes them memory-intensive, expensive at inference, and difficult to integrate into differentiable neural architectures. We introduce Random Voronoi Networks (RVNs), a family of structured three-layer MLPs that express nearest-neighbor geometry through sampled Voronoi comparisons. The first layer computes Random Voronoi Features (RVF) from affine pairwise separators between selected training samples, the second layer aggregates these comparisons into Voronoi cells or top-k neighborhood scores, and the readout layer produces classification or regression predictions. We show that, when all samples and pairwise comparisons are included, step RVNs recover exact Euclidean k-NN, while ReLU RVNs provide a violation-score realization for 1-NN that can be relaxed into a differentiable soft weighting. Subsampling yields approximate RVNs with explicit control over the number of cells and features. We study several unsupervised sampling methods that trade off random coverage, directional diversity, coordinate sparsity, and empirical feature variance. Empirically, RVFs produce a highly effective data encoding strategy, providing performance boosts to downstream methods ranging from linear to modern tabular deep learning models. Finally, we showcase that RVF provides an interpretable feature map due to its k-NN based construction and ties to pairwise local directions. These results position our Random Voronoi formulation as a bridge between nearest-neighbor rules, random features, and modern tabular models.

</details>


### [56] [The Gradient Difference Induced by Detaching the Denominator in QK Normalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33887)
*Yuanqi Zhang,Haocheng Wang,Congping Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文从梯度层面解析了QK归一化中"分离分母"（detached RMS）与标准反向传播的差异，并给出实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QK normalization在计算注意力分数前对query和key做RMS归一化。实践中常把分母当作常数（detach）以节省计算，前向不变但会改变参数梯度。作者希望精确刻画这一梯度差异的来源与后果。

Method: 推导detached梯度与标准梯度的闭式差：对单token，差值平行于该token自身query，只改变query范数，归一化后无一阶影响；由于所有token共享投影矩阵W_Q，W_Q上的梯度差等于W_Q右乘输入的加权二阶矩，并精确分解为径向、切向、上游三个分量。随后在小规模Transformer语言模型上做配对实验。

Result: 在AdamW下，径向分量使W_Q收敛到更大的平衡范数并减小每步角更新。配对实验显示：detach query分母持续抬高测试损失，且该差距跨硬件、评测数据集和学习率稳健；三个分量训练全程均不可忽略，切向分量最大；范数增长与更小角更新仅出现在被detach的投影矩阵上，另一投影矩阵基本不变。引入可学习scale后未观察到可分辨的损失差距。

Conclusion: detach QK归一化分母虽不改变前向，却通过W_Q的径向/切向/上游梯度偏差影响训练，导致损失变差；引入可学习scale可消除该差距，说明问题与归一化的尺度自由度有关。

Abstract: QK normalization divides each query and key by its own RMS norm before the attention scores are computed. Treating this denominator as a constant during backpropagation leaves the forward pass unchanged but alters the parameter gradients. We derive a closed form for the difference between the gradient under this detached rule and the gradient under the standard rule. For a single token, this difference is parallel to the token's own query; it changes only the query norm and has no first-order effect after normalization. The queries, however, are computed by a projection matrix $W_Q$ shared across all tokens. The gradient difference on $W_Q$ therefore equals $W_Q$ right-multiplied by a weighted second moment of the inputs, and it decomposes exactly into three components: a radial component that changes only the norm of $W_Q$, a tangential component that changes the query directions of other tokens, and an upstream component that changes the gradient passed to earlier layers. Under AdamW, the radial component drives $W_Q$ to a larger equilibrium norm and reduces its angular update per step. Paired experiments on a small Transformer language model are consistent with this analysis. Detaching the query denominator consistently raises the test loss, and the gap persists across hardware, evaluation datasets and learning rates. All three components remain non-negligible throughout training, with the tangential component the largest. Norm growth and the smaller angular update appear only on the detached projection matrix, while the other projection matrix is essentially unchanged. With a learnable scale, however, we observe no resolvable loss gap.

</details>


### [57] [On the Learnability of Model Reprogramming: A Reachability and Relabeling Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33889)
*Zesheneg Ye,Pin-Yu Chen,Feng Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从wrapper结构出发，对模型重编程（model reprogramming）所诱导的假设类 F_MR 进行有限样本可学习性分析，给出估计误差的agnostic-PAC界（样本复杂度在可达性项与重标注项上可加）以及精确分解近似误差（源标签信息损失+输出映射限制），并将近似分析扩展到暴露logit的源模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型重编程在经验上取得大量进展，但其wrapper假设类 F_MR 的有限样本可学习性（需要多少目标样本、与目标Bayes风险还差多少）仍缺乏理论刻画。作者希望填补这一空白，并给出能定位误差来源的结构性视角。

Method: 针对仅暴露硬源标签的源模型，利用wrapper结构：输入变换在目标样本上选出可达的源标签，输出映射再对其重标注。据此将 F_MR 的逐样本复杂度分解为"可达性项"（可达源标签模式计数）与"条件重标注项"（重标注计数）；基于此导出估计误差的agnostic-PAC界，其样本复杂度在两项上可加，并在加性输入变换下得到闭式表达，同时用显式二分类构造给出匹配的下界。对近似误差，用同一结构精确分解为源标签信息损失与输出映射限制，并给出源条件上界；进一步将分析推广到暴露logit的源模型，使上界可经验估计。

Result: (1) 得到 F_MR 的agnostic-PAC估计误差界，样本复杂度可加地依赖于可达性项与条件重标注项；在加性输入变换下界为闭式，且由显式二分类构造的下界所匹配（紧致）。(2) 近似误差被精确分解为源标签信息损失与输出映射限制两部分，并给出源条件上界；该框架可扩展至logit暴露的源模型，上界可经验估计。

Conclusion: wrapper侧分析将估计误差与近似误差分别定位到具体组件（输入变换的可达性、输出映射的重标注、源标签信息损失），为模型重编程实践提供了结构化的理论视角与可计算的误差刻画工具。

Abstract: Model reprogramming adapts a frozen source model to a target task by wrapping it in a learnable input transformation from target input to the source-model input space, and an output mapping from source-model outputs to target predictions. Despite extensive empirical progress, the finite-sample learnability of the induced wrapper hypothesis class $\mathcal{F}_{\rm MR}$ remains underexplored. We address this for source models that expose hard source labels and analyze it through the wrapper structure: on any target samples, the input transformation selects reachable source labels that the output mapping relabels. Through this lens, we answer two questions: (1) how many target samples are needed to approach the best predictor in $\mathcal{F}_{\rm MR}$, and (2) what gap remains between the optimum over $\mathcal{F}_{\rm MR}$ and the target Bayes risk. For (1), the wrapper structure factorizes the sample-wise complexity of $\mathcal{F}_{\rm MR}$ into a reachability term counting reachable source-label patterns, and a conditional relabeling term counting their relabelings. This yields an agnostic-PAC bound on the estimation error, with sample complexity scaling additively in the two terms; this bound becomes closed-form on additive input transformations and is matched by a lower bound on an explicit binary classification construction. For (2), the same structure decomposes the approximation error exactly into the source-label information loss and output-mapping restriction with a source-conditional upper bound on this gap. We extend the approximation analysis to logit-exposing source models, where the upper bound is empirically estimable. The wrapper-side analysis localizes both errors to specific components, giving a structural view of reprogramming practice.

</details>


### [58] [Looped Models are Fast Multi-Token Predictors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33896)
*Ayhan Suleymanzade,Sean McLeish,Sehoon Kim,Jinwoo Kim,Antoine Bosselut*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Floodgates：一种用于循环语言模型（Looped LM）的简单自推测解码方法，将一段 mask token 作为后缀并行草拟多个未来 token，实测最高 7.5× 无损加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环语言模型可通过调整循环层数来调节计算量，而无需按比例扩增唯一参数。已有工作利用浅层循环作快速 drafter、深层循环作 verifier 做自推测解码，但草拟阶段仍使用全部参数。作者发现浅层循环不仅能预测下一个 token，还能预测多个未来 token，但此前未有人利用这一性质。

Method: Floodgates 在输入末尾追加一个 mask token 块，通过低秩适配（LoRA）训练模型并行草拟该块中的多个 token；草拟由浅层循环完成，验证由深层循环完成，形成自推测解码；浅层 drafter 以验证阶段计算出的深层前缀缓存为条件，从而间接利用全深度计算。

Result: 在 7 个模型、5 个基准、多种 batch size 以及贪心与采样解码设定下，Floodgates 在多数情形下是速度最快的无损解码方法，最高实现 7.5× 加速。

Conclusion: 该加速来源于循环模型的全深度训练（all-depth training），使浅层 drafter 能借助深层前缀缓存间接利用全深度计算能力；Floodgates 为循环语言模型提供了一种简单有效的无损推理加速方案。

Abstract: Looped language models can adapt computation for predicting a token without a proportional rescaling in unique model parameters by adjusting the number of loops. This property has been used for self-speculative decoding, taking shallow loops as fast drafters and deep loops as verifiers, which is effective since drafting still uses full parameters. In this paper, we show that shallow loops can be also predictive of several future tokens and leverage this to drastically accelerate the inference of looped language models. To that end, we present Floodgates, a simple self-speculative decoding method that appends a block of mask tokens as a suffix and learns via low-rank adaptation to draft these tokens in parallel. Across seven models, five benchmarks, a range of batch sizes, and both greedy and sampled decoding, Floodgates is the fastest lossless decoding method in most cases, attaining up to 7.5x acceleration. Through analysis, we show that this speedup is enabled by all-depth training of looped models, with the shallow drafter's ability to leverage full-depth computation by conditioning on deep prefix cache evaluated during verification.

</details>


### [59] [Correlation-Aware Evidential Fusion for Trusted Multi-View Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33900)
*Tianyang Wang,Mengchi Zuo,Cai Xu,Yilin Zhang,Ziyu Guan,Wei Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CAFE：一种相关性感知的证据融合框架，用于可信多视图分类，通过互补性信任折扣与相关性约束抑制相似证据的重复计数，并自适应结合私有与协同证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可信多视图分类方法将各视图视为独立信息源，忽略视图间相关性。高相关视图会产生相似证据，独立性假设导致一致信息被重复计数、权重过大，互补证据被削弱，从而损害分类性能并造成不确定性估计失准。

Method: 提出CAFE框架：1）对视图私有专家输出的证据施加基于互补性的信任折扣；2）引入相关性感知约束，降低相似证据的重复贡献；3）从全局多视图上下文学习样本特定权重，自适应融合经相关性调整的私有证据与跨视图协同证据。

Result: 在六个真实数据集上的实验表明，CAFE在一般设置下持续提升分类与校准性能，当视图间存在强相关性时增益尤为显著。

Conclusion: 显式建模视图相关性对可信多视图分类至关重要；CAFE通过折扣与约束机制缓解证据重复计数，改善性能与不确定性校准，尤其适用于高相关视图场景。

Abstract: Trusted multi-view classification aims to make accurate and reliable predictions by integrating information from multiple views. Existing methods typically estimate class evidence and uncertainty for each view and combine the resulting opinions through aggregation rules. However, they generally treat different views as independent information sources, overlooking the correlations among them. Highly correlated views tend to produce similar evidence, and treating them as independent sources may repeatedly count consistent information and give it disproportionate influence, while weakening complementary evidence that provides additional information. Consequently, such correlation-unaware fusion may not only limit classification performance but also produce miscalibrated uncertainty estimates. To address this issue, we propose Correlation-Aware Fusion of Evidence (CAFE), a framework for trusted multi-view classification. CAFE applies complementarity-based trust discounting to the evidence produced by view-private experts and introduces a correlation-aware constraint to reduce the repeated contribution of similar evidence. It then learns sample-specific weights from the global multi-view context to adaptively combine the correlation-adjusted private evidence with cross-view collaborative evidence. Extensive experiments on six real-world datasets demonstrate that CAFE consistently improves classification and calibration performance in general settings, with particularly significant gains when strong correlations exist among views.

</details>


### [60] [InstructTTS-OPD: On-Policy Distillation for Multi-Objective Instruction-Following TTS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33937)
*Haitao Li,Tian Tan,Yuguang Yang,Guanrou Yang,Wenhao Guan,Chenglin Li,Shan Yang,Xie Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对Instruct-TTS中"指令遵循、发音正确、自然度"三目标在单一标量奖励+GRPO下相互竞争、训练不稳定的问题，作者改用on-policy distillation（OPD）融合多个单目标教师，并提出冲突感知速度融合以缓解质量与指令遵循的冲突，最终在三项指标上同时优于标量奖励GRPO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Instruct-TTS需同时满足指令遵循、转写正确性和自然度三方面要求。现有做法将三者合并为单一标量奖励并用GRPO优化，但目标间相互竞争，共享更新会漂向最易提升的目标，导致"跷跷板"式此消彼长和训练不稳定。

Method: 1）为每个维度构建可靠奖励：用蒸馏的Qwen-Omni作为昂贵的Gemini评判器的代理，使用去方言数据计算WER奖励，以DNSMOS替代存在语言偏置的UTMOS；2）用GRPO分别训练三个单目标教师，再用OPD在学生自身rollout上匹配各教师的速度场进行融合；3）按数据域将rollout路由至指令遵循教师或WER教师，所有rollout额外跟随MOS教师；4）提出冲突感知速度融合：融合前将质量目标投影到与主目标相反方向的正交补空间，削弱指令遵循/质量权衡。

Result: 指令遵循与可懂度可同时提升而无需权衡；但质量目标与指令遵循存在冲突，直接加性融合会重新引入跷跷板效应。冲突感知速度融合显著降低指令遵循/质量的权衡，融合模型在三项指标上同时优于标量奖励GRPO。

Conclusion: 以多单目标教师+OPD替代单标量奖励GRPO，并配合冲突感知速度融合，可在Instruct-TTS的多目标后训练中避免目标竞争，实现三方面协同提升。

Abstract: Instruction-following text-to-speech (Instruct-TTS) renders a target transcript in the manner described by a free-form natural-language instruction, and a good rendition must succeed on three fronts at once: following the instruction, pronouncing the transcript correctly, and sounding natural. Reinforcement learning has become the standard way to post-train such generative models toward human preferences, and TTS systems increasingly adopt it. When the three objectives are optimized together under a single scalar reward, typically with group relative policy optimization (GRPO), training tends to be unstable: the objectives compete, and the shared update drifts toward whichever one is easiest to improve, producing a seesaw among them. We instead adapt on-policy distillation (OPD), which fuses several single-objective teachers by matching their velocity fields on the student's own rollouts. Realizing this for Instruct-TTS first calls for a sound reward on each axis, which we analyze carefully: a distilled Qwen-Omni proxy for the costly Gemini judge, dialect-free data for the WER reward, and DNSMOS in place of the language-biased UTMOS. We then train three single-objective teachers with GRPO and fuse them with OPD, routing each rollout to the instruction-following or WER teacher by its data domain and letting every rollout additionally follow the MOS teacher for quality. This lets instruction following and intelligibility improve together without a trade-off, but the quality target conflicts with instruction following, so following it additively reintroduces the seesaw. We therefore propose conflict-aware velocity fusion, which projects the quality target off the direction that opposes the primary objective before fusing, and show that it markedly reduces the instruction-following/quality trade-off. The fused model improves over scalar-reward GRPO on all three objectives at once.

</details>


### [61] [CondUQ: Calibrated Conditional Uncertainty Quantification Using Distribution Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33939)
*Shizhuang Wang,Tianhao Wang,Xingqun Zhan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出condUQ：将误差模型条件化于可观测因素（映射为潜在质量指标QI），实现无需质量标签、可黑盒应用的精确不确定量化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可靠性与安全关键应用需对模型/学习系统的输出做精确不确定量化；误差随输入质量与工况变化，单一高斯或无条件GM M/分位数难以刻画误差对因素的依赖。

Method: 在现有系统之上附加、不改动原系统（支持黑盒）：1）将可观测因素映射为潜在质量指标QI，使同QI误差近似服从共同高斯条件分布，QI预测器以分布匹配目标端到端训练，无需质量标签；2）对每个QI在大规模留出集上拟合并校准高斯误差模型。

Result: 在GPS多路径误差、Transformer视觉相对位姿回归的相对姿态误差、世界模型预测误差三个案例中，建模误差分布与经验分布的差异较单一全局模型显著降低；学到的QI揭示因素对误差特性的影响且与物理原理一致。

Conclusion: 条件化误差建模可有效提升不确定量化精度与可靠性，具备黑盒兼容性与可解释性。

Abstract: Many applications, especially reliability- and safety-critical ones, require accurate uncertainty quantification for the outputs of model- or learning-based measurement, estimation, and prediction systems. The errors of such systems often vary with factors such as input quality and operating conditions, and thus cannot be accurately described by a single Gaussian model. Although Gaussian mixture models and calibrated quantiles can approximate such error distributions, their unconditional forms do not capture how the error behavior depends on these factors. Hence, we propose condUQ, an approach that conditions error models on observable factors for enabling accurate and reliable uncertainty quantification. condUQ is built on top of an existing system without modifying it and applies even when the system is a black box. First, condUQ maps the observable factors to a latent quality index (QI) such that errors sharing the same QI follow a common, approximately Gaussian conditional distribution. The QI predictor is trained end-to-end with a distribution-matching objective, without requiring quality labels. Second, for each QI, a Gaussian error model is then fitted and calibrated on a large held-out dataset. condUQ is demonstrated on three examples: (a) GPS multipath errors conditioned on satellite elevation and signal strength, (b) relative attitude errors of a transformer-based visual relative pose regression model conditioned on its internal features of the image pairs, and (c) prediction errors of a world model conditioned on the input observations and actions. In all three examples, condUQ substantially reduces the discrepancy between the modeled and empirical error distributions compared with a single global model. Moreover, the learned QIs reveal how the underlying factors affect the error characteristics in a manner consistent with physical principles.

</details>


### [62] [BReD: Block Replay Dithering for Stable Low-Bit EMA Optimizer States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33951)
*Heshen Zhan,Youhan Huang,Yunke Peng,Yao Wang,Linghui Kong,Ziwei Zhu,Qingyu Han,Yaoyuan Wang,Congliang Chen,Ruoyu Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 BReD（分块重放抖动），将经典减法抖动引入优化器状态量化，降低舍入偏差并控制方差，使 4-bit 优化器状态训练性能逼近全精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低比特存储优化器状态可显著降低大模型训练显存，但实际训练中状态每步都要解量化—更新—重量化—写回。对 EMA 更新的状态（如动量），量化误差被递归反馈进后续更新，导致有偏误差累积、高方差误差被放大，当 EMA 衰减因子接近 1 时尤为严重，引发训练不稳定。

Method: BReD（Block Replay Dithering）：在量化时施加减法抖动（subtractive dithering），以无偏化舍入操作，从而消除舍入偏差并抑制方差放大；采用确定性种子重放 + 分块共享抖动值，避免额外存储随机张量和逐元素生成抖动值。

Result: 默认预训练设置（5 种优化器 @120M；AdamW 与 Muon @1.1B 与 3.4B）以及监督微调（AdamW 与 Muon @7B）：4-bit BReD 与全精度优化器状态训练几乎一致，预训练 PPL 偏移 ≤0.5，微调 ≤0.2，下游平均性能变化在 0.7 分以内；3-bit BReD 在评测设置下仍保持稳定收敛，可作为更激进的替代方案。

Conclusion: 迭代量化下 EMA 状态误差累积是低比特优化器状态的核心失效模式，抖动可有效改善；BReD 在 4-bit 基本无损、3-bit 可稳定训练，且实现开销低（代码已开源）。

Abstract: Low-bit storage of optimizer states can substantially reduce the memory footprint of large-scale training. In practice, however, these states are not quantized once: at every training step, they are dequantized, updated, requantized, and written back. This iterative quantization pipeline can destabilize training. We identify a failure mode behind this instability: for states updated via exponential moving averages (EMAs), quantization errors are recursively fed into subsequent updates. As a result, biased errors accumulate over time while high-variance errors are amplified, particularly when the EMA decay factor is close to 1. To address this error accumulation, we propose BReD (Block Replay Dithering), a method that reduces rounding bias and controls rounding variance during quantization. BReD adapts classical subtractive dithering to optimizer-state quantization by combining deterministic seed replay with block-wise shared dither values, avoiding auxiliary random-tensor storage and per-element dither values generation. Across the default pretraining settings (five optimizers at 120M; AdamW and Muon at 1.1B and 3.4B model) and supervised fine-tuning (AdamW and Muon at 7B model), 4-bit BReD closely matches training with full-precision optimizer states, with PPL shifts $\leq0.5$ in pretraining and $\leq0.2$ in fine-tuning and average downstream performance changes within 0.7 points. Moreover, 3-bit BReD preserves stable convergence in the evaluated settings, offering a more aggressive alternative. Our implementation of BReD is available at: https://github.com/ZhanHeshen/BReD

</details>


### [63] [DuoMI: Dual Mutual Information Regularization for Expert Reuse in Looped Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33952)
*Xinlai Kang,Zhenhe Wu,Haoyu Wang,Weihua He,Yaping Jin,Jie Hu,Hanting Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DuoMI：用双层互信息正则约束循环MoE中跨轮专家路由，无额外参数且推理策略不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环MoE中相邻两轮共享专家池，路由器可能偏向不同专家子集，削弱专家参数跨轮复用；且即便总体使用量一致，也希望单token既有保留专家又有切换专家。

Method: 提出Dual Mutual Information Regularization：(1) 轮级边际互信息限制聚合专家使用对轮次的依赖；(2) token级条件互信息惩罚平均跨轮路由变化，使其落在目标区间内。训练时基于全专家软路由分布计算可微惩罚，不增加模型参数。

Result: 在相邻层共享专家的循环OLMoE骨干上，15个下游任务平均分从50.08%提升至51.82%，较循环基线提升1.74个百分点。

Conclusion: DuoMI有效调控循环MoE的跨轮专家路由，提升下游性能，且保持推理时的稀疏Top-k路由与参数规模不变。

Abstract: We introduce Dual Mutual Information Regularization (DuoMI), a method for regulating expert routing across rounds in looped mixture-of-experts (MoE) Transformers. When two rounds of computation share an expert pool, their routers may favor different expert subsets, limiting the reuse of individual expert parameters across rounds. Even when aggregate expert usage is identical across rounds, we would still prefer each token to retain some experts while switching to others. DuoMI addresses both aspects of routing: it uses round-level marginal mutual information to limit the dependence of aggregate expert usage on the round, and token-level conditional mutual information to penalize average cross-round routing changes that fall outside a target interval. During training, DuoMI computes differentiable penalties from soft routing distributions over all experts. It adds no model parameters and leaves sparse Top-$k$ routing strategy unchanged at inference. On a looped OLMoE backbone in which paired adjacent layers share experts, DuoMI improves the average score across $15$ downstream tasks from $50.08\%$ to $51.82\%$, a gain of $1.74$ percentage points over the looped baseline.

</details>


### [64] [DLaR: Dynamic Stochasticity Control for Latent Reasoning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33962)
*Xiaocheng Luo,Peng Xu,Zaifu Zhan,Xiangyu Duan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DLaR框架，在GRPO下于token级（ASM）与训练级（TAST）两个粒度动态控制潜在推理的随机性，兼顾探索与连贯性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜在推理在连续嵌入空间中运行，但现有方法为确定性推理，缺乏对多样推理路径的探索；近期引入随机性的方法存在两个问题：所有步骤均匀施加随机性并非最优，且无约束扰动会破坏推理连贯性。

Method: DLaR包含两个互补机制，嵌入GRPO强化学习框架：(1) token级自适应随机性调制（ASM），基于置信度-熵融合信号在每步潜变量处动态调整扰动强度，模型不确定时增强探索、确信时保持稳定；(2) 训练级训练感知随机性调节（TAST），按课程式调度逐步衰减基础随机性水平。二者构成token级与训练级的分层探索-利用策略。

Result: 在4个分布内与3个分布外数学推理基准上取得跨模型尺度的最高平均性能：Llama-3.2-1B上Pass@1/Pass@8为27.9%/50.6%，Qwen2.5-1.5B上为46.8%/68.2%。

Conclusion: 分层、动态、训练感知的随机性控制优于均匀、无约束的扰动注入，可作为潜在推理中平衡探索与连贯性的有效方案。

Abstract: Latent reasoning enables LLMs to operate in continuous embedding space, yet existing methods suffer from deterministic inference that precludes exploration of diverse reasoning paths. While recent approaches inject stochasticity to restore exploration, we identify two fundamental limitations: \textit{uniform stochasticity} across all steps is suboptimal, and unconstrained perturbations disrupt reasoning coherence. We propose Dynamic Stochasticity Control for Latent Reasoning (DLaR), a framework with two complementary mechanisms that operate at different temporal granularities within the GRPO reinforcement learning framework. At the token level, Adaptive Stochasticity Modulation (ASM) dynamically adjusts perturbation intensity at each latent step based on a confidence-entropy fusion signal, increasing exploration when the model is uncertain and preserving stability when confident. At the training level, Training-Aware Stochasticity Tuning (TAST) progressively decays the base stochasticity level following a curriculum schedule. Through this dual-level design, DLaR establishes a hierarchical exploration-exploitation strategy that operates at both token and training levels. Extensive experiments on four in-distribution and three out-of-distribution mathematical reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that DLaR achieves the highest average performance across model scales, reaching 27.9%/50.6% Pass@1/Pass@8 on Llama-3.2-1B and 46.8%/68.2% on Qwen2.5-1.5B. Code: \href{https://github.com/xiaocen-luo/DLaR}{https://github.com/xiaocen-luo/DLaR}.

</details>


### [65] [Kolpona: An Agentic Framework with Gradient-Free Neuron Programming for RTL Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33973)
*Musa Tur Farazi,Md Nasir Uddin,Soumik Bhattacharjee,Tishna Sabrina,Chowdhury Mohammad Rakin Haider*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Kolpona：无需梯度训练的智能体框架，通过"神经元编程"重写权重神经元并结合工具型智能体与无标签选择，大幅提升开源权重LLM的RTL设计能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源权重LLM用于RTL设计通常依赖在精选Verilog语料上微调，需要大量经验证的设计与GPU训练资源，成本高昂。作者希望在不做梯度训练的前提下提升此类模型的Verilog生成能力。

Method: 提出Kolpona智能体框架，包含两部分：(1) 神经元编程——一种新的权重空间编程（WSP），以闭式解在权重中写入单个神经元，以消除练习任务中观察到的失败习惯；每个编辑仅在新鲜任务的testbench结果有利时才保留；(2) 推理阶段由工具使用型智能体提出候选设计，并通过无标签选择阶段，仅依据规格、候选及其仿真结果选出每任务唯一设计。

Result: Qwen3.5-4B上，仅16个神经元编辑即将VerilogEval v2 pass@1从31.7%提升至55.4%，完整框架达77.6%。Qwen3.8-27B上，VerilogEval v2从73.1%升至94.9%，RTLLM v2.1从66.0%升至92.0%，单张32GB GPU即可运行，优于对比中所有已发表系统。RTL编辑还可迁移至Python：4B模型单独在HumanEval+从84.8%升至91.5%，MBPP+从67.7%升至77.5%。

Conclusion: 权重空间编程可作为一种免梯度的模型改进途径，与智能体式推理流程互补；少量精准神经元编辑即可显著提升RTL性能，并产生跨语言的正向迁移，且资源开销低。

Abstract: Open-weight large language models (LLMs) for register-transfer level (RTL) design are usually improved by fine-tuning on curated Verilog corpora, which needs large sets of verified designs and GPU training. We present Kolpona, an agentic framework that improves such models without gradient training. Its first component is neuron programming, a new form of weight-space programming (WSP) that writes single neurons in closed form into the weights to remove failure habits found on practice tasks, and keeps each edit only when testbench results on fresh tasks favour it. At inference time, tool-using agents propose designs, and a label-free selection stage delivers one design per task from the specification, the candidates and their simulations alone. On Qwen3.5-4B, edits to 16 neurons raise the VerilogEval v2 pass@1 of the model alone from 31.7% to 55.4%, and the full framework reaches 77.6%. On Qwen3.8-27B, Kolpona lifts pass@1 from 73.1% to 94.9% on VerilogEval v2 and from 66.0% to 92.0% on RTLLM v2.1 on a single 32 GB GPU, above every published system in our comparison. The RTL edits also carry over to Python and raise the pass@1 of the 4B model alone from 84.8% to 91.5% on HumanEval+ and from 67.7% to 77.5% on MBPP+.

</details>


### [66] [Quaternion-Tucker ZigZag Networks: Halving Transformer Backbone Parameters and Enabling Exact 2D Zero-Loss Continual Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33983)
*Shubham Barmase*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出将L层FFN视为3D张量立方体，用四元数GEMM、块对角Tucker核心与ZigZag路由解耦参数与深度，并以零损失逐次扩展宽度/深度，在2x-7.6x压缩下超越标准Transformer。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Transformer每层独立稠密FFN，参数随深度O(L)线性增长且扩容时丢弃已训算力；权重共享的Looped Transformer虽参数O(1)但强制时间同质更新，缺乏深度特异性。

Method: 将L层FFN建模为3D张量立方体；引入QuatTucker-ZigZagGPT：(1) bf16四元数Hamilton GEMM（4x FFN压缩）；(2) 64x64块对角Tucker SwiGLU核心，在共享基上实现深度特化旋转；(3) Braided ZigZag Diamond路由(L0->L2->L1->L3)实现零FLOP自顶向下精炼；(4) 宽度与深度上的精确二维零损失持续扩展；并加入退火门控反弹脚手架(GatedBounce)，自蒸馏后推理开销为0。

Result: 在算法任务(8-bit Parity、56-seed路由消融、Mod-53 Grokking)与语言建模(TinyStories、FineWeb-Edu)上，2x-7.6x压缩即优于标准Transformer；A100上QuatTucker-ZigZagGPT-12(39.0M主干)在5/5受控检查点胜过StandardGPT-12(85.0M)。单一家族经6.00B token持续扩展（宽度6144->8192、两次逐位一致深度扩展L=12->16->20，70.9M主干，max_diff=0.000000、WSD冷却）达3.3582 FineWeb-Edu loss(28.74 PPL)，16,955题零样本准确率44.75%，以50x更少token匹配GPT-3 Small(125M, 300B)。

Conclusion: 张量化FFN参数化解耦与零损失持续扩展可在显著压缩与更低训练预算下保持或超越稠密基线，为容量可增量增长的Transformer提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Standard Transformers allocate independent dense weight matrices to every feed-forward network (FFN) layer, coupling parameters linearly with depth (O(L)) and discarding prior compute when scaling capacity. Conversely, weight-tied Looped Transformers decouple depth from parameters (O(1)) but enforce time-homogeneous updates. We formulate the L-layer FFN stack as a 3D Tensor Cuboid and introduce QuatTucker-ZigZagGPT, unifying: (1) 4D Quaternion Hamilton GEMMs in bfloat16 (4x FFN compression), (2) 64x64 Block-Diagonal Tucker SwiGLU Cores for depth-specific rotations over a shared basis, (3) Braided ZigZag Diamond Routing (L0 -> L2 -> L1 -> L3) for zero-FLOP top-down refinement, and (4) Exact 2D Zero-Loss Continual Scaling in both width and depth. We also introduce Annealed Gated Bounce-Back Scaffolding (GatedBounce), which self-distills to 0.0% inference overhead. Across algorithmic tasks (8-bit Parity, 56-seed routing ablations, Mod-53 Grokking) and language modeling (TinyStories, FineWeb-Edu), our model outperforms standard Transformers at 2x-7.6x compression. On A100, QuatTucker-ZigZagGPT-12 (39.0M backbone) beats StandardGPT-12 (85.0M backbone) at 5/5 controlled checkpoints. Continually scaling one lineage across 6.00B tokens via zero-loss width growth (rank 6144 -> 8192), two bit-exact depth expansions (L = 12 -> 16 -> 20, 70.9M backbone, max_diff = 0.000000), and WSD cooldown reaches 3.3582 FineWeb-Edu loss (28.74 PPL) and 44.75% full-split zero-shot accuracy across 16,955 questions (ARC-Easy, HellaSwag, WinoGrande, BoolQ), matching GPT-3 Small (125M, 300B tokens) at 50x fewer tokens.

</details>


### [67] [MolGPAO: Molecular Generative Pre-trained Autoregressive Octree with Speculative Decoding and Sub-Millisecond Seam Healing for 3D Molecules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33985)
*Shangqian Chen,Guojiang Zhao,Zheng Cheng,Jiankun Wang,Hongshuai Wang,Zhifeng Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 MolGPAO，一种纯3D八叉树自回归分子生成架构与单输出推测式树解码引擎，无需外部代理模型、强化学习或推理期MLIP弛豫，在GEOM-Drugs、QM9、OE62上以极低算力显著提升有效连通产率、几何稳定性与性质保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自适应3D八叉树上的自回归生成（Uni-3DAR）相对稠密O(N^2)等变扩散/流模型具有O(N log N)稀疏优势，但在大药物分子上存在几何平衡差、闭壳层价态不稳定、性质保真度低的问题，根源在于Morton缝坐标跳变、叶子密集计算与根节点计数陷阱（root-count trapping）、内部critic的目标捷径崩塌。

Method: (1) 非陷阱式6维ScaleFreeBudgetEmbedding，含L5→L1共价堆积先验；(2) FlashAttention-2 pause-and-gather推测式八叉树漏斗（m_s1→m_s2→1，M_out=1）；(3) 抗捷径的ground-truth TwoStageOctreeProcessCritic，含64-RBF共价边门控与SO(3)不变12通道多极矩范数；(4) <0.5 ms的4层SE(3) PostTreeGraphRefiner，用于Morton缝修复、expected-XYZ成键监督、选择性CFG与零重原子位移价态调和；(5) 无气泡stride-2 GPU叶解码。

Result: GEOM-Drugs（0.49 GPU-h）：药物尺度有效连通产率提升6.78倍（12.7%→86.2%，原子稳定性99.83%），N=100时比100步FlowMol快7.3倍（或在2.0倍产率下快1.65倍）；QM9（0.43 GPU-h）：HOMO误差-12.7%、偶极矩-21.0%，优于156 GPU-h的PropMolFlow且算力低362倍；OE62（0.56 GPU-h，PBE0/def2-TZVP验证）：DFT命中率翻倍（33.3% vs 16.7%），算力低92.8倍。盲测QM9上critic的相关系数r=0.9867–0.9989。

Conclusion: 纯3D八叉树自回归生成结合树推测解码，可在无外部代理、无RL、无推理期弛豫的严苛约束下，同时实现高有效连通产率、价态稳定、性质保真与数量级算力节省，为大分子3D生成提供高效可扩展范式。

Abstract: Autoregressive generation over adaptive 3D octrees (Uni-3DAR) offers an intrinsic $O(N \log N)$ sparsity advantage over dense $O(N^2)$ equivariant diffusion and flow models on large drug-like molecules, yet lags in geometric equilibrium, closed-shell valence stability, and property fidelity due to Morton-seam coordinate jumps, leaf-heavy compute with root-count trapping, and target-shortcut collapse in internal critics. We introduce MolGPAO (Molecular Generative Pre-trained Autoregressive Octree), a standalone 3D octree architecture and single-output speculative tree-decoding engine using zero external surrogates, zero reinforcement learning, and zero inference-time MLIP relaxation. MolGPAO unifies: (1) non-trapping 6-D ScaleFreeBudgetEmbedding with L5-to-L1 covalent packing priors; (2) a FlashAttention-2 pause-and-gather speculative octree funnel ($m_{s1} \to m_{s2} \to 1$, $M_{\mathrm{out}} = 1$); (3) an anti-shortcut ground-truth TwoStageOctreeProcessCritic with 64-RBF covalent edge gating and $SO(3)$-invariant 12-channel multipole norms (blind QM9 $r = 0.9867$--$0.9989$); (4) a $<0.5$ ms 4-layer $SE(3)$ PostTreeGraphRefiner for Morton-seam healing, expected-XYZ bonded supervision, selective CFG, and zero-heavy-displacement valence reconciliation; and (5) bubble-free stride-2 GPU leaf decoding. Across GEOM-Drugs (0.49 GPU-h), disjoint 50k/50k QM9 (0.43 GPU-h), and PBE0/def2-TZVP-verified OE62 (0.56 GPU-h), MolGPAO lifts drug-scale valid-connected yield 6.78x (12.7% to 86.2%, 99.83% atom stability), runs 7.3x faster (or 1.65x faster at 2.0x yield) than 100-step FlowMol at $N=100$, beats 156 GPU-h PropMolFlow on QM9 HOMO (-12.7%) and dipole (-21.0%) at 362x lower compute, and doubles live DFT hit rate on OE62 (33.3% vs. 16.7%) at 92.8x lower compute.

</details>


### [68] [The Reference Matters: Displacement Probing for Weight-Space Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33998)
*Haechan Shin,Hoonki Lee,Jungwoon Kang,Wooyeol Baek,Sangyeob Kim,Jongyoo Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文将 Model Tree 中探针成功的原因归因于模型相对共享起点的“位移”，据此提出对位移而非原始权重进行探针的 DSProbe 及改进版 R-DSProbe，在 Model Jungle 四类架构上取得 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作显示基于探针的方法可在 Model Tree（共享预训练起源微调所得的模型集合）内有效预测模型属性，但缺乏对其成功机理的解释。作者认为关键在于叶子模型共享同一初始权重，真正区分它们的是相对该起源的位移。

Method: 提出 DiSplacement Probe（DSProbe），对权重位移而非原始权重做探针；沿两条轴拓展：一是扩展到二阶 Gram 响应，其位移在一阶意义上可追踪微调引起的特征变化；二是将参考点视为设计变量，学习一个共享分量在预训练起源之外进一步减除。

Result: 在 Model Jungle 基准的四种架构上，改进后的 Refined-DSProbe（R-DSProbe）达到新的 SOTA；以显著更少的探针保持与先前相当的准确率；并支持跨 Model Tree 共享单一编码器，将 metanetwork 参数量减少 3.61 倍。

Conclusion: Model Tree 内探针的有效性源于共享起源导致的位移主导结构，显式建模位移是一种更高效、更可迁移的权重空间学习范式。代码已开源。

Abstract: Weight-space learning predicts a model's attributes directly from its parameters, and probing-based methods do so efficiently by reading a model's responses to learned probes. Prior work shows that probing succeeds within a Model Tree -- models fine-tuned from a shared pretrained origin -- but not why. We trace this to the shared origin itself: since a tree's leaves start from the same weights, what distinguishes them is their displacement from that origin. This motivates probing the displacement rather than the raw weight, an intervention we call the DiSplacement Probe (DSProbe). We develop this idea along two axes: extending it to second-order Gram responses, whose displacement, to first order, tracks the feature changes induced by fine-tuning; and treating the reference as a design choice, learning a shared component to subtract beyond the pretrained origin. Across four architectures on the Model Jungle benchmark, the resulting Refined-DSProbe (R-DSProbe) sets a new state of the art, matches prior accuracy with substantially fewer probes, and supports a single encoder shared across Model Trees, reducing metanetwork parameters by 3.61 times. Code is available at https://github.com/hoonki2002/DSProbe.

</details>


### [69] [CARROT: Competitive Adaptive Graph Clustering via Semi-Relaxed Optimal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34012)
*Ching Fan Chiang,Jia-Yu Pan,Wen-Chieh Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CARROT，将半松弛最优传输引入深度图聚类，联合解决聚类数K未知与簇规模不均衡两大问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度图聚类方法依赖预先指定的簇数K，K设定错误时无法恢复真实结构；且方法多隐含偏好均衡划分，导致少数簇难以被发现。实际应用中K未知且簇规模高度不均衡。

Method: CARROT先预训练节点嵌入以构建有意义的嵌入空间，再通过自适应聚类机制联合优化嵌入与簇分配。该机制包含三部分：半松弛最优传输（使簇容量自适应数据）、竞争损失（将质量集中于有充分支撑的簇原型，使退化簇"饥饿"）、硬剪枝（逐步剔除退化簇）。

Result: 在五个公开基准上取得SOTA的NMI与ARI，无需监督即可准确恢复簇数，并能忠实保留规模不均衡的社区结构。

Conclusion: 半松弛最优传输结合竞争与剪枝机制，可有效实现无需预设K、对规模不均衡鲁棒的深度图聚类。

Abstract: Deep graph clustering (DGC) has emerged as a prominent paradigm for unsupervised attributed graph clustering. However, existing methods often struggle with two practical challenges encountered in real-world applications: the number of clusters $K$ is unknown a priori, and cluster sizes are frequently highly imbalanced. Methods relying on a pre-specified $K$ may fail to recover the underlying clustering structure when $K$ is misspecified, while many approaches implicitly favor balanced partitions, hindering the discovery of minority clusters. We propose CARROT, a Competitive Adaptive gRaph clustering framework via semi-Relaxed Optimal Transport, which introduces semi-relaxed optimal transport into deep graph clustering to jointly address both challenges. CARROT first pre-trains node embeddings to establish a meaningful embedding space, and then jointly optimizes embeddings and cluster assignments through an Adaptive Clustering Mechanism.
