The mechanism combines semi-relaxed optimal transport to adapt cluster capacities to the underlying data, a competition loss to concentrate mass onto well-supported cluster prototypes, thereby starving degenerate ones, and hard pruning to progressively eliminate them. Extensive experiments on five public benchmarks demonstrate that CARROT achieves state-of-the-art NMI and ARI, accurately recovers the number of clusters without supervision, and faithfully preserves size-imbalanced community structures.

</details>


### [70] [Assessing the Physical Interpretability of AlphaEarth Foundation Embeddings Through Multi-Sensor Satellite Time-Series Benchmarking for Agricultural Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34019)
*El Houcine El Moussaoui,Chouaib El Hachimi,Saïd Khabba,Salwa Belaqziz,Bouchra Ait Hssaine,Abderrahman Lahrouni,Salah Er-Raki,Abdelghani Chehbouni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究评估AlphaEarth基础模型(AEF)嵌入在摩洛哥农业LULC与灌溉类型制图中的效用：AEF嵌入精度略低于多时相时间序列特征但相近（LULC 0.91 vs 0.93；灌溉 0.85 vs 0.87），且显著减少数据处理与人工特征工程；物理解码显示其强编码光谱指数、植被与SAR结构信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确且动态的农业土地利用/土地覆盖(LULC)制图对农业水资源管理至关重要，在水资源稀缺地区（如摩洛哥）尤为关键。传统依赖卫星产品的直接使用需要大量数据处理与人工特征工程，作者因此希望检验基础模型嵌入能否提供高效、可迁移的替代方案，并评估其是否隐含可重建的生物物理信息。

Method: 以摩洛哥为研究区，对比AEF嵌入与单时相、时间序列遥感特征；采用三种机器学习分类器：XGBoost、CatBoost、TabPFN；任务为农业LULC分类与灌溉类型制图；并通过"physical probing"（物理探针/相关性分析）检验嵌入与光谱指数、植被指数及SAR后向散射的关联。

Result: 多时相特征时间序列性能最高：TabPFN下LULC精度0.93、灌溉类型0.87；AEF嵌入结果可比（0.91、0.85），同时减少对大量数据处理和人工特征工程的依赖。TabPFN空间LULC制图在三种特征配置下总体精度相近（0.85–0.86），但树覆盖制图差异显著：地表反射率为14.57%，AEF为29.79%。物理探针显示AEF嵌入强编码光谱指数（A30-SWIR: ρ=0.58–0.67）、水分指数（A38-NDMI: ρ=-0.72）、植被（A49-NDVI: ρ=0.72–0.73）及SAR结构（A25-VH: ρ=0.71）。

Conclusion: AEF嵌入可作为传统遥感特征的高效、可迁移替代方案，在精度略有损失的情况下大幅降低特征工程成本；但其类别组成（如树覆盖比例）对特征配置敏感，总体精度可能掩盖类别级偏差。未来研究应引入物理知识以增强嵌入方法的可解释性，构建更透明可信的农业AI系统。

Abstract: Accurate and dynamic maps of agricultural LULC are important for agricultural water resource management, particularly in areas where water scarcity is prevalent, such as Morocco. Here, we evaluate the utility of AlphaEarth Foundation Model (AEF) embeddings for agricultural LULC and irrigation-type mapping in Morocco. AEF embeddings were compared to single-date and time-series remote-sensing features using three machine-learning classifiers: XGBoost, CatBoost, and TabPFN. We also evaluated whether AEF embeddings can: (a) provide an efficient and transferable alternative to conventional direct use of Earth observation products and (b) reconstruct biophysical information. Results showed that multi-temporal feature time series achieved the highest performance, with accuracies of 0.93 for agricultural LULC and 0.87 for irrigation-type mapping when using TabPFN. However, AEF embeddings yielded comparable results, achieving accuracies of 0.91 for agricultural LULC and 0.85 for irrigation-type mapping, while reducing the need for extensive data processing and manual feature engineering typically required for satellite products. Spatial LULC mapping using TabPFN achieved comparable overall accuracies (0.85-0.86) across the three feature configurations, but revealed substantial differences in mapped tree cover, ranging from 14.57% for surface reflectance to 29.79% for AFE. Physical probing revealed that AEF embeddings strongly encode spectral indices (A30-SWIR: rho=0.58-0.67), (A38-NDMI: rho=-0.72), vegetation (A49-NDVI: rho = 0.72-0.73), and SAR structure (A25-VH: rho = 0.71). Future research should focus on enhancing the interpretability of embedding-based approaches by incorporating physical knowledge to develop more transparent and trustworthy AI systems for agricultural applications.

</details>


### [71] [Decentralized Clustered Federated Learning for Cellular Traffic Forecasting over LEO Constellations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34049)
*Jonas Kirch,Tiago Koketsu Rodrigues,Yuichi Kawamoto*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将去中心化联邦聚类（DFCA）扩展到LEO卫星场景，用于蜂窝网络流量预测，在无地面聚合器条件下通过k近邻星间gossip协作，实现15.3%的RMSE下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LEO星座有望作为6G非地面网络中的轨道边缘计算节点，为地面蜂窝基础设施提供预测与控制任务；但地面小区流量模式异质、与地面站链路间歇、卫星轨道运动导致观测数据持续漂移，使模型训练困难。

Method: 在LEO场景下扩展去中心化联邦聚类（DFCA），应用于Telecom Italia Milan+Trentino数据集的蜂窝互联网流量预测；卫星仅通过k近邻星间gossip协作，无地面侧聚合器；采用零样本头选择使各卫星专门适配其正下方地面小区的潜在流量模式。

Result: 在K=20个训练后轨道快照上取平均，相比同等容量的去中心化FedAvg基线，RMSE降低15.3%，簇恢复准确率达85.3%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，在真实蜂窝-LEO场景固有的结构异质性下，聚类式专门化能够带来可测量的性能增益。

Abstract: Low Earth Orbit (LEO) satellite constellations are positioned to act as orbital edge-compute nodes in emerging 6G non-terrestrial networks, supporting forecasting and control tasks for ground cellular infrastructure. However, training such models is challenged by heterogeneous ground-cell traffic patterns, intermittent links to ground stations, and the satellites' own orbital motion, which continuously shifts the data each satellite observes. In this work, we extend Decentralized Federated Clustering (DFCA) to the LEO setting and apply it to cellular internet-traffic forecasting on the Telecom Italia Milan+Trentino dataset. Satellites cooperate exclusively through $k$-nearest inter-satellite gossip with no ground-side aggregator, while zero-shot head selection lets each satellite specialize to the latent traffic regime of the ground cell currently beneath it. Averaged over $K{=}20$ post-training orbital snapshots, our extension reduces forecasting Root Mean Squared Error (RMSE) by $15.3\%$ over a decentralized FedAvg baseline of equivalent capacity, with cluster recovery accuracy of $85.3\%$. The results indicate that clustered specialization yields measurable gains under the structural heterogeneity inherent to realistic cellular-over-LEO scenarios.

</details>


### [72] [CARE-WM: Same-Horizon Self-Conditioning with Constraint-Adaptive Regularization for Latent World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34054)
*Yuchen Lin,Kai Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CARE-WM：一种即插即用的潜空间世界模型训练方法，通过自预测训练与自适应潜正则化，同时获得准确多步预测与任务对齐的潜距离，无需额外监督或代价模型即可提升规划成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜世界模型规划需同时满足两点：多步预测准确（训练用真值观测、规划时却依赖自身想象，存在分布偏移），以及潜空间距离能反映朝向目标的物理接近度（潜距离近不等于任务效果好）。现有方法或用辅助训练目标，或外接任务代价读出，分别带来额外训练开销或使潜几何与任务代价错位。

Method: 提出 CARE-WM：让预测器在自己的预测上训练（自预测/闭环训练，缓解曝光偏差），并自适应校准潜正则化的强度；不引入额外监督、独立代价模型，也不改动 LeWM 架构与欧氏距离代价规划器，仅一个超参数配置。

Result: 在全部评估的连续控制任务上，CARE-WM 相对 LeWM 提升平均规划成功率，并在多数任务上达到或超过 LeWM 系列其他方法的最佳结果；分析显示开环潜预测误差更低，潜代价排序与物理任务代价更一致。

Conclusion: 自预测训练与自适应潜正则化可同时改善预测精度与潜几何-任务代价对齐，且以极低改动成本实现，说明潜空间结构对齐是提升潜世界模型规划性能的关键且可行的方向。

Abstract: Planning with latent world models requires both accurate multi-step predictions and latent distances that reflect physical proximity to the goal. However, models are typically trained on ground-truth observations but must rely on their own imaginations during planning, while closer latent distances do not necessarily correspond to better task outcomes. Existing methods resort to auxiliary training objectives or additional task-cost readouts, which respectively incur extra training overhead or leave the latent geometry itself misaligned with task cost. We introduce CARE-WM, a drop-in training method that meets both requirements without additional supervision, a separate cost model, or any change to LeWM's architecture and Euclidean-cost planner. CARE-WM trains the predictor on its own predictions and adaptively calibrates the strength of latent regularization. With a single hyperparameter configuration, CARE-WM improves mean planning success over LeWM across all evaluated continuous-control tasks and matches or exceeds the best results of other LeWM-family methods on most tasks. Further analyses show lower open-loop latent prediction error and closer agreement between latent-cost rankings and physical task costs.

</details>


### [73] [Feature Engineered Reinforcement Learning for S-pair Selection in Buchberger's Algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34088)
*Uzma Shafiq,Matthew England,Nayyar Zaidi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将基于S-多项式（而非S-对）的17维统计特征用于PPO策略，在一般多项式理想的Buchberger算法S-对选择上全面超越基线，工作量降低41–53%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: S-对选取顺序对Buchberger算法计算Groebner基的开销影响可达数量级差异。已有工作表明，以指数向量为输入的强化学习智能体在二项理想上优于经典启发式，但尚未推广到一般多项式理想。

Method: 用统计特征刻画多项式，构造17维特征向量，使用PPO在五类随机理想分布上训练S-对选择策略；对比S-对特征与S-多项式特征，并与重新训练的RL基线及degree启发式比较。

Result: S-多项式特征在全部五种分布上显著优于S-对特征；新策略在全部五种分布上优于重训的RL基线，在四种学习有效的分布上相对degree启发式减少41–53%的算法工作量；由于特征工程附加开销，实际耗时（wall-clock）与基线相近。

Conclusion: 基于S-多项式的统计特征是一种有效的强化学习输入表示，兼顾性能提升与更强的跨分布迁移能力；当前代价主要体现在特征计算开销上。

Abstract: Selecting the order in which to process S-pairs is a crucial decision in Buchberger's algorithm for computing Groebner bases: this choice can change the cost by orders of magnitude. Recent work has shown that reinforcement learning agents viewing exponent vectors of the polynomials outperform classical heuristics on binomial ideals. We extend this line of work to general polynomial ideals by using statistical features. We introduce a 17-dimensional feature vector to describe a polynomial and use PPO to train policies on five distributions of random ideals. We find that using S-polynomial features significantly outperforms S-pair features on all five distributions. The new policies also outperform the retrained RL baseline on all five distributions, reducing algorithmic work by 41--53\% relative to the degree heuristic on four distributions where learning helps. The additional cost of the feature engineering means that wall-clock cost remains similar to the baseline, but our new approach benefits from easier transfer to unseen distributions.

</details>


### [74] [Improving Tail-Augmented Speculative Drafting with Hidden State Mixing and Gradient Detach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34092)
*Yaniv Galron,Rabeeh Karimi Mahabadi,Maor Ashkenazi,Roger Waleffe,Talor Abramovich,Tiyasa Mitra,Yoav Miron,Dor Tsur,Sanjeev Satheesh,Jimmy Zhang,Jiaji Huang,Yonatan Geifman,Mostofa Patwary,Mohammad Shoeybi,Bita Rouhani,Ran Zilberstein*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出两种仅用于训练的方法（HSM 与 HSGD）以缓解 MTP/EAGLE 式草稿模型在自回归推理时的训练-推理失配，不增加推理开销，并在多个模型上提升接受率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MTP 模块与 EAGLE 式草稿器在训练时只见到来自主干或单步 MTP 的隐状态序列，而推理时需关注“目标模型前缀 + 递归生成 MTP 状态构成的增长尾部”，造成训练-推理失配。EAGLE-3 的 TTT 虽可模拟该过程，但带来额外显存与专用注意力掩码的开销。

Method: 提出两种纯训练期方法：1）Hidden State Mixing（HSM）：在与同一预测目标对齐的主干隐状态与 MTP 隐状态间采样，使草稿器接触不同 MTP 步的混合状态，而无需显式模拟推理；2）Hidden State Gradient Detach（HSGD）：随机阻断隐状态与嵌入通路的梯度、保持前向计算的正则化方法。二者均不增加推理成本。

Result: 在联合预训练+监督微调下，HSGD 在草稿序列早期位置接受率最高，HSM 在后期位置更优；该趋势使 HSM 适合长草稿长度服务、HSGD 适合短草稿长度服务。在 4 个 Nemotron 模型与 DeepSeek-V4-Pro 上，冻结主干的 HSM 式 MTP boosting 相比协同训练 MTP 将平均接受长度最高提升 52.2%；对 EAGLE-3 草稿器，HSM 与 TTT 接受率相当，并在 Qwen3-8B 四步对比中将训练峰值预留显存降低 48%。

Conclusion: HSM 与 HSGD 以零推理开销有效缓解自回归草稿的训练-推理失配，且可按草稿长度选择策略；HSM 已在 Megatron-LM 与 NVIDIA Model Optimizer 中开源。

Abstract: Multi-token prediction (MTP) modules and EAGLE-style drafters support efficient speculative decoding by reusing target-model hidden states. Their autoregressive use, however, introduces a training-inference mismatch: training supplies sequences of hidden states from the backbone or a single MTP step, whereas inference attends to a target-model prefix followed by a growing tail of recurrently generated MTP states. EAGLE-3's training-time test (TTT) explicitly simulates this behavior during training, at the cost of additional memory and specialized attention masks. We introduce two training-only methods for improving autoregressive drafting. Hidden State Mixing (HSM) samples among backbone and MTP hidden states aligned to the same prediction target, exposing the drafter to mixtures of states from different MTP steps without explicitly simulating inference. Hidden State Gradient Detach (HSGD) is a regularization method that stochastically blocks gradients through hidden state and embedding pathways while preserving the forward computation. Neither method adds inference cost. In joint pretraining followed by supervised fine-tuning, HSGD yields the highest acceptance rates at early draft positions, while HSM performs best later in the draft. These acceptance rate trends favor HSM for long-draft-length serving and HSGD for short-draft-length serving. Furthermore, across four Nemotron models and DeepSeek-V4-Pro, HSM-based MTP boosting with a frozen backbone improves mean acceptance length by up to 52.2% over the co-trained MTP. For EAGLE-3 drafters, HSM offers competitive acceptance with TTT, with 48% lower peak reserved training memory in a four-step Qwen3-8B comparison. HSM implementations are publicly available in Megatron-LM and NVIDIA Model Optimizer.

</details>


### [75] [AlignPre: Skill-Aligned Post-Training for Latent-Free Generative Precipitation Nowcasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34101)
*Demin Yu,Kenghong Lin,Xutao Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 AlignPre——一种端到端像素空间降水临近预报框架，并引入"技能对齐后训练"(SAPT)，用可微的预报检验评分作为训练反馈，在三个真实基准上超越确定性及生成式SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式扩散预报方法依赖分别训练的组件（确定性分支、潜空间压缩模块），流程复杂；更根本的问题是，仅做分布匹配并未显式优化业务关心的预报检验技能（forecast skill）。

Method: AlignPre 采用端到端的 flow-matching 预报器，配有层次化时空架构，直接在像素空间合成雷达场，无需潜变量压缩。后训练阶段 SAPT 利用预报检验评分的解析结构构造可微目标，将评分梯度从雷达场反传至生成器，使检验指标成为直接训练信号。

Result: 在覆盖不同预报时效的三个真实世界基准上，AlignPre 在预报技能上稳定优于SOTA的确定性方法与生成式方法。

Conclusion: 将生成式建模与领域特定的检验评分对齐，是气象预报中一种有效的范式。

Abstract: Precipitation nowcasting requires accurate short-term predictions under rapidly evolving spatiotemporal dynamics. Generative diffusion models can produce detailed and competitive radar forecasts by modeling conditional distributions. However, most existing approaches rely on separately trained components, such as deterministic branches or latent compression modules. More fundamentally, distribution matching alone fails to explicitly optimize operational forecast skill. We propose AlignPre, a streamlined pixel-space nowcasting framework that bridges this gap via skill-aligned post-training (SAPT). AlignPre employs an end-to-end flow-matching forecaster with a hierarchical spatiotemporal architecture for distribution matching and direct radar-field synthesis, without latent compression. During post-training, SAPT exploits the analytic structure of forecast verification scores to construct a differentiable objective. By backpropagating gradients of this objective from radar fields to the generator, SAPT turns forecast verification into direct training feedback. Across three real-world benchmarks spanning different forecast horizons, AlignPre consistently surpasses state-of-the-art deterministic and generative baselines in forecast skill, demonstrating that aligning generative modeling with domain-specific verification provides an effective paradigm for meteorological forecasting.

</details>


### [76] [Do Machine Learning Ensembles Beat Published Quotas? Target Parameterization, Censored Baselines, and Conformal Intervals Under Admissions Reform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34104)
*Dat Tat Mai,James Jin Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文以越南2022–2026本科录取面板（26,895条项目-年记录、309所高校）为对象，将公开招生名额上限（published quotas）作为基准目标，比较机器学习、多层模型与截尾计量估计器的预测表现，并提出以“填报率对数” ln(D/S) 替代对数增长率的重新参数化方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集中式高校录取系统中，教育部与院校分析者普遍把入学前公布的招生名额上限当作默认的招生规模预测。但由于大量项目实际录取数恰好等于公布名额、且存在未填满的"幻影容量"（phantom capacity），不加锚定的增长类模型会产生系统性偏差，因此需要一个更贴合配额约束结构的预测框架并进行严格样本外评估。

Method: 使用2022–2026越南本科审计面板（26,895项目-年、309所高校），链接严格滞后一期的 t-1 职业空缺信号；在5,385个2024年延续项目上做五折按高校分组的交叉验证，并在3,348个匹配（其中2,179个口径一致）的2025年项目上做纯样本外检验。方法上对比机器学习集成、多层模型（REML HLM）、Tobit、Cragg、capped L1 及 hurdle stacking（H-Hard、H-Soft）、QA-Stack、Cragg-ML 等；并做目标变量消融（含放宽非约束截断的单因子检验）、TreeSHAP 归因与 cluster-weighted conformal 覆盖率评估。

Result: 62.8%的2024年项目录取数精确等于公布名额；未锚定的对数增长集成模型精度下降，最差5%的项目通过未填满的幻影容量贡献了一半的总配额误差。改用 ln(D_it/S_it) 参数化后，2024年MAE由14.86降至11.67（降21.5%），使ML与Tobit、Cragg、REML HLM、capped L1对齐；学费与院校层级两项的合并TreeSHAP归因从2.5%升至45.8%。Hurdle stacking 取得最低全样本2024 MAE（H-Hard 11.29；H-Soft 11.32 vs. 12.35，p=0.022，仅通过10%而非5% FDR；fold-overlap p=0.134）；QA-Stack 在混合上报校区优于H-Soft；Tobit（中位数）在两套90% MCS下与H-Soft持平；interior-only Cragg-ML 将RMSE降至28.1。在2025年统一志愿改革下，精确填满率降至10.4%，H-Soft与capped L1持平（p=0.979），90% cluster-weighted conformal覆盖从89.3%降至74.6%。257个上报校区核对的2026年全国总量（627,114–629,256）落在604,533–662,044区间内。

Conclusion: 将预测目标重新参数化为配额填报率而非增长率，是提升招生规模预测精度与可解释性的关键；在此框架下 ML 与截尾计量模型趋于一致，hurdle stacking 在精确填满主导的情境中表现最优。但政策改革（2025统一志愿）显著破坏精确填满结构与区间覆盖性，说明基于公布配额的历史锚定在制度变动后可靠度下降，实际应用需动态校准与共形覆盖监控。

Abstract: Public admission capacity caps published prior to matriculation are widely used by ministries and institutional analysts as default intake projections in centralized postsecondary clearinghouses. Using an audited 2022 to 2026 Vietnamese undergraduate panel (26,895 program-year records across 309 universities) linked to strictly lagged t-1 occupational vacancy signals, this paper evaluates machine learning, multilevel, and censored econometric estimators against published quotas across 5,385 continuing 2024 programs (five-fold university-grouped cross-validation) and 3,348 matched (2,179 grain-consistent) 2025 programs strictly out of time. Because 62.8% of 2024 programs enroll their exact published quota, unanchored log-growth ensembles degrade accuracy, while the worst 5% generate half of total quota error via unfilled "phantom capacity." Controlled target ablations, including a single-factor test under widened non-binding clipping, show that parameterizing the log fill ratio ln(D_it / S_it) instead of log growth reduces 2024 MAE by 21.5% (14.86 to 11.67), aligns ML with Tobit, Cragg, REML HLM, and capped L1 baselines, and lifts combined tuition and institutional-tier TreeSHAP attribution from 2.5% to 45.8%. Hurdle stacking attains the lowest full-census 2024 MAE (H-Hard 11.29; H-Soft 11.32 vs. 12.35, p = 0.022, clearing 10% not 5% FDR; fold-overlap p = 0.134), whereas continuous QA-Stack outperforms H-Soft on mixed-reporting campuses, Tobit (median) ties H-Soft across both 90% MCS sets, and interior-only Cragg-ML lowers RMSE to 28.1. Under Vietnam's 2025 unified-clearing reform, exact filling falls to 10.4%, H-Soft and capped L1 tie quotas (p = 0.979), and 90% cluster-weighted conformal coverage drops from 89.3% to 74.6%. Across 257 reporting campuses, reconciled 2026 national totals (627,114 to 629,256) lie squarely within 604,533 to 662,044.

</details>


### [77] [SysBio-SR: Benchmarking Symbolic Regression for Trajectory Fidelity and Mechanism Recovery in Systems Biology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34106)
*Heng Rao,Dan Gao,Benjamin J Hoffman,Yu Gu,Zhenghao Liu,Ge Yu,Yang Cao,Minghan Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SysBio-SR生物学符号回归基准（198个带反应标注的ODE系统），揭示轨迹精度严重高估机制恢复，并给出兼顾轨迹保真与结构简约的KineSelect框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在系统生物学中，轨迹拟合准确并不等价于恢复真实反应机制；现有基准仅评估数值精度、表达式复杂度或方程级相似度，缺乏对"恢复反应与参考机制一致性"的系统评估。

Method: 构建SysBio-SR基准：198个文献来源的生物学ODE系统，含质量作用、Michaelis–Menten、Hill及复杂动力学反应标注；全部系统评估分布内重建与OOD外推的轨迹保真，其中161个系统评估机制恢复。评测11种符号发现方法的轨迹保真度、3种基于反应的方法的机制恢复；据此提出KineSelect：在生物学合理动力学定律下搜索候选反应拓扑，选出兼顾轨迹保真与结构简约的最优机制。

Result: 联合分析显示基于轨迹的评估严重高估真实机制恢复率；KineSelect相较最强基线，聚合OOD外推R²提升19.2%，整体反应F1提升14.3%。

Conclusion: 轨迹保真度不能作为机制恢复的代理指标，需以反应级一致性评估符号回归；KineSelect通过生物约束下的拓扑搜索与简约性权衡，在OOD泛化与反应恢复上均取得一致提升。

Abstract: Symbolic regression seeks to discover interpretable governing equations for dynamical systems from data. In systems biology, however, accurately reproducing system trajectories does not necessarily imply recovery of the underlying reaction mechanisms. Existing benchmarks evaluate numerical accuracy, expression complexity, or equation-level similarity, but not agreement between recovered reactions and reference mechanisms in biological systems. We introduce SysBio-SR, a benchmark of 198 literature-based biological ODE systems with reaction annotations spanning mass action, Michaelis--Menten, Hill, and complex kinetics. Trajectory fidelity is evaluated across all systems under in-distribution reconstruction and out-of-distribution (OOD) extrapolation, while mechanism recovery is assessed on the subset of 161 systems. We evaluate trajectory fidelity across 11 symbolic-discovery methods and assess mechanism recovery for three reaction-based methods. Joint analysis across systems highlights how severely trajectory-based evaluation overestimates true mechanism recovery. Motivated by these findings, we develop KineSelect, a framework that searches candidate reaction topologies under biologically grounded kinetic laws to select optimal mechanisms that balance trajectory fidelity with structural parsimony. Compared with the strongest baseline, KineSelect improves aggregate OOD extrapolation \(R^2\) by 19.2\% and overall reaction F1 score by 14.3\%.

</details>


### [78] [Unstable Action Boundaries in Lethal Drone Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34121)
*Jacques Dumora,André-Louis Rochet,Simon Roburin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究考察无人机LLM代理的动作边界在目标描述词变化下是否稳定，发现两种失效模式：无需越狱即可对"people"等泛化描述调用致命工具，且看似保守的代理在语义偏移下变得不安全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具调用型LLM代理带来纯文本拒答行为无法覆盖的安全风险，在高影响动作（如自主武器、致命决策委派）场景尤为突出，需检验其动作边界对目标描述词变化的稳定性。

Method: 在基于真实无人机影像的仿真感知-动作流水线中评测近期主流LLM，模型可定位所请求目标并调用模拟致命工具；对比不同目标描述词（含泛化描述与受保护描述）下的调用行为。

Result: 发现两种失效模式：部分代理对"people"等普通泛化描述直接调用致命工具（无需越狱）；初始在受保护描述下表现保守的代理，在简单语义目标偏移后变得不安全。

Conclusion: 代理安全对齐在工具调用与描述词层面脆弱，文本级拒答不足以衡量安全性，需针对动作边界稳定性建立评测与防护。

Abstract: Tool-using large language model (LLM) agents raise safety risks that are not captured by text-only refusal behavior, especially when tools can trigger high-impact actions. This concern is salient in military settings, where autonomous weapons raise the possibility of delegating force decisions to software agents. In this work, we study whether a drone agent's action boundary is stable under changes to the target descriptor. We evaluate recent and popular LLMs in a simulated perception-action pipeline over real-world drone imagery, where models can localize requested targets and invoke a simulated lethal tool. We find two concerning failure modes. First, some LLM agents invoke the lethal tool for ordinary generic descriptors such as \emph{people}, requiring no jailbreak at all. Second, an agent that initially appears conservative under protected descriptors can become unsafe under a simple semantic target shift.

</details>


### [79] [MUSE: Multi-Task Uncertainty-Aware Self-Supervised Encoder for mmWave ISAC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34122)
*Nabeel Nisar Bhat,Jalees Nehvi,Rafael Berkvens,Jeroen Famaey*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MUSE统一框架，用共享自监督Transformer编码器实现多任务毫米波人体感知，并具备不确定性量化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 毫米波ISAC的人体感知任务各自需从零训练专用模型，缺乏跨任务可扩展性、迁移性与可靠性，也无法标记不可靠预测。

Method: 采用逐数据集tokenization的自监督Transformer，在五个数据集上通过掩码自编码（masked autoencoding）联合训练；微调阶段使用证据分类头（evidential classification heads）进行不确定性量化，并用温度缩放与共形预测（conformal prediction）校准置信度。

Result: 以单一共享编码器替代N个独立编码器，骨干参数减少N倍；相比任务专用模型分类性能提升9.7个百分点；四个分类任务上可选择性预测，弃答最不自信的20%预测可提升准确率；跨用户部署时，78%情况下错误预测的不确定性高于正确预测，选择性预测恢复9.2个百分点准确率且无需重训练。

Conclusion: 多任务联合学习可提升单任务泛化性，且不确定性信号在跨用户场景下依然可靠，支持免重训练的选择性预测部署。

Abstract: Integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) allows a wireless system to use the same waveform for communication and environment sensing. At mmWave frequency bands, ISAC enables diverse human sensing tasks, such as localization, gesture recognition, and pose estimation, with high precision. However, each task requires training a dedicated model, typically from scratch on a task-specific dataset, with no mechanism to flag unreliable predictions. This hurts the scalability, transferability, and reliability of mmWave ISAC models. In this work, we propose MUSE (Multi-task Uncertainty-aware Self-supervised Encoder), a unified framework for mmWave human sensing. MUSE leverages a self-supervised transformer model with per-dataset tokenization and is trained via masked autoencoding across five datasets. During fine-tuning, MUSE uses evidential classification heads that enable uncertainty quantification. MUSE replaces N separate encoders with a single shared transformer encoder, an N-times backbone parameter reduction, while achieving a 9.7 percentage points improvement in classification performance compared to the task-specific model, showing that learning jointly across tasks improves per-task generalization. Across four classification tasks, MUSE produces reliable uncertainty estimates that enable selective prediction, improving accuracy by abstaining on the least confident 20% of predictions. We further improve the reliability of the model's confidence estimates using temperature scaling and conformal prediction. Finally, MUSE's uncertainty signal remains reliable even under cross-user deployment, assigning higher uncertainty to wrong predictions than to correct ones 78% of the time, and recovering 9.2 percentage points of accuracy through selective prediction, with no additional retraining on the unseen user.

</details>


### [80] [Agent-as-a-Verifier: From Scores to Evidence for General Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34132)
*Yuxuan Sun,Hao,Chen,Nagarajan Natarajan,Sushrut Karmalkar,Niranjani Prasad,Amit Sharma,Hongxiang Fan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Agent-as-a-Verifier：将角色分离的 LLM 智能体置于形式化方法风格的"主张—义务—证书"协议中，生成可审计的验证证书，而非仅给标量分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证在测试时扩展、递归智能与自动化科研等范式中日益关键。现有判别式奖励模型的标量分数缺乏可审计的证据基础；现有生成式过程奖励模型虽有文本反馈，却未充分利用工具调用与迭代推理等智能体能力，难以支撑事实核查与细粒度主张验证。

Method: 以形式化方法思想构建 claim–obligation–certificate 协议，将角色分离的 LLM 智能体嵌入其中。对每个生成候选：抽取"判决盲"（verdict-blind）的主张依赖图；独立编译任务的接受契约（acceptance contract）；据此导出可检验的验证义务，并产出证书。

Result: 在 Terminal-Bench 2.1 上准确率超过 SOTA；在 FrontierMath 与 CyberGym 上，经其增强的智能体优于原始智能体；并能识别欺骗基准评测器的 reward-hacking 解法。

Conclusion: 可生成证书的智能体验证框架在准确性与可审计性上优于分数式验证，并对迭代与递归的智能体优化具有更广应用潜力。

Abstract: Verification is increasingly critical across various inference and learning paradigms, such as test-time scaling, recursive intelligence and automated research, where feedback can improve efficiency and optimization. Common score-based verifiers, or discriminative reward models, predict a scalar reward for an output. While useful for filtering, rewarding, and ranking, such scores provide no explicit, auditable evidentiary basis for the judgment. In parallel, although existing generative process reward models offer text feedback, most designs do not fully exploit agentic capabilities, such as tool use and iterative reasoning, which are essential for fact-checking and fine-grained claim verification. In this paper, we address these limitations by introducing Agent-as-a-Verifier, an agentic, certificate-producing verification system that places role-separated LLM agents inside a formal-methods-inspired claim-obligation-certificate protocol. Given a generation candidate, Agent-as-a-Verifier produces a certificate by extracting a verdict-blind dependency graph of claims, independently compiling the task's acceptance contract, and deriving checkable obligations. On Terminal-Bench 2.1, Agent-as-a-Verifier achieves higher accuracy than the state-of-the-art approach. Across different downstream applications, the augmented agent also outperforms the plain agent on FrontierMath and CyberGym. More importantly, Agent-as-a-Verifier is able to identify reward-hacking solutions that deceive the benchmark evaluator, highlighting its broader potential for iterative and recursive agent optimization. Code is available at https://github.com/sunyxedu/General-Verifier

</details>


### [81] [SOC-Net: Second-Order-Cone-Constrained Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34136)
*Seungseok Oh,Youngjae Min,Navid Azizan,Namhoon Cho*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SOC-Net架构，通过将主干网络输出的仿射提升投影到固定Lorentz锥乘积的可达切片上，实现对所有输入（含分布外）按构造满足输入依赖的二阶锥（SOC）约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多应用中神经网络需满足物理规律、安全要求与运行极限等先验知识，这些约束常可表示为SOC约束（如摩擦锥、推力界、输入鲁棒CBF条件）。现有方法需调用锥求解器、依赖收敛的迭代方案或为每个输入提供内点，存在可行性与效率问题。

Method: SOC-Net将主干输出的仿射提升投影到固定Lorentz锥乘积的可达切片，再将修正映射回输出空间；对可分离约束，投影分解为逐锥投影，每个投影具有闭式解或化为单个标量方程（与锥维数无关）。理论上证明在正则条件下保持主干网络的表达能力，并提供显式雅可比以支持端到端反向传播训练。

Result: 在带摩擦约束的接触力分配与基于输入鲁棒CBF的三维避障实验中，SOC-Net在每个评估输入上均满足约束，且评估延迟低于所测试的基于求解器和迭代的基线。

Conclusion: SOC-Net以构造方式保证SOC约束满足，兼顾表达能力与训练可行性，并在实验中展现更低的推理延迟，是约束满足型神经网络的一种高效方案。

Abstract: In many applications, neural networks must learn input-output maps that respect prior knowledge such as physical laws, safety requirements, and operating limits. These requirements constrain which outputs are admissible for a given input and often admit second-order cone (SOC) representations, including norm bounds and robust linear inequalities. Examples in control include friction cones, thrust bounds, and input-robust control barrier function (CBF) conditions. However, existing approaches to enforcing general input-dependent SOC constraints require a conic solver, an iterative scheme whose feasibility depends on convergence, or an interior feasible point for each input. We propose SOC-Net, a neural network architecture that enforces input-dependent SOC constraints by construction for every input, even outside the training distribution. SOC-Net projects an affine lifting of the backbone output onto the reachable slice of a fixed product of Lorentz cones and maps the correction back to the output space. For separable constraints, this projection splits into one projection per cone, and each is available in closed form or reduces to a single scalar equation, regardless of the cone dimension. We prove that SOC-Net retains the expressive power of the backbone network under regularity conditions and accommodates end-to-end training by standard backpropagation with an explicit Jacobian. Experiments on contact force allocation with friction constraints and 3-D obstacle avoidance with an input-robust CBF demonstrate that SOC-Net satisfies the constraints at every evaluated input, with lower evaluation latency than the tested solver-based and iterative baselines.

</details>


### [82] [When High Capacity Becomes Undeployable: Pore-Filling Constraints for Multi-Objective Optimization of Direct Air Capture Sorbents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34154)
*Yujie Liu,Xuehua Shen,Zuotai Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将孔填充边界编码为显式约束，并以此为诊断工具，说明无约束MOBO会奖励超出孔体积极限的不可部署配方，进而给出保守的可部署设计窗口（phi_hat ~ 0.4–0.5，45–55 wt%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 胺浸渍吸附剂标称CO2容量最高的配方往往最难部署，因为超出孔体积极限的胺会堵塞活性位点；然而面向直接空气捕获（DAC）的多目标贝叶斯优化（MOBO）通常把配方搜索当作无约束问题，缺乏对孔填充边界的显式刻画。

Method: 构造显式结果约束 c(x) = tanh(1 - phi_hat) - tau 表征孔填充边界，并作为诊断工具应用于人工整理、DOI可追溯的基准数据集（43篇文献、618个固态胺配方）；比较约束与无约束MOBO（100个随机种子）；分层协调仪器/实验室效应以校正动力学差异；评估标准超体积指标的偏置。

Result: 无约束MOBO获得更高标称容量（3.639 ± 0.815 vs. 2.964 ± 0.728 mmol/g，p = 1.0e-11），但63.2%的Pareto最优候选违反孔填充边界，约束下为39.3%；约束将前沿推向更低负载（55.8 vs. 65.2 wt%）与更大孔径。协调仪器效应后，77.8%的动力学方差来自实验室间差异，孔填充带来显著动力学惩罚（p = 0.002）。标准超体积对忽略可行性的方法高估13.9–16.1%（约束法为11.1%）。

Conclusion: 三项替代面分析（约束前沿、再生能耗第三目标、协调后动力学）一致指向一个保守的可部署窗口：孔填充率 phi_hat ≈ 0.4–0.5，对应45–55 wt%负载；基准数据集与分析输出随文提供。

Abstract: Amine-impregnated sorbents with the highest nominal CO2 capacity are frequently the least deployable, as amine beyond the pore-volume limit blocks active sites. Yet multi-objective Bayesian optimization (MOBO) for direct air capture (DAC) treats formulation search as unconstrained. We encode the pore-filling boundary as an explicit outcome constraint, c(x) = tanh(1 - phi_hat) - tau, and use it as a diagnostic instrument on a hand-curated, DOI-traceable benchmark of 618 solid-amine formulations from 43 publications. Unconstrained MOBO attains higher nominal capacity (3.639 +/- 0.815 vs. 2.964 +/- 0.728 mmol/g, p = 1.0e-11), but across 100 seeds 63.2% of its Pareto-optimal candidates violate the pore-filling boundary versus 39.3% under the constraint: it is rewarded for proposing candidates outside the pore-confined design regime. The constraint steers the front to lower loadings (55.8 vs. 65.2 wt%) and larger pores. The boundary is corroborated once apparatus effects are harmonized hierarchically: 77.8% of kinetic variance is between-laboratory, and pore filling carries a significant kinetic penalty (p = 0.002). Standard hypervolume over-credits feasibility-agnostic methods by 13.9-16.1% (vs. 11.1% constrained), and three surrogate-based analyses---the constrained front, a regeneration-energy third objective, and harmonized kinetics---consistently point to a conservative deployable window at pore filling phi_hat ~ 0.4-0.5 (45-55 wt% loading). The benchmark and associated analysis outputs accompany this submission.

</details>


### [83] [Cell-Type-Specific Expression Is Hard to Predict from Bulk, but It Is Still Useful](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34166)
*Gadi Naveh,Amit Miller,Gal Keinan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文用闭式精度上界解释CTSE（细胞类型特异表达）预测远难于比例预测的原因，并对106个经生物学筛选的(细胞类型,基因)目标做定向反卷积；在NSCLC生存分析中估计难度不能预测预后价值，但基于反卷积特征（多为CTSE）的稀疏模型预后表现与bulk基因模型相当，且可归因到具体细胞类型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床bulk RNA队列缺乏细胞类型分辨率，单细胞信息反卷积可提供细胞类型级特征。现有方法多预测细胞类型比例π_k，较少预测CTSE μ_kg；后者能捕捉比例遗漏的细胞状态，但近期基准显示其难度大得多。作者希望从理论上解释这一差距，并评估反卷积特征在临床预后建模中的实际价值。

Method: 推导闭式精度界：对CTSE给出逐基因线性估计器的R²上界；对比例，给出跨基因池化证据的最优线性估计器闭式R²及可计算下界，用于判别哪些细胞类型可学习。据此构建可学习性评分，事后筛选出106个高评分(细胞类型,基因)对进行定向反卷积。在非小细胞肺癌总体生存数据上比较估计难度与预后价值，并构建基于反卷积特征的稀疏生存模型与bulk基因稀疏模型对比。

Result: CTSE的可学习性受闭式R²上界约束，其精度天花板源于单基因信息量低；比例估计因跨基因汇总而精度更高，下界可预测可学习细胞类型。在106个定向目标上，估计难度与预后价值不相关。反卷积稀疏生存模型（主要为CTSE特征）预后性能与bulk基因稀疏模型相当，同时将信号归因到特定细胞类型；部分CTSE特征显示出对应bulk基因未见到的预后信号。

Conclusion: CTSE难估计有可量化的理论根源，但不影响其临床建模价值：反卷积特征可实现与bulk基因相当的预后预测，并提供细胞类型可解释性，甚至揭示bulk层面被掩盖的预后信号。因此应以生物学目标为导向、聚焦可学习性较高的(细胞类型,基因)对开展定向反卷积。

Abstract: Single-cell-informed deconvolution provides cell-type-resolved features for clinical bulk RNA cohorts. Most existing methods predict the proportions $π_k$ of the $K$ constituent cell types (CTs). Fewer predict cell-type-specific expression (CTSE) $μ_{kg}$ of gene $g$ in CT $k$, which can capture cell states that proportions miss, but recent benchmarks find it much harder. We explain this gap with closed-form accuracy bounds. For CTSE, we derive an $R^2$ ceiling for per-gene linear estimators. For proportions, the best linear estimator, which pools evidence across genes, has a closed-form $R^2$, and a computable lower bound on it predicts which CTs are learnable. We study targeted deconvolution of 106 biologically curated (CT, gene) pairs, found post hoc to have higher learnability scores. On overall survival in non-small cell lung cancer, estimation difficulty does not predict prognostic value across these targets. Yet a sparse survival model on deconvolved features, mostly CTSE, predicts survival as well as a sparse model on the bulk genes themselves, while attributing its signal to specific cell types. Some deconvolved CTSE features reveal prognostic signal not seen in the corresponding bulk gene.

</details>


### [84] [Text-Conditioned Low-Rank Adaptation of Time-Series Foundation Models for Multimodal Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34206)
*Kunqi Li,Bin Liang,Zhidong Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 C2LoRA-TS：将文本编码为临时 LoRA 权重更新，作用于冻结时序基础模型，实现文本条件化的多模态预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时序基础模型只提供可迁移的数值预测先验，但预测相关信息可能存在于文本中而未反映在观测序列里；现有方法多通过模型输入或中间表示融合上下文，缺少一种互补的参数层面适配机制。

Method: 编码文本并为冻结 TSFM 的选定权重矩阵生成临时 LoRA 更新；数值主干与文本编码器均保持冻结；无上下文时可精确复原原始 TSFM 输出。

Result: 在 Time-MMD 的九个领域、三种 TSFM 架构上，所提方法在每个架构与随机种子下聚合预测误差均低于冻结模型与静态 LoRA 基线；将实例特定文本替换为单一全局固定上下文后，所有架构性能均下降。

Conclusion: 文本条件化参数生成是冻结 TSFM 多模态预测的一条可行有效策略。

Abstract: Time-series foundation models (TSFMs) provide transferable numerical forecasting priors. However, forecast-relevant information may be conveyed in text without being reflected in the observed series. Many multimodal forecasting methods integrate such context through model inputs or intermediate representations. We investigate a complementary mechanism: text-conditioned parameter adaptation. We propose Context-to-LoRA for Time Series (C2LoRA-TS), which encodes text and generates temporary Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) updates for selected weight matrices of a frozen TSFM. Both the numerical backbone and text encoder remain frozen, and the method exactly recovers the frozen TSFM output when context is absent. Evaluated on Time-MMD across nine domains and three TSFM architectures, C2LoRA-TS achieves lower aggregate forecasting error than the frozen-TSFM and Static LoRA baselines for every architecture and seed. Replacing the instance-specific text with a single globally fixed context degrades performance for every architecture. These results support text-conditioned parameter generation as a viable strategy for multimodal forecasting with frozen TSFMs.

</details>


### [85] [Compute-Aware Submodular Online Learning in Open Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34211)
*Jing Liu,Luca Ballotta,Fangfei Li,Yang Tang,Ruggero Carli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究开放多智能体系统中基于子模效用的合作任务分配策略学习，提出在查询预算下用逆概率加权采样获得无偏梯度估计，并给出1/2近似遗憾界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放多智能体系统中，智能体贡献存在重叠，边际收益递减，效用可用子模函数刻画。标准策略更新需评估每个候选动作，在线计算代价过高，因此需要研究在评估查询预算受限情形下的在线学习效果。

Method: 对智能体-动作对按预设数量进行采样，利用逆概率加权（inverse-probability weighting）估计其边际增益，从而得到期望阶段效用的无偏梯度估计，并用于KL镜像上升（KL mirror-ascent）策略更新；同时分析查询预算对在线学习的影响。

Result: 推导出1/2近似遗憾界，该界可分解为最优策略与学习策略之间的跟踪误差（由KL散度度量）与一个依赖查询预算的反馈项。在合作信息覆盖（cooperative information coverage）任务上的实验表明，方法在显著减少在线计算量的同时保持高性能。

Conclusion: 通过采样与逆概率加权可在有限查询预算下实现高效且理论可保证的策略学习，验证了预算约束下子模合作任务分配策略学习的可行性与有效性。

Abstract: This paper studies policy learning for cooperative task allocation in open multi-agent systems, where submodular utilities model diminishing marginal returns due to overlapping agent contributions. While standard methods evaluate every candidate action to update the policy, this may incur an excessive computational cost online. Hence, we impose a budget on these evaluations and analytically study the effect of query budget on online learning. Our algorithm samples a prescribed number of agent-action pairs and applies inverse-probability weighting to their marginal gains, yielding an unbiased gradient estimate of the expected stage utility for a Kullback-Leibler (KL) mirror-ascent update. We derive a $1/2$-approximate regret bound that elegantly splits into a tracking error between optimal and learned policies, quantified by their KL divergence, and a query-dependent feedback term. Experiments on cooperative information coverage show that our approach retains high performance with significantly less computation online.

</details>


### [86] [Change Point Detection with Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34228)
*Tigran Ramazyan,Denis Derkach,Mikhail Hushchyn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对基于窗口的变点检测(CPD)中的"样本量瓶颈"问题，提出用生成模型进行窗口增强，使简单分类器通过稳定CPD得分分布而超越复杂深度学习基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 窗口式CPD为保证时间精度必须使用小窗口，但小样本提供的统计证据不足，导致用于计算CPD得分的分类器和深度学习模型得分方差大、误判频繁，构成"样本量瓶颈"。

Method: 引入生成模型对窗口进行数据增强，扩大可用样本量，以缓解小样本导致的统计证据不足问题，并稳定CPD得分分布。

Result: 在合成数据和真实数据上的实验表明，所提方法显著提升了CPD性能；经窗口增强后，简单分类器可超越复杂深度学习基线。

Conclusion: 生成式窗口增强是缓解样本量瓶颈、提升CPD质量的有效手段，并可降低对复杂模型的依赖。

Abstract: Change point detection (CPD) methods aim to identify abrupt changes in the properties of time series, which is a key problem in numerous fields. A major class of CPD approaches relies on computing a CPD score, which is either a discrepancy or divergence between two subsequent samples, called windows. A fundamental limitation of window-based CPD is the 'sample size bottleneck.' To ensure temporal precision, windows must be small; however, small samples provide insufficient statistical evidence for the classifiers and deep-learning models currently used to compute CPD scores. This leads to high variance in the scores and frequent mispredictions. Our study tackles this issue by exploring generative models for window augmentation, effectively expanding available data to improve CPD quality. Experiments on synthetic and real data demonstrate that the proposed approach significantly enhances CPD. Specifically, we show that this approach allows simple classifiers to outperform complex deep-learning baselines by stabilizing the CPD score distribution.

</details>


### [87] [Train-Only-Constrained LM for Synthetic Clinical Trajectories: Leak-Safe Calibration and Auditable Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34258)
*Yaser Kord,Moritz Hess*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种仅用TRAIN数据的受限语言模型流水线，通过分位数token与逐生物标志物3x3马尔可夫转移实现因子化生成的临床时间序列模拟，并用单调分位数映射QMap对齐严重度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床时间序列模拟需同时满足时间真实性、保持模型完整性的校准以及隐私透明；现有经验采样器在分布保真度与共现结构上不足，且校准常依赖TEST统计量而泄露信息。

Method: 流水线分三部分：(1) 紧凑GPT式语言模型以分位数token学习跨生物标志物共现；(2) 由TRAIN拟合的3x3逐生物标志物马尔可夫转移强制时间方向，使动力学可审计；(3) 仅在TRAIN上拟合单调分位数映射QMap（SYN(TRAIN)->SIM(TRAIN)），不使用任何TEST统计量，再将该固定映射施加于SYN(TEST)的严重度。

Result: 在5000例模拟测试台（参数处于已发表临床范围）上，受限LM相较经验采样器分布保真度与共现改善；经小幅λ扫描后检测率接近随机水平；时间遵循性由构造保证；基于分类器的成员推断审计AUC≈0.5（不作差分隐私声明）。给出多种子结果与配对95%置信区间，并以Delta W1及置信区间量化QMap对齐的严重度。

Conclusion: 这是受控模拟器上取得的Stage-1方法性结果，未声称差分隐私；后续计划在真实EHR队列上以临床终点开展Stage-2验证。

Abstract: Simulating clinical time series necessitates temporal realism, calibration that preserves model integrity, and privacy transparency. We present a train-only, constrained language-model pipeline that factorizes generation. A compact GPT-style language model learns cross-biomarker co-occurrence through quantile tokens, and temporal directions are enforced by 3x3 per-biomarker Markov transitions fit on TRAIN, allowing dynamics to be audited. We fit a monotone quantile map (QMap) on TRAIN only (SYN(TRAIN) -> SIM(TRAIN)); no TEST statistics are used. The fixed map is then applied to SYN(TEST) severities. The constrained LM demonstrated improved distributional fidelity and co-occurrence in a 5,000-patient simulated testbed (parameters within published clinical ranges) compared to an empirical sampler, maintained near-chance detectability after a small lambda-sweep, and exhibits temporal adherence by construction. A classifier-based membership-inference audit results in an AUC of approximately 0.5; we make no differential-privacy claim. We present multi-seed results with paired 95% confidence intervals and quantify the severity of QMap's alignment (Delta W1 with confidence interval). This is a Stage-1 method result obtained on a controlled simulator; Stage-2 validation on real EHR cohorts with clinical endpoints is planned.

</details>


### [88] [LLM-coupled interpretable tool wear monitoring with multi-evidence reasoning in CNC machining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34270)
*Yuntong Chen,Yingqi Li,Qiwen Shen,Guobin Zhao,Xitian Tian,Lijiang Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出“深度学习磨损分类+LLM辅助推理”两层框架，实现可解释的数控刀具磨损评估与换刀建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有刀具磨损诊断模型精度高，但对刀具更换决策的指导有限，缺乏可解释性。

Method: 第一层采用PCA Loading特征选择与优化GRU，识别三种磨损状态；第二层利用LLM基于分类结果与特征趋势生成自然语言报告及换刀建议。

Result: PHM2010数据集上宏F1达98.38%；工程师评估显示报告优于规则模板；NASA铣削数据的跨数据集测试支持框架的泛化性。

Conclusion: 分类与LLM推理相结合可在保持高精度的同时提升磨损评估的可解释性与决策支持价值。

Abstract: Interpretable tool wear assessment in CNC machining is challenging. Existing diagnostic models have high accuracy but provide limited guidance for tool replacement. This study proposes a two layer framework combining deep learning wear classification and LLM assisted reasoning. The first layer uses PCA Loading feature selection and optimized GRU to recognize three wear states, achieving 98.38 percent macro F1 score on the PHM2010 dataset. The second layer uses LLMs to generate natural language reports and replacement suggestions from classification results and feature trends. Engineer evaluation shows the reports outperform rule templates. Cross dataset tests on NASA milling data support the generalizability of this framework.

</details>


### [89] [LatentCP-DPO: Conformal Pessimism for DPO with Finite-Sample Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34275)
*Hongwei Xu,Jingyi Liu,Vincent Y. F. Tan,Zai Yang,Yulian Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 LatentCP-DPO 框架，将共形预测构造的不确定性集与悲观偏好优化结合，用于 BT 成对反馈与 PL 排序反馈，并给出有限样本的次优性上界与下界分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DPO 在偏好数据有限时容易过拟合噪声或信息量弱的偏好标签，需要一种能对潜在相对奖励引入不确定性、并抑制其负面影响的方法，同时需要刻画这种不确定性对策略性能的理论影响。

Method: 基于 LatentCP 对未观测模型参数构造不确定性集：在可交换性与偏好模型正确设定的假设下，由可观测偏好的共形预测集构造潜在相对奖励的有限样本边际覆盖不确定性集；随后在一组独立的、无偏好标签的提示与候选回答上，通过最大化不确定性集上的最坏情况期望偏好对数似然来学习响应策略（悲观优化），并分别针对 BT 成对反馈与 PL 排序反馈展开分析。

Result: 在适当假设下建立了正则化策略次优性的有限样本上界，将策略类近似误差、共形不确定性与统计估计误差分离；对 BT 反馈进一步给出最坏情况下界，其与上界在无标签优化样本数上的依赖互为倒数关系，并揭示不确定性集会引入随优化样本增大仍持续存在的偏差。实验表明该框架在有限偏好数据集上有收益。

Conclusion: 共形不确定性集可有效用于偏好优化的悲观化设计，理论分析既刻画了其统计精度也揭示了其固有限制（持久偏差），实验支持其在有限偏好数据下的有效性。

Abstract: Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) learns a response policy from human preference data, but with a limited dataset it can overfit to noisy or weakly informative preference labels. We introduce LatentCP-DPO, a framework that combines conformal prediction with pessimistic preference optimization for Bradley-Terry (BT) pairwise feedback and Plackett-Luce (PL) ranking feedback. Building on LatentCP's construction of uncertainty sets for unobserved model parameters, we address how to incorporate this uncertainty into preference optimization and characterize its effect on policy performance. Under exchangeability and a correctly specified preference model, we construct uncertainty sets for latent relative rewards with finite-sample marginal coverage from conformal prediction sets for observable preferences. We then learn a response policy by maximizing the worst-case expected preference log-likelihood over these uncertainty sets, using an independent collection of prompts and candidate responses without preference labels. Under suitable assumptions, we establish finite-sample upper bounds on regularized policy suboptimality that separate policy-class approximation error, conformal uncertainty, and statistical estimation error. For BT feedback, we further establish a worst-case lower bound for LatentCP-DPO that matches the upper bound's reciprocal dependence on the number of unlabeled optimization examples. The lower bound also shows that the uncertainty sets can induce a bias that persists as the optimization sample grows. Together, these results characterize both the statistical accuracy and potential limitations of preference optimization using conformal uncertainty sets. Experiments support the benefits of the proposed framework in finite preference datasets.

</details>


### [90] [Scaling Looped MoE Language Models under Matched Budgets: Information Flow and Parameter Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34276)
*Shipeng Bai,Jiayu Qin,Hong Liu,Xili Wang,Jianchao Tan,Jiacheng Li,He Zhang,Yifan Lu,Jiaqi Zhang,Kefeng Zhang,Yuchen Xie,Xunliang Cai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文在严格等参数、等FLOP约束下系统研究循环Transformer的设计原则：通过门控内循环状态更新与跨阶段残差连接改进信息流，并确定"核心深度占展开总深度25%-35%+将节省参数再分配为逐层路由专家"的最优配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环Transformer可解耦计算深度与参数量，且增加计算量已显示性能收益；但在等参数、等FLOP约束下如何系统配置此类模型的设计原则仍然缺乏。

Method: 改进循环模型中的信息流：(1) 门控内循环状态更新；(2) 跨阶段残差连接。在此基础上开展受控实验，扫描循环参数配置，包括单次通过的核心深度比例与参数再分配策略（将节省的参数转为每层额外的路由专家）。

Result: 最优配置为核心深度取展开总深度的25%-35%，并将节省参数再分配为每层额外路由专家，可在零额外计算下提升性能且兼容现有训练基础设施。1T token预训练后，69B MLA与51B KDA MoE模型在四个基准上分别比等预算非循环基线平均高1.16与2.29个百分点；200B token复现实验中困惑度低于其他循环方法。

Conclusion: 门控状态更新与跨阶段残差可有效改善循环模型信息流；在等参数等计算预算下，适度核心深度（25%-35%）配合专家参数再分配是实现循环MoE性能提升的有效设计原则。

Abstract: Looped Transformers decouple computational depth from parameter count, and prior studies have demonstrated promising performance gains with increased computation. However, systematic design principles for configuring these models under strict iso-parameter and iso-FLOP constraints remain scarce. We contribute to this direction by improving information flow in looped models and systematically studying loop parameter configurations. Specifically, we refine this information flow via gated inner-loop state updates and a cross-stage residual connection. Building on this design, controlled studies identify a configuration combining a single-pass Core depth of 25%-35% of the total unrolled depth with reallocating saved parameters into extra routed experts per layer, improving performance at zero extra compute while preserving compatibility with existing training infrastructure. Evaluated after 1T pre-training tokens, our 69B MLA and 51B KDA Mixture-of-Experts models outperform budget-matched non-recurrent baselines by an average of 1.16 and 2.29 percentage points across four benchmarks, respectively. Further reproduction experiments on a common backbone with a 200B-token training budget show that our method achieves lower perplexity than other looped methods. Code for these reproduction experiments is available at https://github.com/lxi01766/Scaling-Looped-MoE-Language-Models-under-Matched-Budgets-Information-Flow-and-Parameter-Allocation.

</details>


### [91] [RAP: Radial-Angular Random Projection for Hyperbolic Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34298)
*Jiahong Liu,Wenhao Yu,Yifan Li,Yanyu Chen,Rex Ying,Menglin Yang,Irwin King*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出径向-角度随机投影（RAP）：精确保持径向坐标，仅对角度方向施加欧氏随机投影再提升回目标双曲面上，从而首次为双曲表示提供类似 Johnson–Lindenstrauss 的免数据降维原语。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Johnson–Lindenstrauss 引理为欧氏表示学习提供了目标维数 k=O(ε^{-2} log n) 与原始维度无关的免数据压缩原语。随着双曲表示在 Transformer、视觉-语言模型与大规模语言模型中受到关注，同样需要双曲版本的这种 oblivious 降维原语，但目前尚不存在。作者指出两种自然方案存在对偶缺陷：切空间方案有干净的局部保证，但依赖坐标卡并付出曲率惩罚；双曲面模型中的完全双曲投影几何上原生，但会混合径向与角度误差，并将其送入 arcosh 的条件数中。

Method: RAP 保持径向坐标 r 完全不变，只对角度方向施加一次高斯随机投影（单个高斯矩阵-向量乘积），再将结果提升回目标双曲面，故构造上即为双曲到双曲的映射，并把双曲压缩问题归约为 S^{d-1} 上的归一化角度 JL 问题。理论部分包括：显式含 sinh(√c r_x)sinh(√c r_y) 条件数的有限集 RAP 保证；局部切空间 JL 定理；以及针对完全双曲对照方案的 arcosh 条件数引理。

Result: 在原点居中的宽半径点云、平衡树、聚类高斯与 WordNet 名词层级四类数据上，RAP 在每个 (n,k) 组合中取得最低平均相对失真；在宽半径数据上相较最强双曲基线降低约 48%–49%。RAP 可扩展到百万点规模数据集，并在视觉-语言表示检索任务上优于双曲投影基线。

Conclusion: RAP 以极低计算代价（单次高斯矩阵乘）建立了双曲表示的免数据降维原语，兼具几何原生性与显式理论保证，并在合成、层级结构与真实多模态表示上均优于现有双曲投影方法。

Abstract: Random projection is a basic compression primitive for Euclidean representation learning, with the Johnson--Lindenstrauss lemma giving a target dimension $k=O(\varepsilon^{-2}\log n)$ independent of the ambient dimension. As hyperbolic representations gain increasing attention in modern Transformers, vision-language models, and large language models, the same need for oblivious dimensionality reduction arises, but no analogous primitive yet exists for hyperbolic representations. We study this gap. Two natural attempts fail in dual ways: a tangent-space scheme gives a clean local guarantee but is chart-bound and pays a curvature penalty, while a fully hyperbolic projection in the hyperboloid model is geometrically native but mixes radial and angular error before feeding them into the conditioning of $\operatorname{arcosh}$. We propose radial-angular random projection (RAP), which preserves the radial coordinate exactly and applies a Euclidean random projection only to the angular direction, lifting the result back to the target hyperboloid. RAP is hyperbolic-to-hyperbolic by construction, costs one Gaussian matrix-vector product, and reduces hyperbolic compression to a normalized angular JL question on $\mathbb{S}^{d-1}$. We prove a finite-set RAP guarantee with explicit $\sinh(\sqrt{c}\,r_x)\sinh(\sqrt{c}\,r_y)$ conditioning, complemented by a local tangent-space JL theorem and an arcosh-conditioning lemma for the fully hyperbolic comparator. Across an origin-centered broad-radius point cloud, a balanced tree, a clustered Gaussian, and the WordNet noun hierarchy, RAP attains the lowest mean relative distortion in every $(n,k)$ cell, reducing distortion over the strongest hyperbolic baseline by approximately $48\%$--$49\%$ on broad-radius data. RAP also scales to million-point datasets and improves retrieval on vision--language representations over hyperbolic projection baselines.

</details>


### [92] [Interpretable identification of constitutive parameter field for three-dimensional heterogeneous and graded hyperelastic materials from full-field displacements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34310)
*Kanhaiya Lal Chaurasiya,Vaskar Halder,Prajyot Patil,Gaurav Navnath Shinde,Debashish Das,Akshay Joshi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种可解释、高效的反演框架，仅用位移场数据即可预测异质超弹性材料多个本构参数场的三维相对空间变化，并在合成与实验基准上以远低于深度学习方法的计算成本取得可接受精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用实验可得的位移场与边界反力识别非均质固体的空间变化材料属性仍是难题；该能力对功能梯度材料与复合材料的设计制造有益，也可用于体内测量表征非均质生物组织。现有研究多针对均质材料，异质/梯度材料研究较少，且近期方法多依赖参数众多、需在配点处强制力平衡的深度学习框架。

Method: 利用超弹性材料反问题的线性特性，在有限元层面直接优化本构参数，构建可解释且省时的框架，从纯位移场数据预测异质超弹性材料多个本构参数场的三维相对空间变化；在含异质、梯度及各向异性超弹性材料的三维合成与二维实验基准上验证。

Result: 在三维合成与二维实验基准上验证框架，达成可接受的预测精度，且计算时间显著低于近期报道的深度学习框架。

Conclusion: 基于超弹性反问题线性性与有限元级直接参数优化，可在保证精度的同时大幅降低计算成本，为异质与梯度材料（含生物组织）的无损表征提供高效可解释方案。

Abstract: The inverse problem of identifying spatially varying material properties of heterogeneous solids using experimentally available displacement fields and boundary reaction forces alone remains challenging. Being able to characterize heterogeneous and graded solids would be advantageous in the design and manufacturing of functionally graded materials and composites. Additionally, an efficient technique for identifying heterogeneous material properties from in vivo measurements would be useful in biomedical contexts to characterize heterogeneous biological tissues. Although several techniques developed over the past two decades have addressed inverse identification for homogeneous materials, including linear elastic, hyperelastic, viscoelastic, and elastoplastic solids, comparatively fewer studies have considered heterogeneous and graded materials. Furthermore, several recent approaches to characterizing heterogeneous and graded materials employ parameter-heavy, deep learning-based frameworks trained to enforce force equilibrium at specified collocation points within the material. In this work, we propose an interpretable and time-efficient framework that predicts the three-dimensional relative spatial variation of a constitutive parameter field for multiple parameters of a heterogeneous hyperelastic material, using only displacement-field data. In this work, we validate our framework on 3D synthetic and 2D experimental benchmarks involving heterogeneous, graded, and anisotropic hyperelastic materials. By leveraging the linear nature of the inverse problem for hyperelastic materials and directly optimizing for the constitutive parameters at the finite-element level, we achieve acceptable prediction accuracy with significantly lower computational time compared to recently reported deep-learning-based frameworks.

</details>


### [93] [Inverse Reinforcement Learning with State-Dependent Foresight for Human Decision-Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34351)
*Jinil Kim,Jinwoo Jeong,Hyeongjun Yun,Min-hwan Oh,Woo-Young Ahn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SC-AIRL（状态条件对抗式逆强化学习），将“有限视野”与“状态依赖”两项认知特性嵌入规划器，比标准AIRL更贴近人类行为，并提供可解释的状态依赖预见性度量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算建模以拟合行为的模型刻画个体，但自然任务产生高维行为轨迹。标准逆强化学习（IRL）假定近乎无限视野的规划，而人类预见性既有限又随状态变化，存在建模失配。

Method: SC-AIRL：按规划深度设置多个策略，共享同一奖励函数，仅在显式奖励求和的视野长度上不同；再由一个状态条件路由器依据当前状态对这些策略加权混合，从而在对抗式IRL框架中联合推断奖励与状态依赖的规划深度。

Result: 在行人过街与高速公路超车任务的留出回合上，SC-AIRL相较标准AIRL提升模仿精度：top-1准确率分别由0.549升至0.647、由0.629升至0.718，且rollout中更贴近被试行为；推断的规划深度与自评焦虑及运动冲动性相关。

Conclusion: SC-AIRL在行为复现上优于标准AIRL，同时给出可解释、由模型导出的状态依赖预见性指标，将认知约束与逆强化学习相结合。

Abstract: Computational modeling of behavior represents individuals with a model fitted to their observed behavior and characterizes them through its parameters. Naturalistic tasks incorporate the complexity of real environments and yield high-dimensional behavioral trajectories. Inverse reinforcement learning (IRL) infers the latent reward function underlying these trajectories. Standard IRL, however, assumes effectively infinite-horizon planning, whereas human foresight is both bounded and state-dependent. Here we introduce SC-AIRL (State-Conditional Adversarial IRL), which builds these two cognitive properties into the planner. Per-depth policies share one reward but differ in how far ahead they sum it explicitly. A state-conditional router mixes them according to the current state. Across held-out episodes in pedestrian crossing and highway overtaking tasks, SC-AIRL improves imitation over Standard AIRL, raising top-1 accuracy from 0.549 to 0.647 and from 0.629 to 0.718, respectively, while more closely reproducing participant behavior in rollouts. Inferred planning depth correlates with self-reported anxiety and motor impulsivity. SC-AIRL thus reproduces human behavior more closely than Standard AIRL while providing an interpretable, model-derived measure of state-dependent foresight.

</details>


### [94] [RAWPrism: A Three-Task Dataset and a Unified Model for RAW-Domain Image Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34359)
*Tianyi Hu,Yangdong Li,Shuyan Liu,Zihan Cao,Jingze Liang,Shaojie Wu,Chenyang Feng,Jincheng Di,Weijie Xiao,Liangjian Deng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RAW领域的统一多任务图像融合资源：RAWPrism数据集（覆盖多曝光融合MEF、多聚焦融合MFF、红外-可见光融合IVIF）与RAWPrism模型（3.58M参数的轻量统一融合网络），首次在ISP之前的RAW域统一三项融合任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多曝光、多聚焦与红外-可见光图像融合研究大多针对已渲染的RGB图像，而在图像信号处理（ISP）之前的RAW域进行跨任务统一融合的研究相对不足，缺乏相应数据与模型支撑。

Method: 构建RAWPrism数据集：包含422个可用MEF场景、150组配准的MFF RAW图像对，以及1500个源自FMB的IVIF样本（可见光RAW由ReRAW重建、RGB对应图由CycleISP渲染），并提供场景级划分与任务特定的渲染与评分协议。模型方面设计轻量统一网络，采用CFA感知表示、任务条件源门控与共享主干，以单一checkpoint支持三类任务。

Result: 形成覆盖三类任务、具备场景级划分与评估协议的数据资源；RAWPrism模型以仅3.58M参数的单一体支持MEF、MFF、IVIF，据作者所知为首个在RAW域直接统一这三项任务的模型。

Conclusion: 数据集与模型共同为研究曝光、聚焦与光谱互补性之间的共享融合机制提供了基础支撑。

Abstract: Research on multi-exposure fusion (MEF), multi-focus fusion (MFF), and infrared-visible image fusion (IVIF) has largely focused on rendered RGB images, while unified fusion across these tasks in the RAW domain before image signal processing (ISP) remains relatively underexplored. To support research in this setting, we introduce the RAWPrism dataset, covering all three tasks, and the RAWPrism model, a lightweight unified multi-task fusion network. The RAWPrism dataset comprises 422 usable MEF scenes and 150 registered MFF pairs from our RAW captures, together with 1,500 FMB-derived IVIF samples, in which the visible RAW inputs are reconstructed by ReRAW and their RGB counterparts rendered by CycleISP. Scene-level partitions and task-specific rendering and scoring protocols connect these resources to method development and evaluation. To our knowledge, the RAWPrism model is the first to unify MEF, MFF, and IVIF directly in the RAW domain. It combines a CFA-aware representation, task-conditioned source gating, and a shared backbone to support all three tasks with one 3.58M-parameter checkpoint. Together, the dataset and model support the study of shared fusion mechanisms across exposure, focus, and spectral complementarity.

</details>


### [95] [Single-Database Rewards Are Hackable: Counterfactual Execution for Trustworthy Text-to-SQL RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34363)
*Shreshth Rai,Rishii Kumar Singh,Aksh Aggarwal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出反事实数据库(CF)奖励：在若干隐藏的、代码生成的数据库副本上额外执行最终SQL，全部结果匹配才给奖励(严格型)或按匹配比例给奖励(均值型)；严格型显著降低有缺陷查询被全额奖励的比例，且几乎不拒绝正确查询；但在GRPO对照实验中均值型未能提升域内准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本到SQL的强化学习通常只在一个数据库上比对执行结果来给奖励，当数据库缺少能暴露查询错误的行时，错误查询可能"碰巧"结果匹配而被给予奖励，造成奖励黑客与错误的信用分配。

Method: 构造代码生成的反事实数据库副本（隐藏、多样），把最终查询在副本上执行：(1)严格型——所有副本结果均匹配才给奖励；(2)均值型——按匹配副本比例给奖励。在1,302道合成审计问题上评估奖励质量（错误查询被全额奖励比例、奖励黑客比例、正确查询被拒率），并比较族盲生成器；在3.35B模型上用GRPO做对照训练（每臂3个随机种子），并与单库奖励基线对比。

Result: 严格型把被全额奖励的有缺陷查询比例从29.5%降到16.8%，奖励黑客从54.1%降到1.9%，且在2,847条正确查询上零拒绝；族盲生成器效果相当。GRPO对照中，均值型未提升域内准确率（8.4% vs 11.7%），因两种奖励仅在5-7%的rollout上不一致；在Spider上提升1.6分。有一个种子使两个对比符号同时反转，说明单种子奖励比较具有误导性。

Conclusion: CF奖励在奖励信号层面有效抑制偶然匹配与奖励黑客，但奖励差异稀疏使其带来的训练收益不稳定、甚至为负；单种子比较不可靠，需多种子评估；代码与数据已公开。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) for text-to-SQL agents pays a query when its result matches the reference on one database. However, a wrong query can match by luck when the data lack the rows that expose its bug. We study a counterfactual-database (CF) reward that also runs the final query on a few hidden, code-generated copies of the database and pays only if all results match (strict form) or by the share matched (mean form). On 1,302 synthetic audit questions, the strict form lowers the share of buggy queries paid in full from 29.5% to 16.8% and of reward hacks from 54.1% to 1.9%, rejecting 0 of 2,847 correct queries; copies from a family-blind generator do about as well. In a controlled GRPO comparison on a 3.35B model with three seeds per arm, training on the mean form did not improve in-domain accuracy (8.4% vs 11.7%), because the two rewards disagreed on only 5-7% of rollouts; on Spider it gained 1.6 points. One seed reverses both signs, so single-seed reward comparisons mislead. Code: https://github.com/RishiiGamer2201/counterfactual-sql-rewards ; data, models, demo: https://huggingface.co/counterfactual-sql

</details>


### [96] [Strong Starts, Simple Recipes: Unified Multi-Teacher On-Policy Distillation from Multimodal Specialists](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34397)
*Wenzheng Chang,Taiqiang Wu,Yuqi Xu,Yongfu Zhu,Zelin Peng,Ji Zhao,Shuai Dong,Wenpu Liu,Caoyuan Ma,Xuehui Wang,Ziyue Wang,Weichu Xie,Haiyuan Wan,Ngai Wong,Tong He,Wenqi Shao,Jiaqi Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Uni-MOPD：以同源专家模型的免训练均匀合并作为学生初始化，再辅以短程 MOPD（均匀域采样 + 全词表反向 KL）微调，取得优于 Open-MOPD 且训练快 3.3 倍的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 MOPD 方法把学生初始化固定为基础模型或多域 SFT 检查点，将能力整合完全交给蒸馏过程，忽视了"起点"这一设计变量。

Method: 将初始化视为可设计变量：(1) 对同一基模型训练的各领域专家进行免训练均匀参数合并以初始化学生；(2) 施加短程 on-policy 蒸馏，使用均匀域采样与全词表 reverse KL，不设调度、逐 token 加权或截断超参。

Result: 覆盖 5 个多模态领域、19 个基准：(1) 训练前均匀合并已达基模型 200 步 MOPD 后的分数，短蒸馏后媲美基于搜索的更优起点；(2) 短程 MOPD 弥补合并未整合的能力，各领域均提升，专家仍领先处增益最大；(3) 强起点下均匀采样与全词表 reverse KL 不劣于所有对比采样策略与散度。同预算下优于 Open-MOPD，训练速度提升 3.3 倍。

Conclusion: 起点选择比后续训练细节更关键；强初始化 + 简洁蒸馏配方即可高效实现多专家能力整合，并将开源训练框架、专家检查点与统一学生模型。

Abstract: Multi-teacher on-policy distillation (MOPD) integrates separately trained domain specialists into a single unified model. Existing methods focus on refining the distillation procedure while initializing the student from a base model or a multi-domain SFT checkpoint, which leaves capability integration largely to distillation itself. Instead, we treat the starting point as a design variable and propose Unified MOPD (Uni-MOPD), which pairs a strong start with a simple recipe: a training-free uniform merge of specialists from the same base model initializes the student, and short MOPD with uniform domain sampling and full-vocabulary reverse KL refines it. Across five multimodal domains and 19 benchmarks, our study establishes three findings. (1) The starting point matters more than subsequent training choices: before any training, the uniform merge already matches the score that the base model reaches after 200 MOPD updates, and after short distillation it matches higher-scoring, search-based starts. (2) Short MOPD recovers most of the capabilities the merge leaves unintegrated; it improves every domain, with the largest gains where the domain teacher still leads. (3) From a strong start, uniform domain sampling and full-vocabulary reverse KL match or exceed every evaluated sampling strategy and divergence, without schedules, per-token weighting, or truncation hyperparameters. Uni-MOPD outperforms Open-MOPD at the same training budget while training 3.3 times faster than its released code. We will release the training framework, specialist checkpoints, and unified student.

</details>


### [97] [Any-Rate: Half-Bit Multi-Precision LLM Quantization with Coded Bit-Planes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34402)
*Hoonki Lee,Haechan Shin,Hyuntaek Jung,Yeonu Choi,Sangyeob Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Any-Rate，一种从单一 checkpoint 导出 2–8 bit 多精度量化的方法，在 2–4 bit 区间以半比特步长提供精度，缩小相邻精度差距并缓解 2 bit 严重退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 嵌套多精度量化通过只读取权重的最高位从单一 checkpoint 派生多种精度，利于在不同内存与能耗预算下的端侧部署；但现有方法仅支持整数比特，相邻精度间精度差距大，且 2 bit 下退化严重。

Method: Any-Rate 将 8 个权重组成一块，把部分 bit-plane 限制为扩展汉明码字（0.5 bit/权重）；每行跨精度共享一张 LUT；并对所有精度联合编码，每个精度设独立目标，以降低共享 bit-plane 带来的损失。

Result: 在四个 LLM 上，2 bit 与 3 bit 下取得多精度方法中的 SOTA；2 bit 时保留 69% 的 FP16 精度，4 bit 时与最佳方法相差 1 个百分点以内；解码速度与现有 kernel 相当，LPDDR 能耗模型估计在同等精度下最多节省 27% 能耗。

Conclusion: Any-Rate 以单一 checkpoint 支持 2–8 bit、2–4 bit 半比特粒度的多精度量化，在精度、解码速度与能耗上均具优势，适合预算多变的端侧部署。

Abstract: Nested multi-precision quantization derives multiple precisions from a single checkpoint by reading only the leading bits of each weight, which makes it attractive for on-device deployment under varying memory and energy budgets. However, existing methods offer only integer bits, leaving large accuracy gaps between adjacent precisions and severe degradation at 2 bits. To address this, we propose Any-Rate, which serves 2-8 bits from one checkpoint, in half-bit steps over 2-4 bits. Any-Rate restricts some bit-planes to extended Hamming codewords in 8-weight blocks (0.5 bits per weight), shares one LUT per row across precisions, and encodes all precisions jointly with a separate target for each, reducing the loss from sharing bit-planes. Across four LLMs, Any-Rate achieves state-of-the-art accuracy among multi-precision methods at 2 and 3 bits, keeping 69% of FP16 accuracy at 2 bits, and is within one point of the best at 4 bits. It decodes about as fast as existing kernels, and an LPDDR energy model estimates up to 27% less energy at equal accuracy. Code is available at https://github.com/hoonki2002/Any-Rate.

</details>


### [98] [DiversePlace: Diversity-Seeking Curriculum Reinforcement Learning for Macro Placement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34472)
*Wenrui Zhou,Jiashun Liu,Wenji Fang,Zhiyao Xie,Ling Pan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 DiversePlace，将"质量可控的多样性"作为宏单元布局（macro placement）的显式优化目标，通过课程式 PPO 维护一个小型高质量且差异化的布局档案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宏单元位置强烈约束后续物理设计流程，而这些高影响的离散决策必须在下游质量可被准确评估之前做出，只能依赖 HPWL 等快速但不完美的训练期代理指标。现有 RL 布局器多只优化单一 HPWL 解，长序贯决策又需大量搜索辅助或离线专家数据，难以保留多个有竞争力的布局。

Method: DiversePlace：课程式近端策略优化（PPO）框架，结合"质量门控的多样性奖励"与"逐步扩展的布局时间步视野"，支持从零开始的 RL 优化，并将探索限制在 HPWL 竞争性区域内；同时维护紧凑档案以保留 HPWL 有竞争力但彼此显著不同的布局以供下游选择。

Result: 在 ISPD2005 基准上，DiversePlace 在 top-5、top-10、top-20 档案多样性指标上稳定提升；全部 8 个设计上 HPWL 更低或基本持平，且效率更优。

Conclusion: 以质量门控多样性为显式目标、配合课程式扩展视野，可在不依赖离线专家数据的前提下从零训练 RL 布局器，同时获得更高多样性与不劣于单解基线的 HPWL，为下游布局选择提供更丰富的候选集。

Abstract: Macro placement is a critical early-stage decision in physical design because macro locations strongly constrain subsequent implementation stages. The task is difficult because these high-impact discrete decisions must be made before downstream quality can be measured accurately, forcing training to rely on fast but imperfect training-time oracles such as half-perimeter wirelength (HPWL). Existing RL-based placers therefore mostly optimize single HPWL-driven solutions, and their long sequential horizons often require heavy search assistance or offline expert data while leaving little room to preserve multiple competitive layouts. We address this gap by making quality-controlled diversity an explicit objective for macro placement. Instead of relying on a single proxy-optimal solution, we maintain a compact archive of HPWL-competitive yet meaningfully different placements for downstream selection. We realize this idea in DiversePlace, a curriculum proximal policy optimization (PPO) framework that combines a quality-gated diversity reward with an expanding placement horizon, enabling from-scratch RL optimization while keeping exploration inside competitive HPWL regions. On the ISPD2005 benchmarks, DiversePlace consistently improves top-5, top-10, and top-20 archive diversity and achieves lower or virtually identical HPWL on all eight designs with better efficiency. Code: https://github.com/William030422/NIPS2026-DiversePlace.

</details>


### [99] [Auditing Identity and Timing Signals in Operational Logs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34493)
*Andrew Hendel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出两个无需训练事件预测模型的简单审计方法（身份审计、时序审计），量化数据集在单位标识与时间信息上的泄漏；在6个领域7个公开数据集上验证，ICU中"向前填充"使身份AUC从0.916升至0.993，患者重叠使模型优势被高估（0.163 vs 0.046）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测ICU脓毒症、硬盘故障等事件的模型可能仅靠识别"记录属于哪个患者/硬盘"或"记录何时被采集"就获得高分，而非真正预测事件；现有工作缺少在训练任何事件模型之前、直接度量数据集携带此类信息量的手段。

Method: 设计两个审计任务：(1) 身份审计——分类器判断两条记录是否来自同一单位；(2) 时序审计——判断同一单位的两条记录哪条更晚。在设定的零假设下两者期望AUC均为0.5。在6个领域7个公开数据集上运行；并在12小时脓毒症预测任务上对比"患者小时在训练/测试间拆分"与"患者完全留出"两种协议，以及向前填充与原始值两种特征，并加入按时间顺序划分且随访完整的对照；另检验仅用住院小时数与测量次数的纯时序特征表现。

Result: ICU中向前填充使身份AUC由0.916升至0.993；患者重叠下向前填充带来0.163 AUC提升，患者留出时仅0.046；该夸大在第二个ICU死亡预测数据集上同样出现；时序对照显示无正的平均重叠差距。时间信息本身是强预测因子：仅用住院小时数与测量计数即达0.761 AUC，加入原始临床值仅+0.018。ICU之外，95%硬盘、75%贷款样本跨越日历切分两侧，重叠差距在日历切分下依然存在。

Conclusion: 审计可指示应检查哪些特征与预处理选择，而评估协议决定其后果；作者发布代码、可在任意含单位列与时间列的表格上运行的审计库，以及审计使用指南。

Abstract: Models that predict events from operational logs, such as sepsis in ICU records or failures in hard drives, can score well by recognizing which patient or drive a record came from, or when it was recorded, instead of predicting the event. I introduce two simple audits that measure how much of this information a dataset carries before any event-prediction model is trained, and apply them to seven public datasets in six domains. One asks whether a classifier can tell that two records came from the same unit; the other asks which of two records from the same unit came later. Under the stated nulls, both have expected AUC 0.5. In ICU data, the identity audit flags a common preprocessing step: carrying the last measurement forward raises identity AUC from 0.916 to 0.993. On a 12-hour sepsis forecasting task, carrying forward makes a model look 0.163 AUC better than raw values when a patient's hours are split between training and test, but only 0.046 better when patients are held out. The exaggeration under patient overlap also appears in a second ICU dataset with mortality as the outcome. On the sepsis task, a chronological control with complete follow-up, in which training sees only a patient's earlier, resolved hours, shows no positive mean overlap gap. Timing is a strong predictor: with complete follow-up, hour of stay and measurement counts alone, with no clinical value, score 0.761 on held-out patients, and adding the raw clinical values changes that by +0.018. Outside the ICU, 95% of drives and 75% of loans have records on both sides of a calendar cut, and the overlap gap persists under a calendar split. The audits show which features and preprocessing choices to check; the evaluation protocol decides the consequences. I release the code, a library that runs the audits on any table with a unit column and a time column, and a guide to using the audits.

</details>


### [100] [SMOTE 25 Years of Magic: What Has Been Done, What the Evidence Shows, and Whom It Reached](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34511)
*Thanapong Intharah,Pharamut Makjaroen,Kanvara Thawarorit,Supawit Chaingam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文在SMOTE发表25周年之际对其变体家族进行审计：梳理变体修改了SMOTE六项决策中的哪些、证据强度如何、影响范围多大，并提出新的变体本身不应再被视为贡献，而应在强学习器、类加权与调优阈值对照下逐项检验并报告校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SMOTE自2002年发表、2018年十五周年综述以来已被超过3.4万项索引工作引用，其中一半来自计算机科学之外；但方法族究竟改变了什么、支撑证据有多强、技术实际影响到谁，缺乏系统的实证审计。

Method: 构建76篇论文的精选语料（其中37篇提出SMOTE族生成器），并以PRISMA 2020格式对22,426条记录做系统检索以检验覆盖度。从语料中收集1,756条数据集条目、1,511项与SMOTE的已发表比较及71项研究的评估设计；从检索中随机抽取300个SMOTE应用。采用智能体式AI在书面规则下完成抽取、筛选与编码，并报告每一步的核查方式。

Result: 每个变体都会重新决定SMOTE规则的六项决策之一或多者，37个变体中有24个仅在二分类上运行；在提出者自己的论文中，79.0%的已判定比较优于SMOTE，但独立研究显示，在强学习器与调优阈值下增益缩小，且重采样会损害概率校准。语料仅覆盖通过筛选的3,097条记录中的52条，早年覆盖较密、近年覆盖稀疏。技术影响面极广，但结果层面的收益未被证明，已记录四项危害，估计约37%的应用在划分训练/测试集之前即对全数据集重采样（数据泄漏）。

Conclusion: 对于表格分类，新的SMOTE变体本身不应再算作贡献：新生成器应逐项决策地对照类加权与调优阈值、配合强学习器进行检验，并报告校准结果。文末给出选择指南、含15项条目的报告清单与研究议程。

Abstract: Background. SMOTE was published in this journal in 2002 and reviewed here at its fifteenth anniversary in 2018. At twenty-five it is cited by more than 34,000 indexed works, half of them outside computer science. Objectives. To establish what the methods built on SMOTE change, how strong the evidence for them is, and whom the technique has reached. Methods. We audited a curated corpus of 76 papers, 37 of which propose a SMOTE-family generator, and tested its coverage with a systematic search of 22,426 records in the PRISMA 2020 format. From the corpus we collected 1,756 dataset entries, 1,511 published comparisons with SMOTE and the evaluation design of 71 studies; from the search we drew 300 applications of SMOTE at random. We used an agentic AI approach to extract, screen and code under our written rules, and we report how each step was checked. Results. Every variant re-decides some of the six decisions of the SMOTE rule, and 24 of the 37 are only ever run on two classes. In their own papers, proposed methods beat SMOTE in 79.0% of the decided comparisons, but independent studies find that the gains shrink with strong learners and tuned thresholds, and that resampling damages calibration. The corpus holds 52 of the 3,097 records that pass the screen and covers the early years closely, the recent ones thinly. The reach is very wide, but a benefit at the level of outcomes is undemonstrated, four harms are documented, and an estimated 37% of applications resample the whole data set before splitting it. Conclusions. For tabular classification, a new SMOTE variant should no longer count as a contribution in itself: a new generator should be tested one decision at a time against class weighting and a tuned threshold with a strong learner, with calibration reported. We close with a selection guide, a reporting checklist of fifteen items and a research agenda.

</details>


### [101] [Resilience Analysis Through Automated Stage Identification and Quantitative Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34527)
*Anna Strzoda,Krzysztof Grochla,Rafael Munoz-Carpena*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种领域与威胁无关的机器学习方法，自动识别系统性能时序中的PARA（Plan/Prepare、Absorb、Recover、Adapt）韧性阶段，并据此定量计算系统韧性指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂工程系统的韧性分析需要定量刻画系统在扰动事件前后的表现，但现有方法往往依赖特定领域或特定威胁类型，难以跨系统通用；因此需要一个领域无关、威胁无关的自动化韧性阶段识别与量化框架。

Method: 采用四种ML模型（RF、SVM、LightGBM、XGBoost）对系统性能时序进行PARA韧性阶段分类；再利用模型预测结果，结合扰动事件前、中、后的性能数据计算韧性指标（R_t、RL）；在两类运营域网络系统（IEEE 802.11、LTE）与机场交通系统数据集上评估。

Result: 韧性阶段分类有效，各阶段最佳F1分数为74.3%–99.4%；韧性指标R_t与RL估计中，最佳模型MAPE在IEEE 802.11与LTE上为0.35%–0.82%，机场数据为1.65%–9.56%。

Conclusion: 该方法验证了领域无关的韧性阶段识别与量化可行性，为复杂工程系统的威胁无关韧性分析及"抵御—吸收—恢复"能力评估提供基础。

Abstract: This paper introduced an ML-based approach, designed to be domain- and threat-agnostic, for automated recognition of PARA (Plan/Prepare, Absorb, Recover, Adapt) resilience stages in system-performance time series, enabling subsequent quantitative resilience analysis across different systems. We investigate four ML models for resilience stage recognition: Random Forest (RF), Support Vector Machine (SVM), Light Gradient Boosting Machine (LightGBM), and Extreme Gradient Boosting (XGBoost). We then use the ML models' predictions to quantify system resilience by computing corresponding resilience metrics from system-performance data observed before, during, and after disruptive events. We evaluate the proposed approach using datasets from two distinct operational domain network systems and an airport transportation system, demonstrating its domain-agnostic applicability to resilience assessment. The results demonstrate the effectiveness of resilience stage classification, with the best F1-scores ranging from 74.3% to 99.4% across all resilience stages. The overall resilience metrics R_t and RL are estimated, with the best models' MAPE ranging from 0.35-0.82% on IEEE 802.11 and LTE to 1.65-9.56% for the airport. These results provide a basis for threat-agnostic resilience analysis of complex engineering systems, enabling further assessment of how effectively a system withstands, absorbs, and recovers from disruptive events and stress conditions.

</details>


### [102] [Kant-Q: A Q-Learning Algorithm for Kantian Equilibrium in Symmetric Markov Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34536)
*Rishi Bharadwaj,Sujit Gujar,Yadati Narahari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 Kant-Q：每个智能体在"所有智能体采取同一动作"的动作剖面上做 Q 学习，使每次更新等价于单智能体 MDP 采样，从而继承标准 Q-learning 的收敛保证，并在对称博弈中收敛到 Kantian 均衡（最优公共策略）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI 智能体之间的交互大量是混合动机博弈，而主流 MARL 以 Nash 均衡为目标，这类均衡在混合动机博弈中往往 Pareto 低效。作者转而采用"康德式"普遍化原则（每个智能体选择它希望所有智能体都采用的动作），以获得更优的公共策略解。

Method: 提出 Kant-Q 算法：智能体学习形如"全体采取相同动作"的动作剖面，由此每次更新可视为单智能体 MDP 的样本，直接沿用标准 Q-learning 的收敛性质。理论上证明：(1) 每个对称马尔可夫博弈都存在纯 Kantian 均衡；(2) 所有智能体的 Q 表收敛到同一极限，其贪心策略即为 Kantian 均衡；(3) 当每个状态的最优公共动作唯一时，贪心策略收敛；(4) 在对称单调博弈子类中，Kantian 均衡在纯策略剖面中 Pareto 有效且严格个体理性。

Result: 在迭代囚徒困境和最多 200 个智能体的 GovSim 渔业环境中，Kant-Q 学到 Kantian 均衡，而基线方法表现不佳，验证了理论与实际有效性。

Conclusion: 由于相同 AI 系统的副本在对称博弈中天然互为对手，将"普遍化"作为合作型 AI-AI 交互的设计原则具有理论与实践支持。

Abstract: AI agents increasingly interact with one another, and many of these interactions are mixed-motive games, which have posed a persistent challenge for learning algorithms. Most multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) algorithms are designed to reach Nash equilibria, yet in many such games these equilibria are Pareto-inefficient. We instead study Kantian equilibria, an economic solution concept in which each agent plays the action it would want all agents to adopt. In symmetric games, this corresponds to an optimal common policy. We propose Kant-Q, a Q-learning algorithm in which each agent learns action profiles where all agents play the same action. Each update is then a sample from a single-agent MDP, so Kant-Q inherits the convergence guarantees of standard Q-learning. We show that every symmetric Markov game has a pure Kantian equilibrium. We also prove that all agents' Q-tables converge to the same limit, whose greedy policy is such an equilibrium, and that greedy play converges whenever the optimal common action is unique at every state. We further show that in a subclass of symmetric monotone games, the Kantian equilibrium is Pareto-efficient among pure strategy profiles and strictly individually rational. Empirically, on the iterated Prisoner's Dilemma and the GovSim fishery with up to 200 agents, Kant-Q learns the Kantian equilibrium, while the baselines underperform. Since copies of the same AI system naturally face one another in symmetric games, these results suggest universalization as a design principle for cooperative AI-AI interaction.

</details>


### [103] [Investigating the efficacy of vortex beam speckle patterns for the recognition of atmospheric turbulence and fractional topological charge with a deep learning model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34573)
*Ratikanta Swain,Allarakha Shikder,Naseem Babu,Niladri Sarkar,Aryan Raj,Raghavan K. Easwaran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文尝试直接从单张散斑图像回归标量涡旋光束的分数拓扑荷，并界定湍流强度上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分数拓扑荷可扩大OAM链路可用模式集，但在大气湍流后可靠读取困难：干涉/衍射法需无畸变光束与精确对准，多数学习方法只处理整数或粗间隔电荷且不含湍流。

Method: 涡旋光束经1500 m、150层von Kármán相位屏传播，经固定随机散射体产生256×256、16 bit散斑；TC取0–10、步长0.01，湍流强度D/r0取0–50。用CNN主头回归TC，辅助头分类湍流等级；当前使用2万张子集（10^6数据集在筹备）。

Result: 训练未见电荷上：无湍流时MAE=0.031（统一强度标度），D/r0=10时0.78，D/r0=50时1.85，常数预测为2.5；湍流等级分类正确率78%。

Conclusion: 现有训练规模下仅无湍流时电荷读取准确；电荷与湍流共同作用于散斑颗粒尺寸，可能构成读取瓶颈。实验验证与矢量涡旋光束扩展留待后续。

Abstract: Fractional topological charge (TC) enlarges the usable mode set of orbital angular momentum (OAM) links, but reading it reliably after atmospheric turbulence remains difficult. Interferometric and diffractive methods need an undistorted beam and a careful alignment, while most learned approaches treat integer or coarsely spaced charges and do not include turbulence. Here we ask whether the charge of a scalar vortex beam can be regressed directly from a single speckle image, and at which turbulence strength this stops working. We propagate vortex beams over 1500~m through 150 von Kármán phase screens, scatter them with a fixed random diffuser, and record $256\times256$, 16-bit speckle images for TC from 0 to 10 in steps of 0.01 and turbulence strengths $D/r_0$ from 0 to 50; the results reported here use a subset of 20\,000 images, and a data set of $10^{6}$ images is in preparation. A convolutional neural network estimates the TC and, through an auxiliary head, the turbulence category.
